# R12 decisions (D-R12-xx)

Stage A, 2026-11-04. Each deletion or replacement names the requirement it still meets, the AVA practice behind it
(every AVA statement is an Inference: no AVA schematic or BOM is public, `cost_study.md` §4) and its live price.
Labels (AGENTS §2.5): **Datasheet** (maker document, cited), **Live** (JLC component API via `tools/jlc_stock.lookup`,
`jlc_r12_stock.json`, 2026-11-04T02:42:47Z unless stated), **Calculation** (arithmetic on Datasheet / Live values,
`r12_windows.json` from `m3_design/r12_budget.py`), **Inference** (any unmeasured or unverified input). No hardware result here is
measured; later Measured labels refer to CAD or tool results. No AGENTS §2 rule changes (§2 check at the end of §1).

## 1. Decisions

Dated entries through D-R12-174 are historical: their board hashes, coordinates, counts, quotes and analysis
figures belong to those decisions. D-R12-175 … -191 record the current round-12 fix pass. Live open items
are §4; current board state is `docs/M3_R12.md`. A later amendment supersedes the earlier instruction it names.


**D-R12-01 Architecture.** R11's circuit at `0b26da5` (D-R11-85 … -88 included), pruned and re-sourced; every part on
the top face (JLC Economic PCBA: one side, 255 ± 5 °C reflow, single PCB ≥ 10 × 10 mm, BGA ≥ 0.5 mm pitch, JLC
capabilities page, Live per `cost_study.md` §1.1); four layers, 1.6 mm, 0.5 oz inner, ENIG; 46 × ≤ 100 mm. The board is
laid out new: R11's escape, placement and routing are not reused. Basis: `cost_study.md` §0 (R11 $376.55 / $531.81 for
2 / 5 boards; one-sided Economic about half). AVA puts parts on both faces because it is hand-built; at JLC that forces
Standard two-sided assembly (+$33.77 per order and no Economic tier), so one face is the deliberate departure from AVA.
REQUIREMENTS M1 (Standard PCBA, 1 oz inner) becomes an owner acceptance item (§3); R11 already deviated on inner copper.

**D-R12-02 MCU package: TFBGA-100 kept (STM32H743VIH6, C964146, $8.8446, 4221 / 3913, Live).** The LQFP-100 fallback
(STM32H743VIT6 C114409, $9.42–10.70, cost_study §2.2) is not needed. Escape count per ball ring on R11's ball map with
the D-R12-12 changes (ring 1 = outer; Calculation from `pin_table.tsv`):

| Ring | Balls | Signal | Supply (GND / 3V3 / 3V3A / VCAP) | NC | Escape |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 36 | 23 | 4 | 9 | straight out on F.Cu |
| 2 | 28 | 16 | 3 | 9 | F.Cu between ring-1 balls: 9 gaps per side, 36 in all; at most 8 ring-2 balls per side |
| 3 | 20 | 8 | 1 (VCAP1) | 11 | dog-bone via, then B.Cu |
| 4 | 12 | 2 (QSPI_CLK G5, BOOT0 D5) | 4 | 6 | dog-bone via, then B.Cu |
| 5 | 4 | 0 | 4 | 0 | vias to In1 GND / In2 3V3 |

Geometry (Calculation, ST DS12110 Rev 8 Table 219 land: 0.40 mm pad at 0.8 mm pitch): gap between ring-1 pads
0.80 − 0.40 = 0.40 mm ≥ 0.10 mm track + 2 × 0.10 mm clearance = 0.30 mm (JLC multilayer 0.09 mm); a 0.45 / 0.30 mm via
at an interstitial site is 0.566 mm from four ball centres: 0.566 − 0.200 − 0.225 = 0.141 mm ≥ 0.10 mm; two
interstitial vias leave 0.80 − 0.45 = 0.35 mm, one 0.10 mm track. The ten inner signal balls need ten dog-bone vias and
leave the array on B.Cu, which carries no parts on a one-sided board: 9 channels per side between via columns, 36 ≥ 10.
In2 stays a plane. LQFP-100 would cost +$0.58 (5 b) / +$1.86 (2 b) per board and ≈ 190 mm² (cost_study §2.2). Stage C
implements the escape; if it fails there, the LQFP decision is reopened with this table.

**D-R12-03 Board rules.** Through vias 0.3 mm drill / ≥ 0.45 mm pad only (no small-hole surcharge; 0.25 mm adds $16.88,
cost_study §2.1, Live), no via in a soldered pad (no VIPPO: U401 and its two GAP-3 vias are deleted, D-R12-07), track
and clearance 0.10 mm (JLC 0.09 mm). `m3.kicad_dru` (R10 / R11 rules kept by name plus the R12 floors) and the project
net classes (Default 0.10 / 0.10 mm, 0.45 / 0.30 mm via; Power 0.30 mm) are generated by `r12_sch.py`.

**D-R12-04 USB path → diode-OR.** Deleted U10 (TPS2553 USB limit), U11 (TPS2121 mux), D4 (USBLC6), R85–R88, R90, R91,
C85, C86, C98, TP82 (nets USB_LIMITED, USB_ILIM, LOGIC_ILIM, LOGIC_SS, USB_OVLO, USB_FAULT_N, LOGIC_SOURCE_ST). Added D5
(RAW_PROTECTED → VLOGIC) and D6 (VBUS → VLOGIC), both SS34 (C8678 Basic $0.0351, 40 V 3 A, VF 0.55 V at 3 A, Live
listing). Function kept: bench power and log download over USB-C (F11), battery priority (the higher source wins:
VLOGIC ≥ 5.2 V on the pack against ≤ 5.0 V on USB, Calculation / Inference; 5.2 V is stage A's R11-copper 5.203 V:
stage D on the 90 mm board gives 5.124–5.324 V at four stalls, R12F6-TRI-11 / D-R12-143), no back-feed into the host (D6) and C84
hold-up isolated from the port and camera bucks (D5; better than R11, where C84 back-fed them through U11). USB-only
VLOGIC is 4.30–5.00 V from a USB 2.0 host port (4.75–5.25 V, VF 0.25–0.45 V Inference), above the LMR50410's 4.0 V floor
(D-R12-06); a bus-powered hub's 4.40 V low-power port gives 3.95 V, below it (bench rule O-R12-03). Lost margins: USB
inrush / fault current limit toward the host, USB over-voltage lockout, the USB ESD clamp (STM32's own pin ESD only,
Inference), the USB_FAULT_N / LOGIC_SOURCE_ST flags. AVA: three bare micro-USB ports (Inference). Saves about $1.7 of
parts per board and the TPS2121, USBLC6 and 0.1 % codes (cost_study §3 #17, #49–51, #69–70, Live).

**D-R12-05 PA4 port sense.** (Values superseded by D-R12-86: R111 47k / R112 100k, same ratio.) LOGIC_SOURCE_ST (U11
ST) is replaced by PORT_SENSE = PORT_4V8 × R112 10k / (R111 4.7k + R112 10k) on PA4 (both Basic): 3.08–3.40 V with PORT_4V8 on (initial), 3.04–3.45 V (drift), above VIH 2.40 V and below
VDD + 0.3 V = 3.54 V (Calculation / Inference: drift, `r12_windows.json` sense). R10's firmware rule "enable the port UARTs only while PA4
reads low" becomes "only while PA4 reads high" (the ports are powered exactly when PORT_4V8 is up). F10 / F12 functions
kept; the sense is the rail itself instead of the mux's selection.

**D-R12-06 3V3 buck: LMR50410XDBVR (C2841056, Extended $0.3335, 26557 / 26280, Live).** R11's AP63203 (C780769) has
−2896 orderable (Live). TPS563201 / TPS563208 cannot run the 3V3 on USB through D6 (VIN 4.5 V minimum, wake-up up to
4.3 V, TI SLVSD90B §5.3 / §5.5, Datasheet). LMR50410: VIN 4–36 V, UVLO rising 3.55–4.0 V, VFB 0.99–1.01 V, 1 A, EN tied
to VIN allowed (TI SLUSDW3A §6, §7.3, §7.5, Datasheet); SOT-23-6 on R11's land (JLCEDA land of C2841056 0.532 × 1.072 mm
at ±1.149 mm, checked in `r12_parts.geometry_checks`). Feedback R14 47k / R15 20k (Basic): 3V3 = 3.270–3.431 V initial,
3.240–3.464 V drift from §7.5's V_FB alone; since D-R12-139 (R12F6-TRI-03) the window uses §7.8's PFM regulation band
(U2 is the PFM variant): **3.254–3.482 V initial, 3.224–3.516 V drift**, inside 3.0–3.6 V (VDD33USB, flash, card,
sensors; Calculation / Inference: the band at R12's VIN, its stacking and drift). Inductor: SWPA4030 4.7 µH, the code
the two TPS563208 bucks use, instead of TI Table 9-1's 10 µH: ripple ratio K = 0.61 at 8.4 V in, at the top of TI's
20–60 % range (SLUSDW3A eq. 9, Calculation); 0.77 only in the wrong-3S band; loop behaviour with 4.7 µH is an Inference
(bench load step, O-R12-06). Output C8 22 µF (Table 9-1: 22 µF) plus C23, C461 and the decoupling. Input C6 22 µF + C99.

**D-R12-07 Port rail: U400 TPS563208DDCR (C468250, Extended $0.1457, 17432 / 17048, Live) makes PORT_4V8 directly.**
Deleted U401 (TPS7A91 LDO) with its two GAP-3 VIPPO vias, R402, R403, R405, C408, the PORT_5V3 bulk C403–C405, C430, the
second input cap C401 and the per-port 22 µF C411 / C421 / C445 (100 nF per port kept). L400 SRP5030 6.8 µH (C1329837
$1.44) → SWPA4030 4.7 µH (TI Table 7-2: 3.3–4.7 µH at 5 V); output C406 + C407 22 µF (Table 7-2: 20–68 µF; the per-port
22 µF would have taken PORT_4V8 above 68 µF nominal). Feedback R400 51k + R404 1k / R401 10k (Basic; the 0.1 % parts
deleted). TPS563208 (FCCM), not the study's TPS563201 (Eco-mode): SLVSD90B gives VFB 0.749–0.787 V over TJ −40…125 °C
for continuous mode only, and Eco-mode reads 774 mV typical at light load, which could take a lightly loaded port rail
past 5.0 V (Datasheet / Inference); the FCCM part costs $0.08 more. Window (Calculation / Inference: independent resistor drift): **4.561–4.967 V** initial
(module limits 4.5–5.0 V: MicoAir LR900 4.5–5 V, HGLRC M100 Mini 3.3–5 V, project records), **4.511–5.024 V** with an
independent 100 ppm/K drift bound: 24 mV over 5.0 V at the corner (Inference: two thick-film parts of one series track
far closer than independent drift; O-R12-05). AUX 100 mA allowance: no longer limited by U401's thermal floor (GAP-3);
the buck is rated 3 A. Fault containment: cycle-by-cycle valley limit 3.3–5.1 A and UVP hiccup (65 %, 10 ms restart,
Datasheet) instead of the LDO's 1.5–1.9 A limit. AVA: a linear port regulator (Inference); R12 keeps one switcher to
avoid the LDO's code, VIPPO and $0.81.

**Round-12 amendment to D-R12-07 / -08 (D-R12-190):** L400 / L401 are 2 A rated, listed Isat 3.2 A,
below the 3.3–5.1 A valley limit. A 2–3.3 A overload is limited by neither buck; U9's 3.92–4.89 A bounds it
(Calculation / Inference). Record inductor temperature at 2.5 A for 60 s on port and camera loads. No part change.

**D-R12-08 Camera: U403 TPS563208 is the camera switch.** Deleted U402 (TPS2553), R430, R432, U12 (TXU0202), U13
(TPS70933), C90, C91, C92, C93, C100, R102, R103. Originally added U403 (EN = CAM_PWR_EN on PC14 with R431 100k; PC12 since D-R12-93,
CAM_EN through R436 from PC12 with D436 clamping PC12 and R431 47k since D-R12-153;
reset-off hot-leakage qualification remains open). F6 / F11 function kept; TPS563208 EN VENL 0.8 V,
REN 0.6–2.4 MΩ (Datasheet). Added L401 4.7 µH, C432 100 nF bootstrap,
C433 22 µF input, C434 22 µF output (with C431), R433 51k + R434 10k / R435 10k: CAM_5V **5.221–5.691 V** initial, 5.162–
5.757 V drift (Calculation / Inference: drift), conditional on U403 VIN ≥ VO / 0.75 (TI SLVSD90B §7.3;
6.96–7.59 V for the initial window, Calculation). The 0.22 V initial margin over the RunCam 5 V minimum
is at the regulator under that input condition, before PCB and harness loss; it is not guaranteed over the
2S range. At four stalls U403 sees about 5.554 V on `a02a6181…` (D-R12-163); the optimistic camera-pin estimate is about 4.91 / 5.01 V
at maximum / typical off-time with 70 °C copper, the 1 A load and R10 harness / transient allowances (Calculation / Inference;
stageD_analysis.md §6). Camera dropout and harness behaviour need bench qualification. R100 / R101 originally joined the
camera wires to PD6 / PD5 (1 kΩ series, as the ports; FT pins, R11 pin table); since D-R12-93 they join
PC11 / PC10. Keep the camera UART pins analog while U403 is off
(no drive into an unpowered camera). Lost: CAM_FAULT_N, the camera current limit at ~1.1 A (the buck's 3.3–5.1 A valley
limit and hiccup contain a short; a 3–5 A overload short of a hard short could reach U9's 3.9 A limit, Inference) and
the camera UART isolation. CAM_5V_SENSE (PC2_C, D-R11-86 divider) kept: 0.457–0.518 V initial, 0.445–0.531 V drift (Calculation / Inference: CAM_5V window; R12F9-TRI-06). AVA: camera switched
by a transistor (Inference).

**D-R12-09 Battery entry and U9.** U9 TPS259470**L** (C3662793, −94 orderable, min 7) → TPS259470**A**RPWR (C3662799
$1.294, 2047 / 2047, Live): same RPW land and pinout, same EC table; only the fault response differs (auto-retry after
110 ms instead of latch-off, TI SLVSFC9C device table and §6.6 tRST, Datasheet). Auto-retry keeps a transient fault from
ending the flight; a hard fault retries at 110 ms intervals (Inference on thermal duty). OVLO: R82 2M 0.1 % / R83 280k
0.1 % → R82 1M / R83 100k + R93 33k (Basic): rising **9.80–10.71 V**, falling 8.91–9.78 V initial (9.69–10.83 / 8.80–
9.89 V drift; Inference), nominal 10.22 V; a full 2S pack (8.4 V) stays below the lowest falling threshold and a nominal 3S pack
(11.1 V) is cut off, so F9's wrong-3S function is kept (Calculation with VOV(R) 1.183–1.223 V, VOV(F) 1.076–1.116 V,
IOVLKG ±0.1 µA, Datasheet). The study's 12.5–14.0 V proposal is not taken (it would pass a 3S pack). ILIM: R84 750 Ω
0.1 % → R84 + R94 1.5k ‖ 1.5k (Basic): 3.96–4.84 A at 750 Ω (Datasheet), 3.92–4.89 A with the 1 % pair (Inference:
1/R scaling). EN chain (F6): R80 / R165 470k and R81 300k keep their values in 0603 Basic cases (C23178, C23024); C87
220 nF and D402 unchanged; EN = 2.03 V at 8.4 V and ≤ 6.5 V up to the 19.9 V clamp (Calculation); R10 / R11's F6
timing figures carry over (same values and EN limits) until stage D re-runs them. C81 dVdt 4.7 nF C0G 0603 (C85980
Extended) → 0402 X7R Basic (C1538): ±10 % plus bias shifts the inrush slope (Inference; not re-checked: the stage D hot-plug
model is a reverse hot-plug transient with no dVdt / U9 start-up model and carries C81 only in its discharge
inventory, so this record's earlier "re-checked in the stage D hot-plug run" was false, R12F6-TRI-13 / D-R12-143;
bench item, R12_VALIDATION row 15). D8 SMBJ12CA 600 W → SMAJ12CA (current: hongjiacheng C19077532, fee-exempt Preferred, $0.0341 Live 2026-11-09T00:10:43Z; D-R12-165; formerly Brightking C78399): same 12 V standoff / 19.9 V clamp, 400 W
(Live listing); the hot-plug model's surge is re-run in stage D (Inference that the pack transient is under 400 W). D9
B340A → SS34 (Basic). **R166 0.36 Ω and the C89 / C95 damper are kept** (the hot-plug figures depend on them; the
stage D re-run decides; AVA has none, Inference).

**D-R12-10 Pyro FETs → AO3401A ×2 (Q120 / Q121, C15127 Basic $0.0962) and AO3400A (Q124, C20917 Basic $0.0891).**
AONR21357 ×2 ($0.355) and AON7524 ($0.371) are Extended. AGENTS §2.2 / §2.4 kept: RAW_ACT still behind the back-to-back
load switch; every gate keeps its series resistor, pull-down and gate–source capacitor (R119 / R120 / R121 / C120,
R122 / R123 / C123, R126 / R127 / R128 / C126, values unchanged). Datasheet: AO3401A −30 V, ±12 V VGS, RDS(on) ≤ 60 mΩ at
−4.5 V (25 °C), ×1.5 from 25 to 125 °C; AO3400A 30 V, ±12 V, ≤ 48 mΩ at 2.5 V, ×1.43 at 125 °C, VGS(th) 0.65–1.45 V
(AOS datasheets via LCSC). Fire budget (Calculation / Inference: R11's copper placeholder, R10's pack model and hot factors at
70 °C): **four-stall 1.247 A against the 1.125 A floor (+10.9 %)**, idle 1.503 A; R11 had 1.278 A. Shorted
match: 2.16 A (2.42 A with leads shorted), Q120 I²R 0.34–0.43 W and Q124 0.27–0.34 W for ≤ 36 ms (Calculation / Inference: the same pack model and hot factors). Gate
drive (corrected by D-R12-131, R12F5-TRI-03 / -04; Calculation / Inference: the Q120 hot factor and the 0.1 V Q122
drop): during a four-stall fire Q120 / Q121 VGS is −4.569 / −4.560 V in stage D (ACT_FET_S basis with the BAT_IN and
ACT_FET_S copper drops), 69 / 60 mV beyond the −4.5 V row; the stage A placeholder (no copper) gives −4.577 V, −6.87 V
at 8.4 V with the bus on, −10.485 V at 12.6 V (largest ratio). The −4.5 V row holds down to BAT_IN 5.54 V with the bus
on and 5.63 V during a 1.25 A fire. It would pass ±12 V only above 14.41 V on the pack with the bus enabled, a
transient toward the 19.9 V clamp.
The bus is enabled from the on-rail check until switch-off (Inference that no transient above 14.4 V occurs while it is
on, pad or flight; O-R12-10, D-R12-115). Q124 VGS ≥ 2.777 V in stage D and in the stage A placeholder after subtracting the DS12110 Rev 8
Table 158 VOH allowance, with R126 / R128 at their ±1.67 % drift ends (Calculation / Inference: drift; D-R12-108,
corrected from 2.778 / 2.761 V by D-R12-131; **2.761 V** on the PFM 3V3 window since D-R12-139). PE4's steady load,
R127 1k plus R126 220R + R128 10k, is about 3.5 mA (≤ 3.93 mA on the PFM window, D-R12-139; was ≤ 3.9 mA), within Table
158's 4 mA row (Calculation / Inference: drift; D-R12-114).
Both clear the 2.5 V RDS(on) row; VGS(th) ≤ 1.45 V (Datasheet). Nominal gate τ = 48 µs (Calculation).

**D-R12-11 D120 BAT54A (C8591 Extended) → BAT54C,215 (C37704, Preferred Extended $0.0152).** Same 30 V / 2 µA class
(Live listing); pinning A1 / A2 / common K (Nexperia BAT54C Table 2, Datasheet): A1 = 3V3, K = PYRO1_COLD, A2 unused —
the same single diode from 3V3 to PYRO1_COLD as R11 (cold screen function kept). JLC's free feeder for Preferred
Extended on Economic was an Inference here; FAQ 7 confirms it and `jlc_cost.py` applies it since D-R12-110
(O-R12-12 closed). Historical totals below retain both cases.

**D-R12-12 U1 re-pins.** R11's ball map kept (D-R11-49 escape map and D-R11-85's ball assignments; **correction
(D-R12-86):** D-R11-85's inert argument for G3 did not carry over: G3 (PA4) became PORT_SENSE, driven high whenever the
battery path is on, and check_r12 did not run K-neighbours until D-R12-86). Deleted
functions free their balls: USB_FAULT_N (PD10), CAM_FAULT_N (PC13), CAM_UART_OE (PC15) → NC (analog, never driven);
LOGIC_SOURCE_ST (PA4) → PORT_SENSE (D-R12-05). `pin_table.tsv` columns r12_net / r12_mode / r12_source.

**D-R12-13 (superseded by D-R12-52: LPS28DFW is JLC Standard Only) Barometer: MS5611 (250 °C reflow limit, cannot see Economic's 255 °C) → LPS28DFWTR (C3263277, Extended
$6.6979, 367 / 324, Live).** F3 (readings to ≥ 300 hPa) kept: 260–1260 hPa in mode 1 (DS13317 Rev 1 p. 1, §3, Datasheet);
supply 1.7–3.6 V on 3V3A; I²C on R11's I2C1 nets (same pull-ups R12 / R13); SA0 to GND (R64 deleted); INT_DRDY NC;
PAD2LID soldered to GND as DS13317 §5 recommends; "qualified for soldering heat resistance according to JEDEC
J-STD-020" (§6.1, Datasheet; the 260 °C class for a package this size is an Inference from J-STD-020). Land from JLCEDA
(C3263277), its pin order checked against DS13317 Figure 3 (top view mirrors the bottom view). Not taken: MS5607 (same
250 °C family limit), ICP-10111 / BMP388-class (stop at 300 hPa: no margin), LPS22DF (no stock), LPS22HH ($6.48, 185
orderable). F3's bench vacuum characterisation still applies; firmware needs an LPS28DFW driver.

**D-R12-14 Crystal: NX3225GA 8 pF (C1985619 Extended $0.39) → X322525MOB4SI 12 pF (C9006 Basic $0.0953).**
The original C25 / C26 change, 10 → 18 pF C0G C1549, is superseded by **D-R12-107: 15 pF C1548**. Same 3.2 × 2.5
land (YXC suggested layout 1.4 × 1.2 mm pads at 0.8 / 0.5 mm gaps, identical to R11's). AN2867:
gm_crit = 4·ESR·(2πf)²·(C0 + CL)² with ESR ≤ 50 Ω and C0 ≤ 3 pF (YXC YSX321SL sheet, LCSC C9006 PDF, Datasheet).
With 18 pF ±5 % and 2–5 pF stray, gm_crit is 0.906–1.5027 mA/V: the +5 % / 5 pF corner exceeds DS12110 Rev 8
Table 141 Gm_critmax 1.5 mA/V (Calculation / Inference: stray; R12F3-TRI-05). The earlier 0.97–1.43 claim omitted
capacitor tolerance. With 15 pF ±5 %, CL is 9.125 / 11.0 / 12.875 pF and gm_crit 0.726–1.244 mA/V,
17.1 % below the limit (Calculation / Inference: stray). R10 rejected C9006 on an assumed C0 of 7 pF;
the maker's 3 pF maximum replaces that assumption. Cold start-up, drive level and frequency (MCO) remain
bench items (O-R12-09).

**D-R12-15 Connectors: JST GH → XUNPU GH-1.25 clones.** J410 / J420 / J440 SM04B (C189895 $0.654) and J400 SM02B
(C189893 $0.433) → GH1.25-4PWT (C52037406, Extended $0.1124, 4537 / 4489, Live); J9 SM06B (C133065 $0.358) and J122
SM05B (C189896 $0.204) → GH1.25-6PWT (C52037408, Extended $0.1393, 2645 / 2622, Live): two codes instead of four. Pin
orders unchanged; J400 uses pins 1–2 (3–4 unused), J122 pins 1–5 (6 unused): the harness uses a 4-pin and a 6-pin GH
housing. Lands from JLCEDA (no maker PDF on JLC): contacts 0.6 × 1.7 mm at 1.25 mm, tabs 1.0 × 2.8 (4P) / 1.3 × 2.7 mm
(6P) — the same contact and tab geometry as JST eGH p. 2 within 0.1 / 0.3 mm on the tabs. Mating with JST GH housings
and latch retention are Inferences (O-R12-04). AVA: one connector family per job.

**D-R12-16 Owner-fitted parts (superseded by D-R12-85: every part JLC-assembled).** LS120 MLT-5020 (250 °C peak, cannot see Economic reflow) → QMB-09B-03 (C96256, $0.1183)
THT 9 mm passive magnetic buzzer: 3 V o-p rated, 16 Ω, 2.7 kHz, ≤ 50 mA, −20…+70 °C (the same +70 °C limit as LS120, so
REQUIREMENTS F11 / owner row 36 stand), but its own reflow test peaks at 250 °C (Huawha spec 2019-12-3 items 1–9,
Datasheet). It is hand-fitted by the owner after delivery with C84, so JLC charges neither its $3.07 feeder nor the
$3.58 THT labour. Same Q123 / D123 / R162 / R163 drive (F11 function kept). Land 1.0 mm hole / 1.8 mm pad at 4.0 mm
(JLCEDA C96256; pin 1 = (+) is an Inference, check the mark when fitting). C84 stays owner-fitted (R10 GAP-4).

**D-R12-17 Capacitor consolidation.** Every 22 µF (C45783 0805, C21397 1210 Extended) → CL31A226KAHNNNE 1206 X5R 25 V
(C12891 Basic $0.1198): 13 places; 1 µF 25 V X7R (C29936 Extended) → 1 µF 50 V X5R 0603 (C15849 Basic) at C97, C120;
C128 100 nF 0603 automotive (C161115 Extended) → 0402 Basic (C307331). X5R is rated to 85 °C, above the 70 °C bay
(Inference: bay limit from R10). Less bias-retained capacitance than 1210 X7R: the hot-plug and hold-up re-runs use the
new parts (Inference, stage D). Deleted redundant bulk: C7 (VLOGIC), C9 (3V3), C11 (3V3A).

**D-R12-18 Port and camera UART ESD deleted.** D400, D401, D410, D411, D420, D421, D440, D441 (ESD103, $0.2305 each):
each port line keeps its 1 kΩ series resistor, which limits the current into the MCU pin clamp (Inference). The ports
are wired to modules inside the airframe, not operator-touched; AVA shows no clamps on its UART JSTs (Inference). D402
(J400.2) is kept: J400 is operator-touched and F9's EN calculation includes it. REQUIREMENTS F10's "1 kΩ plus
ground-only ESD" is an owner acceptance item (§3).

**D-R12-19 Kept as they are.** STM32H743VIH6, W25Q128 (F5 primary log), ISM6HG256X (F1 / F2: no in-stock cheaper part
meets ±4000 dps and ≥ ±64 g, cost_study §3 #66), microSD J430 and its pull-ups (F5 requires the removable log; the
hinged socket was retained on an unverified push-push ejection Inference, cost_study #29; replaced by push-pull C585350 under D-R12-162), SRV05-4 (F4 names it), R134 CRM2512 2 Ω (F7 shorted-igniter
pulse rating), USB-C J3 (no Basic USB connector found on JLC by keyword search, Live), R150–R153 0603 (Basic already),
the test pads, J1 / J121 wire pads and J2 (no BOM cost).

**D-R12-20 Gate.** `tools/check_r12.py` (skeleton: L-1, M-1, F-1, N-1, V-1, V-2, K-rules, M-5 implemented; the R10 /
R11 rules listed PENDING fail its full stage until ported), `fit_check --profile r12` (four layers, ≤ 100 mm, no
flipped footprint, GH-clone mated envelopes), `route_check --profile r12` (every r10 rule, empty lists), `check.sh`'s
R12 section (runs once `m3_design/r12/m3.kicad_pcb` is tracked; requires `tools/fab_manifest_r12.py` then),
`tools/test_r12_check.py` (always runs), `tools/test_check_shell.py` R12 cases. R10's and R11's gates are unchanged in
behaviour (one more self-test at the end of the run).

**D-R12-21 Stage A margin items (stage B, 2026-11-04).** (a) O-R12-05: the port buck divider becomes R400 47k (C25792)
+ R404 4.7k (C25900) / R401 10k (all Basic, already on the BOM): PORT_4V8 **4.539–4.943 V** initial and **4.489–4.999 V**
with the independent 100 ppm/K drift bound, so the worst corner is ≤ 5.0 V; the drift-bound minimum is 11 mV under the
LR900's 4.5 V (Calculation / Inference: drift, `r12_windows.json`). No E24 pair holds both ends in the drift bound (it needs a 0.516 V
window against 0.5 V); the over-voltage end was chosen because it is the abs-max side (Inference). (b) O-R12-07: the original 1 % divider and 0.42–0.45 V PA6 band are superseded by
D-R12-164 (orchestrator, round 9): R60 / R61 **0.1 % 25 ppm/K**, band **0.425–0.455 V**.
VLOGIC trip **4.675–5.005 / 4.667–5.014 / 4.652–5.029 V** nominal / initial / drift; trip bottom above
U9 turn-off through D5 **+0.123 / +0.114 / +0.051 V** (Calculation / Inference: SS34 VF, drift).
O-R12-17's trip-bottom item closes as a calculation; READY calibration and firmware/bench work stay open.
Stage A's below-four-stall margins are **+0.191 / +0.182 / +0.167 V** (Calculation / Inference, no CAD
copper); current CAD fire margins and hold-up are D-R12-164. Threshold bottoms needed before other
margins are **0.4146 / 0.4203 V** initial / drift (Calculation / Inference).

**D-R12-22 Copper layers.** In1.Cu one GND plane; In2.Cu the 3V3 plane with three islands (priority 1) in
`m3_design/r12_planes.tsv`: RAW_PROTECTED under the aft buck cells (the RAW fan-out lands there instead of a 1 A track
tree), 3V3A under U4 / U5 (eleven 3V3A pads, one island), PORT_4V8 under the forward GH row (the port rail reaches all
three ports and its bulk cap). F.Cu parts and signals; B.Cu signals under a GND pour of minimum width 0.5 mm. A B.Cu
critical signal crossing an island edge changes reference plane: the K-region rule (PENDING in check_r12) will count it
in stage D.

**D-R12-23 Via hole clearance 0.2 mm.** A 0.3 / 0.45 mm via at a TFBGA-100 interstitial site keeps 0.216 mm from the
balls' copper to its hole, under R10's general 0.25 mm; JLC lists "via hole to track 0.2 mm" (capabilities page, read
2026-11-04, Datasheet), so `r12_sch.dru()` adds rule "R12 via hole to copper" (vias against tracks, SMD pads, vias and
pours; plated pad holes keep the PTH rules). D-R12-02's escape table assumed copper clearance only.

**D-R12-24 Outline 46 × 100 mm and supports.** *(Outline and forward supports superseded by D-R12-99: 46 × 90 mm, forward supports (3.5, 84.1) / (42.5, 84.1); since D-R12-144 (round 7) 46 × 88 mm, forward supports (3.5, 82.1) / (42.5, 82.1).)* 100 mm is JLC's four-layer price step; 90 mm was placed first and could
not be routed one-sided under the R12 gate (stageB.md §1). Supports (3.0, 5.9), (43.0, 5.9), (3.5, 94.1), (42.5, 94.1):
the aft pair 3.0 mm in so the aft GH row clears both washers (stageB.md §4). GH rows: aft J400 / J122 / J9, forward
J420 / J410 / J440; J1 and J121 wire pads on the west edge, J3 on the east edge.

**D-R12-25 Sensor rule areas on F.** U4 keeps R10's body (no via, no pour) and pad-ring (no track) areas, now on F.Cu;
U5 keeps only its body area: R10's no-track band between the baro pad rows fitted the MS5611, and the LPS28DFW's centre
GND pad 7 (PAD2LID, DS13317 §5) can only join its GND pad 5 across it (a fixed 0.3 mm F.Cu link). The R12 IMU axis map
is not derived yet (U4 moved to the top face; stageB.md §6).

**D-R12-26 Routing method.** As R11 (D-R11-07): the build never searches. A drafting aid (`m3_design/r12_aid/`: an
octilinear A* in C with numpy clearance fields, rip-up and reroute) writes `m3_design/r12_routes.tsv`, which is
reviewed and frozen; `r12_route.py` only replays it. The aid builds the route_check rules into the search (45-degree
bends only, middle segments ≥ 0.5 mm, end segments longer than the width and 0.2 mm, ≤ 2 vias on a critical net without
a pour, plane vias inside their own island). Not complete at this stage (stageC.md).

**D-R12-27 U1 fan-out.** U1 at (23.0, 44.0), rotation 0 (R11's ball map: column 1 west, row A aft, column 10 east).
Ring-1 / ring-2 signal balls leave on F.Cu by fixed stubs (one channel per ring-2 stub), every other used ball by a
dog-bone via at an interstitial site; decoupling sits at the four corners of a 6.5 mm fan-out ring (stageC.md §3).

**D-R12-28 Block placement.** Power at the aft end (entry ladder, U9 and its networks, damper, RAW cluster, camera /
port / 3V3 buck cells in a column), the pyro cell west beside J121, the IMU on the roll axis aft of U1 with the baro,
the flash south-east of U1, the microSD forward of U1 with its contacts toward it, the hold-up C84 and the USB diode
D6 east, the buzzer, LEDs and Tag-Connect forward (stageB.md §5).

**D-R12-29 J121 pyro: R10's DB125-2 screw terminal (owner decision, 2026-11-03).** J121 is the black DORABO
DB125-3.5-2P-BK-S (LCSC C52993880), with R10's land and model `M3_DB125_2P_BK` copied verbatim (`r12_parts.R10_REUSE`).
Pin 1 PYRO1_OUT (E1−), pin 2 PYRO1_IGN (E1+). Pad 1 carries no unconnected inner rings, as on R10. The S-8 keep-outs on
In1 / In2 round both barrels are kept (`r12_route.S8_KEEPOUT`). Placement: (7.4, 37.0), rotation 270, wires leave west.
The face sits 3.40 mm in from the edge, so the R10 bare-bore wire-room rule passes: 5.00 mm against the 4.95 mm floor
(fit_check r12 now checks J121 as a terminal; Calculation on CAD envelopes). Assembly: JLC-fitted THT. Economic PCBA
takes THT parts (cost_study.md §1.1: about $0.016 per manual joint plus $3.58 hand-soldering labour per order, Live
2026-11-04). The part is Extended (feeder fee); stage D reruns the cost. It is not owner-fitted.

**D-R12-30 J1 battery: soldered pigtail pads ready for a 5.0 mm terminal (owner decision, 2026-11-03; superseded by D-R12-174: J1 is the KF301-5.0-2P terminal on the 86.85 mm board).** The two plated
pads become `M3_WIREPAD_2P_P5`: 1.40 mm finished holes, 2.60 mm lands, 5.00 mm pitch. Pin 1 BAT_IN, pin 2 GND. A tinned
16–20 AWG lead fits, and so do the pins of a later 5.0 mm two-position screw terminal, with no board change (hole size
for both is an Inference). The courtyard is a 10 × 8.2 mm keep-clear for that future body, its wire side at the west
edge. Placement: (3.5, 15.3), rotation 90. No terminal is placed or on the BOM. Because the keep-clear moved the entry
ladder, D8, C96, C80, TP80, C88 and R167 moved (stageB.md §5); C96's BAT_IN pad now lies on U9.5's bar line.

**D-R12-31 Ports: JST SH 1.0 mm clones (owner decision, 2026-11-03).** Reason (owner): the vehicle's primary flight
computer, the MicoAir H743, uses SH 1.0 ports, and one module harness should plug into either board.
- **Retention (owner deviation):** the owner accepts SH's friction-only retention, which R11 D-R11-05 had rejected.
  The per-flight procedure gains a retention step (an adhesive / RTV dab or a tie on each plug); see O-R12-18.
- **Parts:** Megastar ZX-SH1.0-4PWT (C7430446) for J400, J420 and J440; ZX-SH1.0-6PWT (C7430448) for J410, J9 and J122.
  Both are Extended, 1 A per contact, with 189 k / 94 k in stock (JLC listing, Live 2026-11-04). It is still two codes.
- **Land:** JST's SMnnB-SRSS-TB land as KiCad's library draws it from JST eSH.pdf. JLCEDA's land returned HTTP 403.
  That the clone takes the JST land is an Inference.
- **Pin orders.** J420 / J440 copy the H743 telemetry UART port: 1 GND, 2 5 V (PORT_4V8), 3 TX, 4 RX. J410 is SH-6 and
  copies the H743 GPS port: 1 GND, 2 5 V, 3 TX, 4 RX, 5 SCL, 6 SDA; the I2C pins are NC on R12. Source: the
  MicoAir743v2 port image on ardupilot.org (common-MicoAir743v2.html, read 2026-11-03). The image lists the pins in this
  order but does not mark which end is pin 1, and the owner's board may be the v1. The order is an Inference until a
  real H743 lead is checked (O-R12-19).
- **Unchanged orders:** camera J9 keeps its order (two contacts each for CAM_5V and GND: 1 A per contact, against R10's
  1 A camera load); servo J122 keeps 1–4 SERVO1–4, 5 GND, 6 NC; switch J400 uses pins 1–2 of a 4P body.
- **Fit:** fit_check models the SH header and plug from the listing and JST's family dimensions (Inference). The mouths
  sit 1.1 mm inside their short edge. This supersedes D-R12-15's GH clones.

**D-R12-32 Stage C changes.** The IMU moved 3 mm aft, to (23.0, 31.6), for room for U1's A-row fan-out (its 3V3A parts
followed). D6 is rotated so its VBUS pad faces J3. The drafting aid gained:
- a straight run of at least 0.4 mm after every via, so two vias of one path stay apart (hole to hole);
- a check that rejects any path whose own vias are closer than 0.6 mm;
- NPTH holes kept 0.25 mm from copper (the general hole rule);
- joins to an own via from any side;
- linked pin groups that fan out once;
- repeated passes over the failed connections with rip-up.

**AGENTS §2 check.** §2.1: J400 is still the only switch and still drives U9 EN through R80 / R165 / R81 / C87 with
D402. §2.2: RAW_ACT still comes only through Q120 / Q121 from BAT_IN; R134 2 Ω kept. §2.3: firmware rules unchanged
(new rules added: PA4 polarity, camera UART parking, LPS28DFW driver). §2.4: all three gate networks unchanged in value
and topology; the R12 schematic contract checks them by net (`r12_sch_check.SAFETY`, 37 seeds). §2.5: labels as above.
No owner question is needed.

**D-R12-33 Stage C placement pass 2 and router clean-up (2026-11-04).** Placement changes in `m3_design/r12_place.tsv`,
each to open a routing channel the stage C drafts kept failing in (stageC.md §4):
- **U3 (QSPI flash)** to (34.5, 52.6), rotation 90: pins 5–8 (IO0, CLK, IO3, 3V3) now form the column facing U1, IO0
  aft, in U1's G9 / J10 order. C19 (VCAP1) to (27.3, 51.0), C16 to (25.0, 51.6), R10 (NCS pull-up) to U3 pin 1 at
  (36.5, 57.0), C29 to U3 pin 8 at (31.0, 56.9), D6 forward to (41.2, 59.0), C23 aft to (34.0, 43.0). U3 keeps clear of
  J3's courtyard (it cannot sit at y 50.5 there).
- **Servo network:** the four 0603 series resistors sit in line with their J122 pins at 1.0 mm pitch in two staggered
  rows (R153 / R151 at y 9.0 on pins 4 / 2, R152 / R150 at y 12.1 on pins 3 / 1), so each pin runs straight into its
  resistor and the other row's track passes between two resistors (0.43 mm pad clearance). An in-line 0402 pull-down
  cannot follow at 1.0 mm pitch, so the pull-downs form a 2 × 2 block beside D121 ((25.9 / 28.0, 12.75 / 13.75)), their
  GND pads facing each other.
- **Port series resistors** directly behind their SH pins at 1.0 mm pitch, the wire pad toward the pin (TX resistors
  rotation 270, RX 90; pins 3 TX / 4 RX per D-R12-31), each port's 100 nF behind pins 1–2 (GND / PORT_4V8), y 92.2.
- **SD pull-ups** each on its J430 contact's x (R464 DAT2 16.8, R460 CS 17.89, R461 MOSI 19.0, R462 MISO 23.39, R463 DAT1
  24.5).
- **IMU south band:** C10 (3V3A bulk) moves from U4's south side to its aft side, (21.9, 27.95); R164 to (25.2, 28.2),
  rotation 270 (3V3 pad aft). The 3V3A island in `r12_planes.tsv` gains the strip x 19–24.3, y 27–29.4 under C10; R164's
  3V3 pad stays outside it.
- **LED2 / R33 (yellow)** to the free aft-east corner (43.3, 30.0 / 33.5): LED_YEL leaves U1 at B7 on the aft side.
Drafting aid (not run by the build): turn cost 2 and via cost 8 (were 1 / 5; `R12_TURN`, `R12_VIA`), the search budget
`R12_MAXEXP`, a `--clean` pass that re-routes every unit an earlier draft's route_check report flags with a higher turn
cost and keeps the old copper if it no longer completes, and slot-aware plated-hole via keep-outs (J3's shell slots:
the circular model let a via sit 0.46 mm from a slot, under JLC's 0.55 mm inner PTH rule). Runs need
`PYTHONHASHSEED=0` to be repeatable (the aid iterates Python sets).
**Result:** the placement passes stage B (fit_check, placed DRC 0 / parity 0, check_r12 placement). Routing is still not
complete: drafts leave 11–22 connections unrouted depending on the run (best 11, stageC.md §4), so no copper table is frozen.
AGENTS §2: no circuit, gate network or fire-path part moved; the pyro cell, J121 and J1's keep-clear are unchanged.

**D-R12-34 In2 becomes a mixed layer: 3V3 pour plus signal routing (owner's supervisor decision, 2026-11-04).** With
only F and B for signals, every draft stalled at 11–22 unrouted connections (stageC.md §4). In1 stays one solid,
unbroken GND plane under the whole board. In2 keeps the 3V3 pour with its RAW_PROTECTED, 3V3A and PORT_4V8 islands
(`r12_planes.tsv`, unchanged shapes) and now also carries low- and medium-speed signal tracks, the 3V3 pour filling
round them (zone clearance 0.2 mm, minimum fill width 0.25 mm; **correction, D-R12-128:** the 0.2 mm zone clearance is not
in effect, the DRU's 0.1 mm floor governs the fills, Measured):
- **Which nets may use In2** (`r12_aid/plan.py` `in2_ok`): every signal net except the fast interfaces (HSE, USB data,
  QSPI, SPI1, IMU_CS, microSD: regex `FAST`), the power nets with their own widths, the plane nets and the fire path
  (F.Cu, no via, unchanged). Servos, sense dividers, LEDs, port and camera UARTs, pyro control and sense, I2C,
  BAT_FAULT_N, PORT_SENSE, SWD / NRST may.
- **Where:** no In2 track inside an island (0.5 mm from its outline), under U1's 3V3 core (a 10 × 10 mm square round
  the ball field, `IN2_CORE` 5 mm half-size: the supply-ball dog-bones), within 2.35 mm of J121's barrel centres (S-8:
  pyro barrels ≥ 1 mm from foreign inner copper; In2 tracks count as inner copper), or nearer than 0.55 mm + clearance
  to any plated hole wall (JLC inner PTH rule). No via of any net within 2.1 mm of J121's barrels. The S-8 rule areas
  in `r12_route.py` now also forbid tracks and vias on their layers, so KiCad's DRC enforces it.
- **Reference:** a fast net's B.Cu track has In2 3V3 as its reference, so no In2 track runs under one and a fast net's
  B.Cu search keeps off In2 tracks (`Router.ref_block`). In2 tracks are referenced to In1 GND and to the B.Cu GND pour.
- **Costs:** In2 costs 1.4 per step against F / B's 1.0 (`R12_IN2`), but a slow net whose pads are more than 6 mm apart
  prefers it (0.7, `R12_IN2_SPAN`, `R12_IN2_PREF`), and those long slow nets are routed first after the dog-bone
  escapes, so the long aft-to-U1 runs leave F / B to the fast interfaces. A through via joins all three signal layers;
  vias to In2 are layer changes for the GND partner-via rule.
- **3V3 current path:** the 3V3 load is bounded by U2 (LMR50410, 1 A). IPC-2152 sizing for 1 A on 0.5 oz inner copper at a
  10 °C rise needs about 0.75 mm (Inference: a chart reading, not a calculation from the standard's tables); the check
  (scratch `plane3v3.py`, to become a check_r12 K-plane rule in stage D) requires the 3V3 fill opened by 0.5 mm, a
  1.0 mm neck, to keep U2's output vias and U1's core in one piece.
- **Placement:** C24 (NRST 100 nF) moves from (17.6, 44.6) rotation 90 to (16.6, 45.0) rotation 0: its pads sat in
  front of U1's F1 / F2 stubs (PYRO1_CONT, ACT_EN_MCU), which then could not leave westward in any draft.
- **Gate:** route_check's r10 rules already judge In2 copper (R10 routed on In2); check_r12's K-region / K-reference
  ports (stage D) must read In2 as a signal layer: K-region checks a slow / port net's In2 run sits over one In2
  reference and K-reference samples B.Cu fast tracks against the In2 3V3 fill.
**Result (2026-11-04):** In2 carries 60–90 tracks per draft (the 3V3 pour one piece in a2, but one 3V3 via cut off in
a4: the aid needs a pour-connectivity guard), but the rip-up search
still plateaus at 15–22 failed units (best a4: 15, 23 DRC unconnected; stageC.md §4.1), so no copper table is frozen.
Next: a negotiated-congestion search (stageC.md §5).

**D-R12-35 Negotiated congestion (PathFinder) replaces the rip-up search in the drafting aid (2026-11-04).** `astar.c`
now takes a per-node, per-layer float cost (`ncost`) and a per-node via cost (`vcost`) instead of the unused `avoid`
array; with `goal_via` the end via pays its congestion cost too (a "paid" state bit). `route.py` gains a negotiated mode
(`Router.pf_setup`, `Board.soft`): routed copper is recorded but not merged, every search sees only the fixed copper
(U1 fan-out, necks, links) as hard obstacles, and another unit's copper costs `pres × (units it conflicts with) +
history` per node, using the hard mode's own rules (clearance, via keep and via spacing, the fast-net In2 reference
rule; `Router.congestion`). `route.conflicts()` checks every pair of routed units exactly (segment geometry) after
each iteration; the history grows by `R12_PF_HIST` on every conflicting spot and `pres` by ×1.6 up to `R12_PF_PMAX`.
`plan.py --pf N` runs it: the first `R12_PF_FULL` iterations re-route every unit in the fixed order (deterministic,
PYTHONHASHSEED=0), later ones only the units in conflict, failed or cutting the pour; the best iteration is kept
(`--save-state` / `--load-state` pickle it), then `finish()` re-routes the still-conflicting units against all other
copper with the old rip-up search. An expansion-limit stop retries with a heavier heuristic weight, a larger budget,
and last with the costs capped; a path whose own vias end up closer than 0.6 mm retries with a longer straight run
after each via. Side fixes found on the way: `own_block` no longer walls a fan-out in behind a fixed pad-to-pad link of
its own net (the U4 GND 2–3 / 6–7 and R154–R157 pairs could not fan out on an empty board), and a cluster lead may join
its tree from its own fan-out vias.
**Result (2026-11-04, scratch drafts r1 … x3):** the full-board negotiation converges from about 300 conflicts to
8–20 and then oscillates; no setting tried (present-factor cap 15–1000, history 0.3–1.5, turn cost 2–4, In2 core
0.05–5.0 mm, incremental after 8–10 iterations, 40–120 iterations) reached zero, and the hard-mode finish leaves 8
connections. The residual conflicts sit in one place: U1's west side (x 14–20, y 35–48: the H1 3V3A cluster's run to
its island, SERVO3 / SERVO4, CAM_5V_SENSE, NRST, the C24 GND fan-out, the crystal) plus a few under U1 on B
(dog-bone escapes of VCAP2 / QSPI / SD). See stageC.md §4.2.

**D-R12-36 3V3 In2 pour guard (2026-11-04).** In negotiated mode the aid models the In2 3V3 fill on the 0.1 mm grid
(`Plan.pour3v3`: the fill keeps 0.2 mm from foreign copper, the islands, the board edge and the S-8 keep-outs), opens it
by 0.5 mm (a 1.0 mm neck, D-R12-34's IPC-2152 figure) and labels the pieces (`label4()` in `astar.c`); every 3V3 via
must lie in the piece holding most of them. Inside U1's 10 × 10 mm box (`core_guard`), where the supply-ball dog-bones
sit at 0.8 mm pitch and no 1 mm neck can exist between antipads, the plain fill (0.25 mm minimum width) counts instead,
and the box joins the rest through 1 mm necks. Each isolated via adds history cost on the In2 tracks and vias that
bound its piece (`Plan.plane_penalty`), and their units count as conflicting, so the negotiation moves them. The
check is an Inference until `plane3v3.py` (KiCad's real fill) or check_r12's K-plane rule (stage D) confirms it on the
frozen board.

**D-R12-37 Local fixes (2026-11-04).** (a) R401's GND pad (boxed in by the port buck's feedback divider R404 / R400 and
U400) is joined on F.Cu to C94.2 (GND, 2.4 mm away) and shares C94.2's fan-out (`plan.py`, a fixed `join`). (b) The
3V3A H1 cluster and the PORT_4V8 cluster fan their lead pad out first and then join the tree (the old order boxed the
lead pad in behind the cluster's own tracks); the 3V3A lead's In2 tier may place its intermediate vias in the 3V3 pour
and only the end via in the island (`goal_mask`). No part moved; no pyro, gate-network or fire-path part touched.

**D-R12-38 U1 stub-tip vias (2026-11-04).** A ring-1 / ring-2 escape stub may now also be reached from B or In2 through
a via at its tip, 0.8 mm outside the ball field (a dog-bone at the stub end), where the via is legal; the stub itself is
unchanged. It adds one via to that connection (route_check extra_vias still budgets two per critical net).

**D-R12-39 Negotiation: adaptive first window and focus mode (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** In negotiated mode a path
always exists inside the first search window (another unit's copper only costs), so a unit could never detour more than
4 mm round congestion. A unit now gets `R12_PF_WIN` mm more first-window margin per iteration it has spent in conflict
(up to 16 mm). `R12_PF_FOCUS=N` adds a focus mode: after N iterations without a better score, every unit not in
conflict is frozen (merged into the base fields as a hard obstacle, `route.Board.freeze`) and only the conflicting units
negotiate; a unit that then finds no path thaws the frozen units with copper within 2 mm of its failing pad. `plan.py
--save-final` pickles the state after clean and partners. **Result:** neither converges to zero. Best run (f3: turn 2.5,
window 1.5 mm, present-factor cap 50, history 0.6, 60 iterations) reached 8 conflicts (iteration 42) and then oscillated
between 8 and 13; focus mode (f2) plateaued at 12-18.

**D-R12-40 J3 pin-pair links and a search fix (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** (a) USB_DM's A7 / B7 link is now on F.Cu,
round USB_DP's two link vias (39.12 -> 37.75, 45 degrees to x 37.4, 0.3 mm straight, back), so DM has no via at J3:
route_check flagged the old B link as a needless hop (extra_vias). DP keeps its B link (its F stubs cross DM's row). DM
starts from the link's straight middle (`usb_starts`; the link does not wall in its own starts). (b) Bug: a linked group's
link vias were both starts and goals of its own search, so USB_DP / USB_DM were joined to themselves and never routed to
U1 in any earlier draft (each draft's DRC "unconnected" list included them); a goal at a start node is now dropped. This
also stops a cluster lead joining its own fan-out via as a zero-length path.

**D-R12-41 Hand-drawn fixed copper (2026-11-04).** `m3_design/r12_aid/hand_fixed.tsv` (r12_routes.tsv columns) is fixed
copper drawn before any search; its nets are complete and are not searched (`plan.py HAND_TSV`, `Plan.hand`). First entry:
PYRO1_OUT, the fire path (F.Cu, no via, AGENTS section 2): J121.1 round J121.2's west side to Q124.3 (0.8 mm, a 0.5 mm
pad-entry neck reaching Q124.3's copper 0.81 mm from the width change), Q124.3 -> C128.1 -> R131.1 at 0.4 mm (0402 pad
limit), and the 0.2 mm continuity-sense branch R131.1 -> R173.1 under D120 and up the 0.7 mm gap between D120 and the
R129 / R130 / R178 column. About 26 mm of copper against the net's 20.04 mm octilinear MST (route_check detour limit
1.6 x); every searched draft drew this net with three parallel runs (detour ratio 2.48). Clearances to J121.2, Q124's
other pads, C128.2, R131.2, D120 and the R129 column were computed by hand from the placed pads (Calculation; KiCad DRC
re-checks them on the frozen board). Nothing moved in the pyro cell; no gate-network part, no via.

**D-R12-42 Partner-aware vias outside the negotiation (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** In hard mode (finish, clean,
hand-driven re-routes) a critical net (AGENTS section 3 partner list) may place a via only where a legal GND via site
lies within 1.0 mm in x and in y (so within 1.41 mm, inside the 1.5 mm rule): `Plan.partner_block`, a box filter of the
GND via-legal mask; `R12_PARTNER_BLOCK=0` turns it off. The partner pass failed 4-18 times per draft before.

**D-R12-43 Fixed B.Cu escapes for U1's signal dog-bones (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** Most residual conflicts of the
D-R12-35 drafts were under U1 on B (BOOT0, VCAP1 / VCAP2, SD_CS / SD_MOSI, SPI1_MOSI, CAM_MCU_RX). Under the ball field
B.Cu is a grid of lanes along the ball rows and columns, 0.4 mm from the interstitial via sites on either side (one
0.1 mm track per lane, 0.125 mm to the via rings). `Plan.b_lanes` gives each of the 13 signal dog-bones (ARM1_SENSE,
BAT_FAULT_N, BOOT0, CAM_MCU_RX, IMU_INT1, PORT_SENSE, PYRO1_GATE_N, QSPI_CLK, SD_CS, SD_MOSI, SPI1_MOSI, VCAP1, VCAP2) a
diagonal from its via to one of its four lane crossings and a straight run along that lane to 0.8 mm past the outer ball
row, crossings exclusive (so no two escapes touch), toward the net's other pads; the lane end on B is the net's terminal
and B under the field is closed to every search. VCAP1 / VCAP2 are 0.1 mm there (a 0.2 mm track does not fit a lane):
adequate for the VCAP pins' decoupling current is an Inference, not checked against ST's AN4938 / DS12110 guidance.
**Result:** the under-U1 B conflicts are gone from every later draft (f1-f4, g1).

**D-R12-44 Placement: the U1 cluster 10 mm forward (2026-11-04).** The board's forward 40 % (y 58-88) was nearly empty
while every draft's conflicts sat round U1 (y 39-49), whose west flank faced the pyro cell (x <= 13.5 up to y 51.5). In
`m3_design/r12_place.tsv`: U1 to (23, 54) with its decoupling (C13-C22, C24, C26), R5, R89, U3 and its C29 / R10, the SD
pull-ups R460-R464 and J430 (20, 78), all +10 mm; Y1 (14.6, 52.5) and C25 (14.6, 49.6) (C127's courtyard); D6 (41.2,
57.6); C84 (40, 74.5); J2 (38.6, 84); LED1 / R32 (30.5, 75.6 / 73.6); LS120 (7.2, 85); the buzzer driver Q123 / R162 /
R163 / D123 / TP88 to the free west-forward area (6-10.5, 74-77.5). The outline, the supports, every connector, U4 / U5
(roll axis unchanged) and the whole pyro cell (J121, Q120-Q124, R134, their gate R / C) are unchanged. `r12_pcb.py`'s
legality checks pass (courtyards, edges, washer / standoff radii). **Result:** the U1 flanks open, but the residual
conflicts move to the U1 stub tips (adjacent tips 0.4-0.8 mm apart cannot all take a via) and to the aft power pocket
(servo network, PORT_4V8 cluster): the best negotiation reached 8 conflicts (D-R12-39), against 15-20 before.

**D-R12-45 Pour repair and dead-copper pruning (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** `Plan.no_in2` routes listed nets without
In2. A hard-mode re-route (the finish) ignores the D-R12-36 pour guard and cut U2's 3V3 output region off the pour in
f3 (15 isolated 3V3 vias: CAM_5V_SENSE and LED_YEL In2 runs closed the gaps between the RAW_PROTECTED island, the 3V3A
island and the east edge); re-routing the In2 culprits without In2 restores it (0-1 isolated). `m3_design/r12_aid/prune.py`
drops dead copper of non-plane nets (track ends touching nothing, vias with copper on fewer than two layers) and, with
`--loops`, the longest segments whose removal leaves every pad group joined (route_check track_loop); on the c1 draft it
cut route_check from 52 to 35 findings.

**D-R12-46 3V3A: a fixed feed from H1 to the island (2026-11-04).** Every searched draft joined the H1 cluster (U1.H1,
C21, C22) to the 3V3A island by a long F.Cu run (h1: about 150 mm round the west, forward and east edges; route_check
chain_detour 152 mm against 32 mm). 3V3A is now complete hand copper in `r12_aid/hand_fixed.tsv`: the nine island fan-outs
of the h1 draft (C10 two vias, R164, C30, C32, U5.6, U4.5, U4.8, C31), a via at H1's stub tip (18.6, 56.0), an In2 0.3 mm
feed from it straight aft along x 19.0 into the island at (19.5, 36.5) (about 19.6 mm), and H1 -> C22.1 -> C21.1 on F.Cu
(0.3 mm, between C24 and C22 at y 55.6). The feed keeps the west-side U1 nets (servos, pyro sense / enable, NRST) west of
it on In2. Clearances were computed from the placed pads; KiCad DRC on this copper alone (with PYRO1_OUT) shows no
clearance violation (scratch check). `plan.py` drops a hand net's `cluster:` and `fan:` units from the queue.

**D-R12-47 Seed copper: fixed stub-tip vias (drafting aid, 2026-11-04).** `r12_aid/hand_seed.tsv` (`plan.py
HAND_SEED_TSV`, `R12_HAND_SEED` to override) is fixed copper drawn before the search like `hand_fixed.tsv`, but its nets
are still searched. Entries: SERVO3_MCU and SERVO4_MCU leave their stub tips diagonally to vias at (18.2, 55.2) /
(18.2, 56.8) (the 3V3A tip via of D-R12-46 sits between their tips, so neither can take a via at its own tip, and on F.Cu
the tips are walled in by C24 / C22 and the other west stubs); LED_YEL takes a via at its tip (24.6, 49.6), among the
north SPI1 / servo stubs. None is a critical net (no GND partner). This is §5 item 2's second option (fixed tip vias
for slow nets). The stagger (alternate stub lengths) was not adopted: on the west side the long tips would reach x 17.8,
0.5 mm from C24 / C22, and close the F.Cu corridor (x 17.3-18.4) the west nets use.

**D-R12-48 Servo pocket not moved; routing still unfinished (2026-11-04).** §5 item 1 (D121 and R154-R157 into the band
y 38-47) was laid out (D121 rotated 90 at (21.5, 42), the pull-downs on the lines' x) and dropped before any run: that
band is the corridor of U1's north-side fan-out to U4 (SPI1_SCK / MISO / MOSI, IMU_CS, IMU_INT1) and to the west; in the
h1 draft it is full of routing, and the parts would sit across the SPI1 runs. Runs on the D-R12-44 placement with
D-R12-46 / -47 (`--pf 60`, f3's settings; scratch r12c7, not frozen): v1 (S3 / S4 seeds) / v2 (+ LED_YEL) / v3 (v1, turn
2.0) / v4 (v2, turn 2.0, window 2.5) reached 15 / 7 / 12 / 9 conflicts; after the finish, `--clean`, partners and prune:
13 / 14 / 22 / 10 DRC unconnected, route_check 49 / 27 / 41 / 64 (v3 without the clean pass). Hard finishing of the leftovers (rip-up with up to 25
units per connection) and a local negotiation (the units within 2 mm of the failing connections, everything else
frozen, 40 iterations) both made things worse (12-32 unconnected): every leftover crosses 10-18 routed units on its
shortest path. The best draft is still the h1 lineage: h1 + the H1 cluster re-routed In2-first and its two dead GND
fan-outs re-done (h2), then SD_SCK routed with the partner block off and four vias allowed (h5): 4 unconnected
(the 3V3A C21.1-C22.1 link, LED_YEL, SERVO3_MCU, RADIO_TX_MCU) and one dangling GND via; route_check 47. Not frozen.

**D-R12-49 Owner: 250 °C-rated parts accepted on Economic's 255 ± 5 °C reflow; "go fully Economic" (owner, 2026-11-04).**
Owner instruction (via the supervisor): every JLC-placed part must be allowed on JLC Economic PCBA, and parts whose maker
rating is a 250 °C peak are accepted on Economic's 255 ± 5 °C profile (an owner deviation; JLC's "Standard Only" flag is a
separate rule and still applies). Kept as they are, so `economic_parts.md` §3.3 / §3.4 swaps are **not** made: D5 / D6 /
D9 SS34 (MDD maker rating 250 °C, Datasheet), D121 Semtech SRV05-4 (TL 260 °C / 10 s lead soldering; body reflow
unverified) and J430 Molex 472192001 (250 °C search-reported, Inference). The barometer goes back to the MS5607 family (D-R12-52), which is rated 250 °C,
40 s max (TE MS5607-02BA03, 06/2017, "Maximum Soldering Temperature", Datasheet). What a 5–10 °C over-peak does to each
of these parts (package, sensor offset, contact plating) is an **Inference** not checked with any maker; TE gives a
+0.4 mbar reflow impact with 7 days' recovery at its own profile (Datasheet), not at 255–260 °C. Owner acceptance row A-9.
Changes that remain: U4 (JLC "Standard Only", D-R12-50 / -51) and U5 (LPS28DFW, "Standard Only", D-R12-52).

**D-R12-50 U4: ISM6HG256XTR → ST LSM6DSVETR (C41785564), same land and nets.** ISM6HG256XTR is JLC "Standard Only" and
unorderable (−3, `economic_parts.md` §3.1). LSM6DSVETR is JLC Economic, Extended, 502 in stock / 430 orderable, $6.1927
(Live 2026-11-04T23:25:09Z). Range: ±2 / 4 / 8 / 16 g, ±125 … ±4000 dps, 1.71–3.6 V, SPI / I²C / I3C (JLC listing and the
LCSC page https://www.lcsc.com/product-detail/C41785564.html, whose description is ST's LSM6DSV text word for word). F1
(±4000 dps) is met; the accelerometer is the low-g channel only (F2 moves to U6, D-R12-51). Pins: JLCEDA's C41785564
symbol (https://easyeda.com/api/products/C41785564/components, read 2026-11-04) gives 1 SDO/SA0, 2 SDX, 3 SCX, 4 INT1,
5 VDD_IO, 6 / 7 GND, 8 VDD, 9 INT2, 10 OCS_AUX, 11 SDO_AUX, 12 CS, 13 SCL, 14 SDA, the ISM6HG256X's functions pin for pin
(its JLCEDA symbol, C51931514), and its package name LGA-14_L3.0-W2.5-P0.50-TL is exactly U4's existing land: **land and
nets kept** (SDX / SCX to GND, INT2 / OCS_AUX / SDO_AUX NC, as R10 / R11 wired the ISM6HG256X). Not read: an ST datasheet
for the "E" variant (st.com has no LSM6DSVE page or datasheet, 404 on 2026-11-04; the LCSC PDF is empty), so the
identity LSM6DSVE = LSM6DSV (DS13510) pin map, axis frame and register set is an **Inference**; so is its reflow rating
(JLC flags it Economic, MSL 3). Firmware: an LSM6DSV-family driver (Inference, close to the ISM6HG256X's low-g / gyro
registers; the high-g block is gone).

**D-R12-51 New high-g accelerometer U6: NXP FXLS90322AESR2 (C5190712) on the IMU SPI bus, chip select HG_CS on PA10 (moved to PC11, ball B8, by D-R12-64A).**
F2 (≥ ±64 g axial against the 37 g flight peak) moves to U6. JLC Economic (MSL 3), Extended, 1348 / 1348, $2.8717 (Live).
Evidence: (a) **axes and range**: NXP's part page (https://www.nxp.com/part/FXLS90322AES, read 2026-11-04) calls it an
"XY Dual-Axis Medium-g Inertial Sensor", Active; distributor and NXP-community text give medium-g ranges ±15.5 … ±150 g
(search summaries, Inference until the dual-channel datasheet FXLS9xxxx Rev 7 is read: nxp.com served "not found" to
the browser and refused scripted access). The JLC listing's "±500 g, XYZ" is wrong for this part number (it is the
family's high-g ceiling). ±150 g ≥ ±64 g: F2 conditionally met (Inference from the same search summaries;
O-R12-21), with the user range set in firmware. (b) **SPI without DSI3 / PSI5**:
FXLS9xxx0 Rev 6 (LCSC copy for C5190712, the single-channel sheet) Table 84 / 85: FXLS90xxx parts ship with COMMTYPE 000
(DSI3 or 32-bit SPI); with VCC on pins 1 / 13 / 15 / 16 (Figure 5, Table 9) the part runs SPI (Datasheet for the
single-channel sheet; that the dual-channel part matches is an Inference). I²C is not available on COMMTYPE 000, so U6
stays on SPI. SPI: 32-bit frames with CRC, SCLK period ≥ 88 ns (≤ 11.3 MHz), POR to first command 0.4–0.7 ms (Rev 6
items 10794, 10937). (c) **Supply**: VCC_BUF 3.135–5.25 V, ≤ 6.0 mA in SPI (items 10501 / 10502 / 10510). U6 is on 3V3,
whose budget window is 3.240–3.464 V (drift case, Calculation / Inference, `r12_windows.json`; the 3V3 lower limit is now 3.135 V; 3.224–3.516 V on the PFM band since D-R12-139, 89 mV over U6's floor): 105 mV over
U6's floor at the regulator; copper drop and load steps at U6 are an **Inference** (6 mA). (d) **Land**: Rev 6 Figure 110
/ 111 (drawing 98ASA00761D rev F, SOT1688-1(SC)), new footprint `M3_FXLS_HLQFN16` (pads 0.5 × 0.85 at 1.975, die pad
2.6, mask +0.05, paste −0.3 on the die pad; since D-R12-129 the eight corner lands carry NXP's 0.143 mm inner-corner
cut, Figure 104 / 110 / 111: corner gap 0.344 mm, mask web 0.244 mm, was 0.141 / 0.041 mm). (e) **Pins and decoupling**: Table 9 / Figure 9: 1, 13, 15, 16 VCC; 7, 14
and the die pad 17 VSS; 8 SS_B (internal pull-up); 9 SCLK, 10 MOSI (internal pull-downs); 11 MISO; 2 PCM1/ARM1 (output, left open; NXP dual-channel Rev 7.0 Figure 9 / Table 9 read in round 12, D-R12-188), 3–6 TEST and
12 PCM0 / ARM0 unterminated (NXP's recommendation); one 0.1 µF X7R ≥ 10 V at VCC (Table 6 C1): **C33** (C307331, 100 nF
50 V X7R 0402, Basic). (f) **Reflow**: NXP part page: MSL 3, peak package body 260 °C for 40 s (Datasheet-class maker
page). Chip select: **PA10 (ball D10, R11 NC)** as `HG_CS`, a push-pull GPIO; at reset it is analog and SS_B's internal
pull-up holds U6 deselected, and the H74x loader's USART1_RX pull-up on PA10 (AN2606) does the same; D10 is on the outer
ring (east column) and is not a neighbour of PE4 (C3) or PC1 (F2). No interrupt: the FXLS has no data-ready pin (PCM0 /
ARM0 is a PCM / arming output), so firmware polls at its sample rate. Not taken: ADXL375 / KX134 / H3LIS331DL (JLC
Standard Only), FXLS93230 (PSI5 only), ADXL1005 (5 in stock, $62). Remaining Inference: axis frame and range configuration pending the specific dual-channel checks,
the 3V3 drop at U6, firmware CRC / frame handling. Pin 2’s name is Datasheet, not Inference (D-R12-188).

**D-R12-52 U5: LPS28DFWTR → TE MS5607-02BA03 (C97627) on R11's MS5611 land and wiring (supersedes D-R12-13).**
LPS28DFW is JLC "Standard Only" (`economic_parts.md` §3.2); the owner's 250 °C acceptance (D-R12-49) allows the MS5607,
which is JLC Economic, Extended, 3645 / 3540, $4.3854 (Live). TE MS5607-02BA03 datasheet (06/2017, LCSC copy
https://www.lcsc.com/datasheet/lcsc_datasheet_2010281834_TE-Connectivity-MS560702BA03-50_C97627.pdf): operating range
10–1200 mbar, full accuracy 300–1100 mbar, VDD 1.8–3.6 V, peak supply current 1.4 mA, max soldering 250 °C / 40 s. F3
(≥ 300 hPa readings) kept, and the 260 hPa / 10 km margin is inside the extended (linear) range, at reduced accuracy
(Datasheet). Pins (Datasheet "Pin configuration"): 1 VDD, 2 PS (high = I²C), 3 GND, 4 / 5 CSB, 6 SDO, 7 SDI/SDA, 8 SCLK,
the MS5611-01BA03's order, so R11's land `SENSORS-SMD_MS5611-01BA03` and symbol `M3:Barometer` are reused (copied from
R11's pinned kilib); the land's 1.25 mm pitch and 2.2 mm row spacing match JLCEDA's C97627 land (pads 0.7 × 1.2 there,
0.6 × 1.1 on R11's). Wiring, R11's: VDD and PS on 3V3A (I²C), CSB to GND ("do not leave unconnected"; address 0x77),
SDO NC (I²C uses SDA only, Datasheet table "Pin PS / Mode / Pins used"), SDA / SCL on I2C1 with R12 / R13; 100 nF C32
at VDD ("a 100 nF ceramic capacitor must be placed as close as possible", Datasheet). Nothing of the LPS28DFW is left:
its land, model and symbol are deleted from the R12 library; it had no extra parts (R64 was already deleted). R10's
`BARO_no_inter_row_tracks` rule area is drawn again (TE pad layout: "do not route tracks between pads"). The 250 °C
rating is the D-R12-49 deviation.

**D-R12-53 Placement for D-R12-50 … -52.** U4 unchanged (23.0, 31.6, 180). **U5** (29.0, 32.0) now rotation 180 so SDA /
SCL (pins 7 / 8) face R12 / R13 and U1, VDD / PS next to C32; its 5.5 × 4.6 mm courtyard needed **R12 / R13** 0.2 mm east
(x 32.8). **U6** at (27.5, 40.5), rotation 180, between U4 and U1 east of the roll axis: the free block x 25–31, y 38–43
on the IMU SPI1 path, clear of U1's west and aft fan-out (U1 courtyard starts at y ≈ 49.5); after rotation SS_B / SCLK /
MOSI / MISO (pins 8–11) sit on the west and aft-west edge facing the SPI1 runs (x 21–24). **C33** at (30.7, 42.0),
rotation 90, by U6's VCC corner (pins 1, 13–16). All on F; placed-board DRC, fit_check r12 and check_r12 placement pass.

**D-R12-54 IMU axis maps on the top face (closes O-R12-15 as a calculation; bench check stays).** U4 (top, rotation 180):
R11's signed frame (D-R11-11: bottom, rotation 180 after the flip, +X_imu → −x_board, +Y_imu → −y_board) gives the
footprint frame +X_imu = −x_fp, +Y_imu = +y_fp; on the top at rotation 180 this is **+X_imu → +x_board (east), +Y_imu →
−y_board (aft), +Z_imu out of the top face** (right-handed). Roll about +y_board (forward) reads **−gyro_Y**; forward
thrust reads **−a_Y**; lever arm x = 0.000 mm (Calculation / Inference from R11's frame; that the LSM6DSVE uses the ISM6HG256X / LSM6DSV
family frame is an Inference). U6 (top, rotation 180): FXLS9xxx0 Rev 6 Figure 8 / Table 8 give +X = −x_fp (towards pins
1–4) and +Y = −y_fp (towards pins 13–16) for the single-channel parts; rotated 180 on the top: **channel 0 (X) → +x_board
(east, lateral), channel 1 (Y) → +y_board (forward, the rocket axis)**: forward thrust reads +a on channel 1 (Inference:
read from the figure, and the dual-channel part's frame is assumed equal). Both signs need a 1 g bench check before
flight; firmware must not reuse R11's map without it.

**D-R12-55A 3V3A at the MS5607 and C32 without vias of their own (routing attempt A, 2026-11-04).** The D-R12-46 fan-outs
of the old U5 land (U5.6 via at (27.4, 30.1), C32 via at (28.5, 29.9)) now sit in U5's pads and in the BARO_body no-via
area. In `r12_aid/hand_fixed.tsv` the MS5607's VDD (pad 1) and PS (pad 2) and C32 take no via: R164.2 -> C32.1 on F.Cu at
y 29.0 (0.3 mm, 0.54 mm from C60.1 / R61.1 and 0.8 mm from U5's pads), C32.1 -> U5.2 on a 0.2 mm diagonal past C32.2
(0.147 mm edge clearance, Calculation), U5.2 -> U5.1 on F.Cu at y 30.3 (between the pads of one row, outside the
no-track band y 31.45-32.55). The group reaches the island through R164.2's existing via (25.5, 29.9), next to the
3V3A source. Clearances computed from the placed pads; KiCad DRC on every attempt-A draft shows no violation on them.

**D-R12-56A 3V3A feed from H1 moved west of the stub-tip column (routing attempt A, 2026-11-04).** D-R12-46's In2 feed
along x 19.0 ran 0.4 mm from U1's west stub tips (x 18.6), so none of the west nets (HSE, NRST, ACT_EN_MCU, PYRO1_CONT,
CAM_5V_SENSE, servos) could take a tip via (a via needs 0.5 mm to a 0.3 mm track): putting the h5 draft on the new
placement, five west units broke against it. Now: H1 -> C22.1 on F.Cu as before; C22.1 -> C21.1 one straight 0.3 mm
track at x 15.5 (inside both pads; the old three-segment link was a route_check tiny_jog); C21.1 west to a via at
(14.7, 58.0); In2 0.3 mm diagonally to (13.0, 56.3), along x 13.0 to y 39.0, along y 38.0 and into the island's north
edge at (20.9, 36.5). In the h5 draft no via of another net lies within 0.5 mm of that path, and only ACT_EN_MCU's In2
run crosses it (re-routed by the search). The tip via (18.6, 56.0) is gone. Inference: the long In2 3V3A run (about
33 mm) is a supply feed with tens of mA, adequate at 0.3 mm (IPC-2152 not yet checked, stage D).

**D-R12-57A U6 same-part links (drafting aid, routing attempt A, 2026-11-04).** `plan.py u6_joins()`: U6's die pad 17 to
its GND pins 7 and 14, and VCC 15 -> 16 -> 1 plus 16 -> C33.1, as same-net F.Cu links, so the die pad and the three GND
pins share one GND fan-out and pins 1 / 15 / 16 and C33 one 3V3 fan-out (pin 13 sits beyond GND pin 14 and fans out
alone). In every attempt-A draft U6 and C33 connect (no U6 power item unconnected).

**D-R12-58A Routing attempt A on the D-R12-53 placement: not finished (2026-11-04).** Placed board `ec461057…`
(`r12_build.sh` stage A / B pass). Runs (scratch `r12ra`, not frozen): the h5 draft ported onto the new lands with the
changed-area nets re-routed (13-16 failures); two full negotiations (`--pf 60`, D-R12-48's settings, turn 2.0 / window
2.5 and turn 2.5 / window 1.5): 19 and 15 DRC unconnected, route_check 41 / 45; then a greedy hill-climb (each failing
unit re-routed with 0-30 rip-ups, kept only if failures plus isolated 3V3 vias drop, the pour repaired first): 12 DRC
unconnected; then the D-R12-59A PORT_4V8 feed, single-net re-routes (LED_YEL, SWDIO) and hand fixes (two GND fan-out
stubs joined to a neighbour's pad, two tip-via pairs 0.2 mm apart redrawn on F.Cu) and `prune.py --loops`: best draft
**7 DRC unconnected, no DRC violation, route_check 71** (31 staircase, 12 local_bump, 11 tiny_jog, 8 extra_vias and
singles). Unrouted: SERVO2_MCU, QSPI_NCS, QSPI_IO2, SD_MISO, VBUS_SENSE, CAM_MCU_TX, HG_CS. No table is frozen. Findings: (a)
**SPI1 topology**: walking round the region between U1's A-row stubs, U6's west face and U4's north face, U6's pins
(MISO 11, MOSI 10, SCK 9, north to south) and U4's (MISO 1, MOSI 14, SCK 13, east to west) come in the same rotational
order, so at most one of the three nets can join U6 and U4 on one layer; with MOSI's B.Cu dog-bone lane and the
MISO / SCK swap between U1's tips and U4, one SPI1 net needs three layer-changing vias (route_check extra_vias allows
two; an allowlist entry with this reason, or a U6 rotation / position change, is needed). (b) The leftovers sit where
D-R12-48 found them: each needs 4-17 routed units moved (blocker sets from a path against the fixed copper only), the
U1 A-row bundle (12 F.Cu stub tips and 7 B.Cu lane ends within x 19.4-25.8) and the forward / east band between U1,
U3 and J430. (c) PORT_4V8 needed a hand feed (D-R12-59A): no draft joined the buck cluster to the forward island.

**D-R12-59A PORT_4V8 feed along the east edge (seed copper, routing attempt A, 2026-11-04).** No search joined the port
buck's output (C406 / L400 / R400, y 17-21) to the PORT_4V8 island (y >= 88.4), and an In2 run would split the 3V3 pour
into east and west halves. `r12_aid/hand_seed.tsv`: C406.1 north to a via at (44.6, 16.6) (0.65 mm from C406.1 and
C434.2), a 0.5 mm B.Cu run along x 45.2 (0.55 mm from the board edge, 0.44 mm from J3's shell pads, no other B copper
east of x 44 in the drafts) and a via 0.6 mm inside the island at (44.8, 89.0). Seed copper: L400 / R400 and the island
pads are still searched and reach it. KiCad DRC on the attempt-A draft with it: no violation, PORT_4V8 joined. B.Cu
under it is GND pour on a board whose In1 is a solid GND plane (the cut is 0.5 mm wide along the edge).

**D-R12-60A Stage C pass 1 (connect first, hygiene later; owner, 2026-11-04): stalled at 6 unconnected.** From the o9
draft (D-R12-58A), each leftover was re-routed alone after ripping one or two of the routed nets its path against the
fixed copper crosses, those nets re-routed after it, every variant judged by KiCad DRC (8 in parallel, best kept; via
budget 6, partner block off, hygiene not judged). SD_MISO joined (BOOT0 re-routed round it); the best draft has 6 DRC
unconnected, no DRC violation (scratch `r12ra/BEST6.tsv`). Left: CAM_MCU_TX, HG_CS, QSPI_IO2, QSPI_NCS, SERVO2_MCU,
VBUS_SENSE. Every further single or pair swap leaves 6 or more, and each of the six alone stops at the search's expansion
limit even with a 12x heuristic weight and In2 at F / B cost: their U1 stub tips sit in the A-row bundle (12 F.Cu tips
and 7 B.Cu lane ends within x 19.4-25.8) and the east column, where every corridor out is taken. Finishing them needs a
change of the fan-out itself (stub / lane assignment at the A row and column 10, or a DS12110-checked pin swap to a ball
facing its pads), not more re-routing. No table is frozen.

**D-R12-61A … -64A U1 re-pins for the stalled fan-out (stage C pass 1, routing attempt A, 2026-11-04).** D-R12-60A
showed that the six leftovers could not finish because U1's aft row (row A / B) and east column carried nets whose pads
lie on the other side of the board (QSPI_NCS and the microSD bus forward-bound from the aft rows, I2C forward of U1 for
a barometer aft of it, HG_CS / VBUS_SENSE on the crowded east column). Moved, so that every net leaves the ball field on
the side facing its pads (`m3_design/r12_sch.py` `R12_MOVES`; the pin table, schematic and netlist are generated from it;
every new ball was NC; none is a ball next to PE4 (C3) or PC1 (F2), and PE4, PC1, PC0, PC5 and the GPS / radio / AUX
UARTs are unchanged):

| Decision | Net | Was | Now | Function used |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-R12-61A | QSPI_NCS | PB6 (B5, aft ring 2) | **PB10 (J7, forward ring 2)** | QUADSPI_BK1_NCS (the only other BK1_NCS ball on TFBGA-100) |
| D-R12-62A | I2C1_SCL | PB10 (J7) | **PA8 (D9, east ring 2)** | I2C3_SCL (peripheral I2C2 → I2C3; net names kept) |
| D-R12-62A | I2C1_SDA | PB11 (K7) | **PC9 (E9, east ring 2)** | I2C3_SDA |
| D-R12-63A | SD_SCK | PC10 (B9, aft) | **PE12 (J6, forward ring 2)** | SPI4_SCK (peripheral SPI3 → SPI4) |
| D-R12-63A | SD_MISO | PC11 (B8, aft) | **PE13 (K6, forward ring 1)** | SPI4_MISO |
| D-R12-63A | SD_MOSI | PC12 (C8) | **PE14 (G7, dog-bone, B lane forward)** | SPI4_MOSI |
| D-R12-63A | SD_CS | PD0 (D8) | **PA7 (K3, forward ring 1)** | GPIO out, high before output mode |
| D-R12-64A | HG_CS | PA10 (D10, east) | **PC11 (B8, aft ring 2)** | GPIO out, high before output mode (U6 SS_B pull-up holds it deselected at reset) |
| D-R12-64A | VBUS_SENSE | PC8 (F9, east ring 2) | **PA10 (D10, east ring 1, the ball HG_CS left)** | GPIO in |

Freed: PB6 (B5), PB11 (K7), PC10 (B9), PC12 (C8), PD0 (D8), PC8 (F9), now NC (firmware: analog). QSPI_IO2 cannot move:
QUADSPI_BK1_IO2 exists only on PE2 (A3) in this package. SERVO2_MCU (PA15 TIM2_CH1) and CAM_MCU_TX (PD5 USART2_TX) keep
their balls: the D-R12-66A runs routed them once the moved nets left the aft rows. VBUS_SENSE was tried on PC10 (B9) as
well: the two negotiations with it there stalled at 12-21 conflicts, against 3 with PA10 (D-R12-66A). **Sources:** balls from DS12110 Rev 8
Table 9 (pp. 64-88); the alternate functions (QUADSPI_BK1_NCS on PB10, I2C3_SCL / SDA on PA8 / PC9, SPI4_SCK / MISO /
MOSI on PE12 / PE13 / PE14) were read from KiCad 10's `MCU_ST_STM32H7` symbol `STM32H743V_G-I_Hx` (pin alternates
generated from ST's open pin data); st.com refused scripted access in this pass, so the DS12110 Tables 10-14 rows and
the AF numbers written in the pin table (AF9 / AF4 / AF5, ST's usual mapping for these peripherals) are an **Inference**
until the datasheet is re-read (O-R12-23). **Loader pins (Inference, AN2606 not re-read):** PA8 / PC9 (I2C3), PE12-PE14
(SPI4) and PB10 (USART3_TX) are among the H74x system-memory boot loader's interfaces; the ROM loader runs only on a
BOOT0 / option-byte boot, and the flash's NCS on PB10 is harmless while the loader idles (no QUADSPI command reaches the
flash). **Firmware binding:** the barometer bus is I2C3, the microSD bus SPI4, U3's NCS PB10.

**D-R12-65A Two U1 escapes changed in the drafting aid (2026-11-05).** `m3_design/r12_aid/plan.py`: (a)

of an F.Cu stub into the aft bundle; no B lane is free for it, so it keeps the free B escape under the field and reaches
U3.3 round the west and north sides of U1. (b) `NO_LANE = ('IMU_INT1',)`: IMU_INT1's dog-bone (C7) takes no fixed B lane:
the lane along column 8 ended at (25.0, 49.6), the SERVO2_MCU stub tip, between the LED_YEL and HG_CS tip vias, where no
path could leave it. (c) Hand-guided copper (scratch `seg.py`, searched with explicit waypoints, then judged by KiCad
DRC): IMU_INT1 leaves U4.4 east on F.Cu to a via at (24.9, 30.6), outside the IMU_body no-via area, and runs B.Cu north
to its dog-bone; SERVO2_MCU runs west of the IMU through a waypoint via at (19.5, 40.0) (it used to pass between U4 and
C30 and walled U4.4 in). Both are in the frozen table, not in `hand_fixed.tsv`.

**D-R12-66A Stage C pass 1 frozen: `m3_design/r12_routes.tsv` (2026-11-05).** Routing on the D-R12-61A … -64A pin map
(placed board `3b4f64da…`): four full negotiations (`--pf 60`, D-R12-48's settings with turn 2.0 / 2.5 and window
2.5 / 1.5): VBUS_SENSE on PA10 reached 3 conflicts (best D-R12-58A run: 10-17), on PC10 12-21; after the finish the
better run (turn 2.0, window 2.5) had 4 DRC unconnected (IMU_INT1, SPI1_SCK, SERVO2_MCU, QSPI_IO2) and no violation;
DRC-judged blocker swaps (scratch `swap.py`) took it to 2 (IMU_INT1, QSPI_IO2), and D-R12-65A to 0. Further runs with
IMU_INT1 on PB6 (B5) and a third setting (turn 2.2) and local negotiations of 10-26 units did not do better and are not
used. **Result** (`r12_build.sh`, twice, same hash): board `da48bb99031f30d7a79c4a985b83898543d62011ee13092409aca7f8cf395600`,
1423 tracks, 436 vias; KiCad DRC 0 violations, 0 unconnected, parity 0; fit_check r12 OK; check_r12 copper OK (V-1,
V-2); route_check r12 **83 failing** (28 staircase, 13 local_bump, 9 tiny_jog, 8 critical_via_no_gnd, 6 extra_vias, 5
detour, 3 acute_angle, 3 side_by_side_overlap, 2 fold_back and singles), which the owner's pass-1 rule allows: so
`r12_build.sh` reports route_check's findings without stopping until `R12_ROUTE_HYGIENE=strict` (pass 2). Safety checks
on this copper: the fire path (BAT_IN, PYRO1_OUT, PYRO1_IGN, RAW_ACT, ACT_FET_S) is F.Cu with no via; the S-8 keep-outs
are drawn by `r12_route.py` and DRC passes on them; no foreign F.Cu track enters U6's pad ring (|dx|, |dy| < 2.45 mm
from its centre) and the U4 / U5 rule areas pass DRC; the gate networks are unchanged (no gate part moved) and
D-R12-61A … -64A put no moved net on a PE4 / PC1 neighbour ball. **Correction (D-R12-86):** "K-neighbours are unchanged"
was wrong: check_r12 did not run K-neighbours, and F2's neighbour G3 carried PORT_SENSE (D-R12-05), which is not inert
(one F2-G3 bridge gave Q122 1.00-1.12 V). **Pass-1 deviations for pass 2:** QSPI_IO2 has six vias (its
dog-bone and five; the pass-1 allowance was a third via), and eight critical layer changes have no GND partner within
1.5 mm (route_check critical_via_no_gnd); every route_check finding above; the hygiene clean is pass 2.

**D-R12-67A Merge of the ported copper rules; two-tier copper check for pass 1 (2026-11-05).** `m3-assy-r12` (12c66e7:
`tools/check_r12.py` with the ported S- / W- / LC- / M- / K- rules and their review fixes, the R12 fab tooling) is
merged into `r12-route-a`. The D-R12-66A copper failed `check_r12 --stage copper` with 101 findings: S-8 47, S-9 8,
M-7 2, K-inner 10, K-region 32, K-reference 1, K-plane 1. `check_r12` gains `--advisory RULES`: the named rules still run
and are printed and reported (`advisory`, `reported`), but do not fail the run. Only `ADVISABLE` = K-reference, K-region,
K-plane (the signal-reference family) may be named; any S- / M- / W- / LC- rule (`ALWAYS_FATAL`) or any other rule is
refused (exit 2). `r12_build.sh` passes `--advisory K-reference,K-region,K-plane` while `R12_ROUTE_HYGIENE` is `report`
(pass 1, the default now) and nothing when it is `strict` (pass 2's default), the same switch that makes route_check's
hygiene fatal. Every other copper rule (S-, M-, W-, LC-, V-, K-inner, K-copper, K-fill, …) is fatal in both tiers.
Test: `tools/test_r12_check.py` `test_advisory_tier` (a K-plane split and an S-8 breach: K-plane reported only, S-8
still fails; S-8 / M-7 / W-1 / LC-1 / K-inner / K-copper refused as advisory, also on the command line).

**D-R12-68A S-8 on R12 copper (2026-11-05).** Every barrel of the eleven S-8 nets (check_r10 `BARREL_NETS`) and the
inner ring it keeps is now >= 1 mm from foreign In1 / In2 copper (check_r12 S-8: 0 findings). (a) `r12_route.py`
draws a fill-only rule area (fills kept out, tracks and vias allowed so its own copper may pass; S-8 holds foreign
copper off) round every via of an S-8 net: In2 always, In1 as well for the GND-hazard nets (ACT_GATE, ACT_DRV,
PYRO1_OUT); radius 1.35 mm (0.225 mm ring + 1.0 + 0.05 mm, over cos(pi / 16) for the 16-gon). J121's two keep-outs
(tracks, vias and fills) now cover both inner layers and have a 2.05 mm radius (the 1.9 mm inner ring: 0.95 + 1.0 +
0.05 mm, over cos(pi / 16)); D-R12-66A's J121.1 keep-out was In2 only, but PYRO1_OUT is a GND-hazard net, so In1 GND
counts (it sat 0.551 mm from the barrel). The fill voids alone cleared the vias of BAT_SW_RETURN (13.4 / 15.1, 7.5),
ACT_GATE (6.8, 30.1) / (8.6, 31.0), PYRO1_GATE (8.2, 45.6) / (4.4, 49.4) and PYRO1_GATE_N (17.6, 43.7). (b) PYRO1_GATE_N
(PE4, U1.C3) has no via: its dog-bone among the U1 3V3 / QSPI dog-bones could not keep 1 mm, so it leaves the ball

R126 / R127), drawn by hand (`r12_aid/hand_fixed.tsv`; `plan.py` `NO_ESCAPE` skips C3's dog-bone and closes its two
sites). (c) ACT_EN_MCU (PC1) keeps its stub-tip via at (18.6, 54.0); the non-GND vias within 1.46 mm moved: HSE_OUT runs
on F.Cu from its tip to Y1 (both its vias gone, CAM_5V_SENSE's F loop that forced them is gone), SERVO3_MCU's seed via
(18.2, 55.2) -> (18.1, 55.4) (`hand_seed.tsv`), PYRO1_CONT's via (17.6, 54.4) -> (17.1, 54.3) with C24.2's GND via
moved to (17.6, 54.9). ACT_EN_MCU's aft via (17.1, 28.2) -> (16.4, 28.2), 1.4 mm from SERVO4_MCU's In2 run. (d)
CAM_5V_SENSE (In2 from a via at (21.1, 41.0) to (17.5, 53.0), then F to its tip) and VLOGIC_SENSE (re-routed by the aid)
leave the band PE4 now crosses on F. PC1, PE4, PC0, PC5 and the port UART balls are unchanged; no part moved.

**D-R12-73A S-8 keep-outs at J121 measured from what each barrel keeps (2026-11-05).** J121.1 (PYRO1_OUT) already has no
inner ring (the R10 DB125 land's `remove_unused_layers` on pad 1, D-R20-05), so its keep-out (tracks, vias, fills; In1 +
In2, PYRO1_OUT being a GND-hazard net) is sized from the 1.30 mm barrel wall: 16-gon vertices at 1.72 mm, flats 1.687 mm,
1.037 mm beyond the wall (S-8's 1.0 mm holds) and inside check_r10's own-barrel void (wall + 1.071 = 1.721 mm), so
PYRO1_OUT's F.Cu leaving the pad keeps its In1 reference (K-reference: the 2.05 mm keep-out left 576 PYRO1_OUT and 145
PYRO1_IGN samples without In1). J121.2 (PYRO1_IGN) keeps its 1.9 mm ring and its 2.05 mm keep-out, on In2 only: under S-8
GND is foreign only to the GND-hazard nets, and a PYRO1_IGN-to-GND short loads the bus through R134 rather than firing.
`r12_route.py` `S8_KEEPOUT` now carries a radius per pad. S-8: 0 findings.

**D-R12-75A route_check R12 allowlist: two forced extra_vias (2026-11-05).** `tools/route_check.py` `ALLOWLIST_R12` /
`ROUTE_FIGURES_R12` (profile r12 now reads them; measured_check fails an entry whose route length or via count changes,
and a stale entry fails). SPI1_SCK, 3 vias, 19.34 mm (MST 19.43): the three SPI1 pin orders interleave round the
U4 / U6 / U1 region (U4 north face SCK, MOSI, MISO; U6 west face SCK, MOSI, MISO top to bottom; U1 A row MISO, SCK with
MOSI on C5's dog-bone), so SCK must cross MOSI at U6 and MISO toward U1 while MOSI (dog-bone + one) and MISO (one hop
under U6) sit at the two vias allowed; clearing it needs U6 rotated (an axis-map change). USB_DP, 3 vias, 19.22 mm (MST
16.4): J3's 0.5 mm interleave forces one of the pair to link its pins on B.Cu through two vias (D-R12-40), and both run
on B.Cu past the I2C1 / VBUS_SENSE / SWCLK F.Cu fan east of U1, so DP surfaces once more at B10 (DM has two vias).
`tools/test_r12_check.py` `test_route_profile_r12` checks the profile carries exactly these entries, each with a
D-R12 reason and route figures equal to ROUTE_FIGURES_R12. Non-critical hygiene leftovers stay informational on the
R10-like profiles (audit 2 item 7): 13, located in stageC.md §4.8.
