**D-R12-78 Third damper capacitor C79 (stage D, 2026-11-05).** R10's hot-plug model (2304 required corners, limits
unchanged) on the frozen stage C copper failed 8 / 2304 cases, minimum U9 IN −15.097 V against −15.0 V, all at the 2 µH
harness corner with high bulk and low damper capacitance (Simulation / Inference; `stageD_analysis.md` §8). C79, a third
CL31A226KAHNNNE 22 µF 1206 (C12891, Basic, already on the BOM: no new code or feeder fee, about $0.12 per board, Live),
sits in parallel with C89 / C95 between BAT_IN and BAT_DAMP at (16.4, 21.075), rotation 270: BAT_IN from C95.1 by
0.8 mm F.Cu, BAT_DAMP to C95.2 by 0.8 mm. `tools/r12_hotplug.py` models C79 as C89 / C95 are modelled, by asserted
string replacements on R10's model and solver source (neither R10 file is edited). Result on the final board: stageD.md
§3. R166 0.36 Ω and the damper stay (D-R12-09 decided: keep; R166 shorted fails 1164 / 2304, no damper 1920 / 2304,
the pre-C79 44 µF 8 / 2304; Simulation / Inference). Schematic: `circuit.json` added_parts C79, battery_entry sheet;
r12_sch_check 37 / 37 seeds, 187 parts.

**D-R12-79 C88 on the U9 feed (stage D).** R10's C88-topology check (every feed to U9.5 passes C88.1) failed: C88 hung on
a stub off C96.1. C88 moves to (14.3, 16.92), rotation 270, pad 1 north; the feed is C96.1 → 0.4 mm → C88.1 → 0.4 mm
(E, NE, N, NE, E) → U9.5, 3.03 mm and 3.72 mΩ, 0.99 nH (R10's local-path bounds 4 mm, 5 mΩ, 2.6 nH; Calculation / Inference: extracted inductance
model). Its GND via moves to (14.95, 17.34). To pass a 0.4 mm run under U9.4 the BAT_FAULT_N escape via moves 0.1 mm
north to (15.5, 14.5) (In2 run re-drawn at 45°). The C95.1 → C96.1 run widens from 0.4 to 0.8 mm. Open (Inference, an
IPC-2152 upper bound with no plane credit): the 0.4 mm C96.1 → C88.1 → U9.5 run rises about 55 °C at R10's 2.9 A design
current (about 36 °C at the 2.34 A fire-time current), over the 30 °C basis (stageD.md §4).

**D-R12-80 Post stage, legend table and the copper it needed (stage D).** `m3_design/r12_post.py` (r11_post's apply and
contract with R12's revision, KIID seed 12120, table and gate data, gated by check_r12 K-legends / K-fill; no open rows
allowed) applies the frozen table `m3_design/r12_legends.tsv`, drafted once by `m3_design/r12_legends_aid.py` (R11's
search helpers in R12's order: every contact legend first, then J122's note, the revision, SHOWN_REFS, words, J2's rows,
pin-1 dots, designators, Fab references; two aid runs write the same file). At the SH ports' 1.0 mm pitch two neighbours'
1.0 mm legends cannot share a face side by side (ink about 1.15 mm plus the 0.155 mm gap), so the contact legends
go on the bottom face (no parts there, D-R12-01), except J400's `1:SW` and `4:NC` on the top face. Each follows its
own column, alternately outward (ink ends at the pad centre line) and inward (ink starts 0.2 mm past it), both alternations tried; the aid's ownership margin is 0.05 mm (the gate's
0.01 mm; R11's aid 0.1 mm). All 30 SH contact legends, J1, J121 and every word, SWD row and SHOWN_REFS designator are
placed; 111 designators shown (35 on the bottom), 76 hidden on F.Fab. Copper moved to clear legend columns (each step
DRC 0, route_check r12 0 failing, check_r12 copper 0): J410 / J420 pin 2 reach PORT_4V8 through their 100 nF pad and its
via (outward vias at (21.6, 95.5) / (11.1, 95.5) removed); J122.5's GND via to (19.9, 7.0); J122's SERVO3_OUT hop 0.05 mm
west to x 22.05; J9's CAM_5V via 0.6 mm west to (33.0, 8.8); D402 1.0 mm west to (10.6, 7.8) so J400's footprint pin-1
mark stays nearest its own pad 1 (r10_post pin-1 ownership, 0.5 mm).

**D-R12-81 J122's harness note optional on R12 (owner acceptance pending).** `SERVO + FROM HARNESS` is 18.9 mm of ink at
1.0 mm; no free strip exists for it within the 6 mm legend reach of J122 on either face with J122 the nearest subject
(Measured by a probe on the drafted board: 0 free positions at 0° or 90°, F or B). check_r12 K-legends waives exactly

**D-R12-83 PORT_4V8 setpoint: R401 becomes a 0.1 % 25 ppm/K part, R404 5.1k (O-R12-16, O-R12-26; branch `r12-port-fix`).**
The port devices' supply limits: HGLRC M100 Mini (GPS) "Input: 3.3V-5V" (hglrc.com product page, read 2026-11-05: the
5.0 V top, Datasheet-class maker page); MicoAir LR900-F (radio) "Supply Voltage: 5V DC (from USB or JST-GH)" with no
range (micoair.com/radio_telemetry_lr900f, read 2026-11-05), so its 4.5 V floor and 5.0 V top stay the project
records' figures, an **Inference** until bench-confirmed; AUX up to 100 mA at the same rail and limits. The floor applies
at the port pins under the 0.561 A always-on budget (GPS 0.15 + radio 0.30 + AUX 0.10 + 0.011 A), the top at no load.
Why the old divider could not be fixed by copper or by a 1 % pair (Calculation / Inference: independent drift, TI SLVSD90B VFB 0.749-0.787 V, IVFB
±0.1 µA, each resistor at its worst end, drift = tolerance + 100 ppm/K × 67 K): the drift-bound window of any 1 %
pair spans (1.0167 / 0.9833)² = 1.0691 in its ratio term, while 4.5-5.0 V allows 1.0689 even with zero copper drop
and zero FB leakage, so no setpoint and no feed width meets both ends at the drift corner (D-R12-21 found the same:
0.516 V needed against 0.5 V). One precision resistor is the cheapest fix: R401 (bottom) → YAGEO RT0402BRD0710KL
10k ±0.1 % ±25 ppm/K (C190095, JLC Extended $0.0254, Economic-flagged: componentProductType 0 as every Economic line;
1 621 115 in stock, Live 2026-11-05); JLC's Basic library has no 0.1 % or 0.5 % resistor (Live search). Top: R400 47k +
R404 **5.1k** (C25905, Basic, already on the BOM: R7 / R8 / …) = 52.1k. Window (Calculation / Inference: drift):
**4.603-4.938 V initial, 4.571-4.972 V drift** (was 4.539-4.943 / 4.489-4.999), nominal 4.769 V (was 4.739 V). At
the ports, with the unchanged east-edge copper (DC solves L400 → J410 / J420 / J440 73.6 / 74.8 / 69.6 mΩ, an upper
bound on the drop): J420 ≥ **4.561 V initial / 4.529 V drift** (+61 / +29 mV over 4.5 V), J410 4.562 / 4.529 V, J440
4.564 / 4.532 V; no-load top 4.938 / 4.972 V (−62 / −28 mV under 5.0 V). Widening the feed was not needed and would not
by itself have helped (the drift window alone is too wide). PA4 PORT_SENSE becomes 3.11-3.38 V initial, 3.08-3.42 V
drift (VIH 2.39 V, ≤ 3.52 V, as first written; Calculation / Inference: drift. Corrected by D-R12-131: VIH 0.7 × the
3V3 maximum is 2.402 V initial / 2.425 V drift, the ceiling VDD + 0.3 at the 3V3 minimum 3.570 / 3.540 V; on the PFM
window since D-R12-139: 2.438 / 2.461 V and 3.554 / 3.524 V; PORT_SENSE is on PD10 since D-R12-93). The camera rail (U403, CAM_5V) and the "device TX ≤ 3.6 V" UART rule are
untouched. Cost: one Extended code, +$3.07 feeder per order and $0.03 of part (Live). Net names unchanged.

**D-R12-84 U9 feed and output copper (O-R12-25).** The 0.4 mm C96.1 → C88.1 → U9.5 run (4.13 mm, IPC-2152 upper
bound 55.2 °C at R10's 2.9 A, Inference) could not be widened where it was: route_check's short_segment (a segment shorter than
its own width) and neck_down rules, the 0.7 mm corridor between U9.4's and U9.6's pad corners and C96.1's corner left
no legal 0.8 mm path (0.7 mm is not an R12 width; 0.6 mm rises 34.6 °C at 2.9 A, IPC-2152 upper bound, Calculation / Inference). U9 moves **0.3 mm east to (17.3, 15.35)**
(courtyard 0.06 mm from C82's), which opens 1.01 mm between C96's and U9's courtyards, and C88 moves into it at
(15.3, 15.52), rotation 270, pad 1 on the y 15.1 bar line. The feed is now straight: C96.1 → **0.8 mm, 1.3 mm** → C88.1
→ 0.4 mm, 1.3 mm → U9.5 (its last 0.53 mm outside pads, the pin-entry class: U9.5's bar is 0.3 mm wide and the
corridor between U9.4 and U9.6 takes at most 0.5 mm). U9's connections were redrawn 0.3 mm east (EN, OVLO, FAULT_N
and its In2 run with the via at (15.8, 14.5), DVDT, GND via (16.9, 16.9), ILIM), C88.2's GND via is at (15.3, 16.75).
U9 OUT: the 0.3 mm bar track ends 0.4 mm past the bar at (18.9, 15.6) and continues 0.8 mm into C82.1; the two
C82.1 → In2 via stubs are 0.45 mm (each carries about half the current). IPC-2152 (no plane credit, upper bound;
Inference) at 2.9 A: every U9 IN segment is 0.8 mm (24.9 °C) except the 1.3 mm pin entry; U9 OUT has only pin-entry
lengths over 30 °C. C88 local path (Simulation input): 1.3 mm, 1.60 mΩ, 0.42 nH (R10 bounds 4 mm, 5 mΩ, 2.6 nH; was
3.03 mm, 3.72 mΩ, 0.99 nH). Hot-plug re-run: 0 / 2304, minimum −14.255 V, steepest fall 9.254 V/µs, every check
passes (Simulation / Inference). Legends: r12_legends_aid re-run on the routed board; only U9's designator, pin-1 dot
and Fab text and C88's Fab text moved.

**D-R12-85 Every part JLC-assembled (owner, 2026-11-05: "I want everything to be asssembled by jlc").** circuit.json's
owner_fit is empty and check_r12 S-4 now fails any part listed there (`tools/test_r12_check.py`
test_s4_owner_fit_refused); the export writes no owner BOM, and fab_manifest_r12 still requires the owner BOM to be
exactly r12_parts' (empty) owner set (`tools/test_r12_fab.py` test_no_owner_bom, test_owner_bom_refused,
test_out_of_bom_part_in_jlc_bom replace test_owner_part_in_jlc_bom).
* **LS120** (QMB-09B-03, C96256, Extended, Economic-flagged, 91 050 orderable, Live) moves to the JLC BOM / CPL as THT,
  as J121: BOM Assembly "THT (JLC THT assembly)", no paste, hand-soldered by JLC after reflow. CPL rotation 0: JLCEDA's
  "BUZ-TH_BD9.0-P4.00-D0.6-FD" matches our land with residual 0.0 mm (next best 2.83 mm), and JLCEDA prints its '+'
  beside pad 1, which is our pin 1 (+, net 3V3); the maker drawing marks the (+) lead (Huawha spec item 4, Datasheet).
  Pin 1 = (+) is no longer only an Inference.
* **C84**: MA25V470M8x10 (C46550466) has no maker reflow rating (its 4-page MA series sheet, JLC mirror, read
  2026-11-05, has none), so it is replaced by **Honor Elec HV1E477M0810PZ (C5246546)**: 470 µF ±20 % 25 V conductive
  polymer, D8 × 10.5 mm, ESR ≤ 30 mΩ, 2.8 A ripple, −55…+105 °C; lead-free reflow peak 255 °C for 5 s, 230-255 °C ≤ 30 s
  (Honor CDHV specification items 1, 9, 14, Datasheet). Economic's 255 ± 5 °C can be 5 °C over that peak: inside the
  owner's A-9 acceptance of 250 °C-rated parts (effect an Inference). Economic-flagged, Extended $0.28, 3964 orderable
  (Live 2026-11-05). Same 8.3 mm base and 3.1 mm terminal gap as MA, so R11's copper land is kept in a new footprint
  `M3_CAP_D8_SMD` with paste on both pads (`r12_parts.cap_d8`); its envelope model is 8.5 × 8.5 × 11.3 mm (L 10.5 plus
  R10's allowances, Inference; fit_check r12 OK). JLCEDA's land (4.0 × 1.6 mm at ±3.5 mm) matches within the two-pad
  tolerance (residual 0.25 mm, rotation 0). The value, tolerance and voltage are unchanged, so hold-up keeps its 300.8 µF
  allowance and figures, and the hot-plug wrapper gives it R10's doubled C84 envelope in the retained-output bound
  (`tools/r12_hotplug.py`: C84_PARTS; 5.585 V at 1419.8 µF, unchanged). Ripple need: hold-up bulk, far below 2.8 A.
* **Cost:** two more charged Extended codes (+$6.14 per order); the THT labour ($3.58 per order) was already charged for
  J121, and LS120 adds two THT joints ($0.0164 each).

**D-R12-86 PA4 sense divider ×10 and K-neighbours on R12 (review round 1, R12F1-TRI-01, Major).** U1.G3 (PA4,
PORT_SENSE) is diagonal to F2 (PC1, ACT_EN_MCU). D-R11-85 let G3 stay as LOGIC_SOURCE_ST because U11's ST was open drain
and low on the battery path; R12's PORT_SENSE is driven **high** exactly then. One F2-G3 bridge with PC1 high-Z (reset,
boot, MCU unpowered) gave Q122 (AO3400A, VGS(th) 0.65 / 1.05 / 1.45 V at 250 µA, AOS Rev 3.1 p. 2, Datasheet)
1.05 V nominal, **1.00-1.12 V** over PORT_4V8 and 1 % + drift (Calculation / Inference: drift), enough to enable RAW_ACT (Inference).
* **Options.** (a) Move PORT_SENSE to a ball not next to C3 / F2: G3 becomes NC, but the frozen copper
  (`r12_routes.tsv`) must be redrawn round U1's escape. (b) Keep the ball and raise the divider ten times: no copper
  change. **Chosen: (b)**, because no copper changes and the bridged gate stays far below the limit even with a second
  fault (R112 open).
* **Change.** R111 4.7k → **47k** (C25792) and R112 10k → **100k** (C25741). Both are Basic 1 % UNI-ROYAL parts already on
  the BOM, so there is no new code, and C25900 / C25744 stay for their other parts. The ratio is the same (100 / 147 =
  10 / 14.7). Thevenin 3.2 kΩ → **32.0 kΩ**.
* **Bridged gate.** The worst corner with PORT_4V8 at the drift top 4.972 V, every resistor at 1 % + 100 ppm/K × 67 K
  and R122 / R123 included: **≤ 0.200 V** (0.186 V nominal), and 0.205 V with R112 open (Calculation / Inference: drift).
* **PA4 still reads high.** TT_xx input leakage is ±250 nA (DS12110 Rev 8 Table 60 p. 139, Datasheet), which moves PA4
  by ≤ 8 mV. PA4 reads **3.103-3.388 V initial, 3.068-3.426 V drift** (was 3.111-3.380 / 3.076-3.418). That is above
  VIH 2.40 V and below VDD + 0.3 = 3.52 V (`r12_windows.json` sense; Calculation / Inference: drift). With PORT_4V8 off,
  R112 holds PA4 ≤ 0.025 V. The higher impedance only slows PA4's edge (32 kΩ × about 10 pF is about 0.3 µs,
  Inference), which firmware's PA4 deglitch covers.
* **Gate rule: check_r12 K-neighbours**, run in the placement, copper and full stages.
  * It runs R11's ball rule (`check_r11.gate_neighbours`) with an R12 table. R11's LOGIC_SOURCE_ST entry is dropped.
  * For each inert net it computes the one-bridge gate voltage from the board's own resistors: each value is read, its
    tolerance comes from the UNI-ROYAL MPN letter, and drift is added. The source bounds are `r12_windows.json`'s drift
    tops, the 8.4 V pack, and NRST's 30 kΩ minimum pull-up (DS12110 Table 65). Every chain's topology is verified, and
    any other part on a sense net fails.
  * **GATE_LIMIT_V 0.45 V** is the 0.65 V minimum less a 0.20 V allowance for the threshold's fall at +70 °C and for
    sub-250 µA conduction (Inference: AOS gives no VGS(th)-temperature curve).
* **Figures on board `f60d8239…` (Calculation / Inference: resistor drift and source bounds):**

| Neighbour of F2 | Bridged gate (worst corner) |
|---|---|
| PORT_SENSE | 0.1998 V |
| PYRO1_CONT (pack 8.4 V, bus already on; with the bus off its source is about 1.6 V) | 0.399 V |
| CAM_5V_SENSE | 0.273 V |
| NRST | 0.213 V (0.2165 V on the PFM 3V3 window, D-R12-139) |

  C3's neighbour net identities are unchanged: B2 / D2 3V3 (D-R11-23), C2 GND, the rest NC.
  The spacing is not unchanged: on `be81f40b…` the PE4 lane is 0.150 mm from U1.B2's 3V3 land
  (0.300 mm from its track), while ball-to-ball spacing remains 0.731 mm (Measured by round-2 triage).
  D-R12-104 records this retained exception in R12_VALIDATION row 25 for owner acceptance; a bridge
  may fire an enabled match (Inference).
* **Proof the rule works.** On the round-1 board `d6e4f7ee…`, K-neighbours fails with "Q122 gate up to 1.122 V"
  (Measured, `check_r12 --only K-neighbours`). `tools/test_r12_check.py` Round1Fixes covers three cases: setting the
  committed board back to 4.7k / 10k fails (> 1.0 V); a push-pull net on G2 fails; an unrecorded part on PORT_SENSE fails.
* **Corrections.** D-R12-05's values, D-R12-12's "D-R11-85 neighbours included" and D-R12-66A's "K-neighbours are
  unchanged" are corrected in place.
* **Not addressed here (observation for the next round, Inference).** PA4 is TT_a with 0 mA injection allowed (DS12110
  Table 22 p. 107). If PORT_4V8 rises before 3V3, PA4 sees up to 3.4 V with VDD low. The ×10 impedance cuts that
  current to ≤ about 0.1 mA, but does not make it zero.

**D-R12-87 Generator labels and the CAM_5V input condition (R12F1-TRI-03, -17, generator parts).**
* **PA4 sense label (`r12_budget.py` sense).** Now "Calculation (1 % resistors, rail windows above; DS12110 leakage);
  drift Inference".
* **New `cam_input` block in `r12_windows.json`.** The CAM_5V window (5.221-5.691 V initial) holds only while U403's VIN
  ≥ VO / 0.75 = **6.96-7.59 V initial / 6.88-7.68 V drift** (Calculation / Inference: drift). The source is TI SLVSD90B §7.3: maximum recommended duty 75 %.
* **Historical stage-A four-stall estimate.** U403 VIN was about 5.63 V using U9 RON 0.04 Ω (Inference);
  the corrected stage-A value is **5.619 V** with the 45 mΩ datasheet maximum, and the round-8 CAD-derived
  input on `418e5ea9…` was **5.574 V** (D-R12-156, Calculation / Inference). The following 92.7 %, 5.29 / 5.19 V estimates
  retain the original 5.63 V input as history; use D-R12-163 for current figures. Holding 5.221 V took 92.7 % duty lossless (Calculation / Inference: that supply estimate). U403's highest held output is D_max × (VIN −
  1 A × RDS(on)h 95 mΩ):
  * 5.29 V at tOFF(MIN) 220 ns typical;
  * **5.19 V** at 310 ns maximum, with fsw ≥ about 200 kHz (§5.5, §6.4.1).
* **Result.** CAM_5V may fall below its window low end to about 5.19 V. That is still above the camera's 5.0 V minimum
  before harness drop (Calculation / Inference: inductor DCR, copper and hot RDS(on) not included). The camera is not
  shown to fail.
* **Not edited here.** D-R12-08's margin text is the docs lane's.

**D-R12-88 DRU rule order (R12F1-TRI-11).** `r12_sch.dru()` now writes every unconditional rule first, then the scoped
(condition or layer) rules, each group in its listed order.
* **What was wrong.** KiCad applies the last matching rule. The unconditional 0.1 mm "R12 board floor: copper clearance"
  had followed "Tag-Connect contact to foreign copper" (0.508 mm) and the TP 1.0 mm rule, so both were dead.
* **New check.** check_r12 K-rules gains `dru_order`, which refuses an unconditional rule written after a scoped rule
  with the same constraint type. It fails the round-1 DRU and passes the regenerated one (test_dru_unconditional_after_scoped_refused).
* **DRC.** KiCad DRC on the rebuilt board: 0 violations, 0 unconnected, parity 0. The J2 0.508 mm rule is now live and holds.

3.8 mm below-seating tail (`R10_TERMINAL_TAIL_MM`, Inference: copper-pad AABB transversely). It checks them against the
bore and every other body, and reports them (`terminal_tails`).

**D-R12-91 Stage D: the 12 V VGS bound uses the largest gate ratio (R12F1-TRI-19, generator part).**
`r12_stageD.py` now uses 51k / 60.8k = 0.8388 (Rgs + 2 %, R121 − 2 %) for `q120_vgs_exceeds_12V_above_pack_V`, which is
12 / 0.8388 + 0.1 = **14.41 V** (was 14.60 V on the smallest ratio; Calculation / Inference: 0.1 V Q122 drop). It also records `q120_vgs_ratio_min_max`. The
drive-strength figures keep the smallest ratio. Test: test_vgs_12V_bound_uses_largest_ratio. The docs' "14.60 V" text is
the docs lane's.

**D-R12-92 JLC "difficult to process" flag on U5 and Y1: parts kept, order-day owner item.**
* **The flag.** JLC's Economic order page shows, for U5 (MS5607-02BA03, C97627) and Y1 (X322525MOB4SI, C9006): "The
  processing of this component is difficult. After placing an order, it will be reviewed. If PCBA advanced option is
  required, additional fees will be charged."
* **Cause (Measured, JLC endpoint `…/smtComponentShopCart/getComponentDetail?componentCode=`, 2026-11-06T00:58Z).**
  * Both parts carry `needAuditFlag: true`. Their specialComponentFee is 0, with no order instruction, so there is no
    stated fee and no stated reason.
  * The part pages show "Assembly Difficulty: High".
  * The controls at that read were not flagged: C1549 (C25 / C26 replaced by C1548 in D-R12-107), C25744, and YXC C9002 / C13738 of Y1's own series.
* **Why U5 stays.** Every brand-name MEMS barometer checked on JLC carries the flag: MS5611, MS5637, BMP388/58x, DPS368,
  ICP-10111/20100, LPS28DFW and others. A plausible reason is TE's 250 °C maximum soldering temperature and the no-clean,
  no-wash rule (MS5607 datasheet, Inference). The unflagged ones are Tokmas / WF clones on other lands with unverified
  resolution. None meets the swap condition, so U5 stays.
* **Why Y1 stays.**
  * YXC C5444544 (XL2EL89COI-111YLC-25M) is not flagged. It has the same YSX321SL sheet: 25 MHz, ESR ≤ 50 Ω,
    C0 ≤ 3 pF, same 3225 land and pinout. The sheet gives CL as "10pF, 20pF, or specify"; C9006's 12 pF is from its
    LCSC listing, and C5444544's CL is 12 pF only by Inference until its listing is read (D-R12-117). With 12 pF,
    C25 / C26 stay 15 pF and D-R12-107's gm_crit 0.726–1.244 mA/V would be unchanged (Calculation / Inference: stray, CL).
  * But it is Extended, which adds a $3.07 feeder fee per order (JLC Economic, jlc_cost).
  * U5's flag puts the order through JLC's review anyway, so the swap removes no review step. That would be cost grown
    without need, so Y1 stays on Basic C9006.
  * C5444544 is recorded as the drop-in if JLC's review charges more than that fee for Y1.
* **Owner item.** R12_VALIDATION row 21 records this as an order-day item.

**AVA re-layout (D-R12-93 … -99, branches `r12-relayout-int` / `r12-relayout-90`, 2026-11-06).** The stage C copper
was AVA-unclean (r12_plan's critique: 414 vias, 36 % diagonal copper, 11 vias on one servo net). The board was placed
again from the plan's floorplan and routed again by zone; the circuit, the part values and every AGENTS §2 network are
unchanged. The plan and its reasons are the layout scratch notes `CRITIQUE.md`, `FLOORPLAN.md`, `PINMAP.md` and
`ROUTING_PLAN.md`; the stage record is `stageE_relayout.md`. Labels as AGENTS §2.5.

**D-R12-93 U1 re-pins for the new floorplan (14 functions).** Applied in `m3_design/r12_sch.py` `R12_MOVES`;
`pin_table.tsv` and the schematic are regenerated from it.

| Net | Was | Now | Peripheral / note |
|---|---|---|---|
| SERVO1_MCU | PB7 A5 | PA15 A8 | AF1 TIM2_CH1. PA15 is JTDI at reset with its pull-up; against the 10 k servo pull-down the line reaches at most 0.864 V initial / 0.877 V drift (Calculation / Inference: drift, ±1.67 % per resistor; D-R12-116; 0.877 / 0.890 V on the PFM 3V3 window, D-R12-139). Firmware releases JTAG and keeps SWD |
| SERVO2_MCU | PA15 A8 | PA10 D10 | AF1 TIM1_CH3; USART1_RX pull-up against the 10 kΩ 1 % pull-down gives the same 0.864 V initial / 0.877 V drift bound (Calculation / Inference: drift; D-R12-116; 0.877 / 0.890 V on the PFM window, D-R12-139) |
| SERVO3_MCU | PA1 H2 | PA8 D9 | AF1 TIM1_CH1; keep MCO1 off |
| SERVO4_MCU | PA2 J2 | PA9 C9 | AF1 TIM1_CH2. The loader's USART1_TX may hold a steady high: a level, not pulses (the allowance R11 took for PA2) |
| I2C1_SCL | PA8 D9 | PB6 B5 | AF4 I2C1_SCL (was I2C3) |
| I2C1_SDA | PC9 E9 | PB7 A5 | AF4 I2C1_SDA |
| VBUS_SENSE | PA10 D10 | PC9 E9 | GPIO input |
| CAM_MCU_TX | PD5 B6 | PC10 B9 | AF8 UART4_TX (was USART2) |
| CAM_MCU_RX | PD6 C6 | PC11 B8 | AF8 UART4_RX. The loader's SPI3_MISO may drive it; the camera is unpowered in the loader and R100 1 kΩ 1 % feeds J9.5, the camera's TX output, with a resistor-only bound ≤ 3.47 mA initial / 3.52 mA drift (3.4641 V / 983.3 Ω; Calculation / Inference: drift; D-R12-116; 3.52 / 3.58 mA on the PFM window, D-R12-139) |
| HG_CS | PC11 B8 | PD2 B7 | GPIO, initialise high |
| CAM_PWR_EN | PC14 A1 | PC12 C8 | GPIO. A loader SPI3_MOSI input; R431 keeps the camera off |
| LED_YEL | PD2 B7 | PA1 H2 | GPIO |
| BAT_FAULT_N | PA5 H3 | PC13 A2 | GPIO input, EXTI13 |
| PORT_SENSE | PA4 G3 | PD10 H9 | GPIO input (FT_h) |

* **Freed (NC, analog):** A1 PC14, B6 PD5, C6 PD6, G3 PA4, H3 PA5, J2 PA2.
* **Evidence (Datasheet, read from the DS12110 Rev 8 PDF text).** Table 9 pp. 64–88 for every ball (PC13 A2 p. 64,
  PD10 H9 p. 75, PC9 E9 p. 79, PA8 D9 / PA9 C9 / PA10 D10 pp. 79–80, PA15 A8 / PC10 B9 / PC11 B8 / PC12 C8 p. 82, PD2 B7
  p. 83, PB6 B5 / PB7 A5 p. 86); Tables 10–12 pp. 89–92 for the AF numbers of ports A, B and C. The same text confirms
  D-R12-61A's PB10 AF9 QUADSPI_BK1_NCS and D-R12-63A's PE12–PE14 AF5 SPI4, so **O-R12-23's DS12110 half is closed**.
* **AN2606 not re-read.** st.com refused the download again (HTTP/2 error, then a timeout). The loader roles above come
  from `pin_table.tsv`'s AN2606 Rev 64 Table 119 column (read in R11): Inference. O-R12-23's AN2606 half stays open.
* **K-neighbours.** G3, PC1's neighbour, is now NC, so D-R12-86's PORT_SENSE inert entry is dropped (check_r12
  `NEIGHBOUR_INERT`, test updated). PC1's bridged-gate set is now PYRO1_CONT / CAM_5V_SENSE / NRST (0.399 / 0.273 /
  0.213 V, D-R12-86's figures; NRST 0.2165 V on the PFM window, D-R12-139; Calculation / Inference: resistor drift and source bounds).
* **Budget (`r12_budget.py`; corrected by D-R12-131, R12F5-TRI-16 / -17).** PD10 is FT_h, and DS12110 Rev 8 Table 60
  p. 139 gives FT_xx ±250 nA (±350 nA is FT_u only), the same as PA4's: PORT_SENSE 3.103–3.388 V initial,
  3.068–3.426 V drift, 0.025 V or less with PORT_4V8 off, against VIH 2.402 / 2.425 V (2.438 / 2.461 V on the PFM
  window, D-R12-139) (Calculation / Inference: drift; this bullet first gave ±350 nA and 3.100–3.392 / 3.064–3.430 / 0.036 V). PD10 is 5 V tolerant, so D-R12-86's PA4 injection observation no longer applies.
* **Firmware** (docs/M3_R12.md "Firmware"): TIM1 is an advanced timer, so set BDTR.MOE; PA15 is JTDI at reset; PORT_SENSE
  reads on PD10 ("PA4 high" becomes "PD10 high").

**D-R12-94 Placement.** `m3_design/r12_place.tsv` is the plan's `place_proposal.tsv`: 79 parts moved (FLOORPLAN.md has
each move and its reason). Main moves (90 mm board, Measured on `r12_place.tsv`): U1 at (23.0, 54.0); the sensor stack
on the roll axis, U5 (23.0, 28.4), U6 (23.0, 34.4), U4 (23.0, 41.6), all rotation 180; C84 beside U2 at (36.0, 31.2);
the 3V3 buck cell U2 / L1 forward of the RAW island; J2 at (32.8, 45.0), J3 at (41.4, 45.5); the port buck rotated
−90° as a block and moved to (42.6, 70.5) with its input capacitor C400 at the forward end of the RAW trunk; J430
(20.0, 69.0); LS120 (7.2, 67.4); LED1 / LED2 (8.0, 52.8) / (8.0, 55.0).

**D-R12-95 In2 planes.** **The 3V3A island is dropped**: 3V3A is local F copper in the sensor stack plus an In2 link to
H1 (owner-acceptance row; supervisor decision). The RAW_PROTECTED island is reshaped: final polygon (21.3, 0) (46, 0)
(46, 16.8) (32.2, 16.8) (32.2, 7.6) (26.15, 7.6) (26.15, 23.4) (19.8, 23.4) (19.8, 14.4) (21.3, 14.4). Its west piece
holds U9 / C82 / D9 / TP81, its east piece the camera-buck VIN and the trunk vias; they join at y 0–7.6 under J122 /
J9 (about 7 mm wide). A 3V3 pocket at x 26.15–32.2, y 7.6–16.8 lies under the CA and camera UART lanes. The
narrowest neck is about 3.2 mm. U9 → camera buck about 4 mΩ and to the trunk vias about 5–6 mΩ (Calculation /
Inference from a squares count at 0.5 oz, 1.13 mΩ/sq). The port buck's VIN is fed from the east piece by a 0.6 mm
B.Cu trunk along x 45.2 (57.6 mm, Measured).

**D-R12-96 Routing method: zones and merged fragments.** Five work zones (`m3_design/r12_aid/zones/zones.json`): W1 aft
power / pyro / camera / servo, W2 east power / USB / J2 / RAW trunk, W3 U1 escape and core, W4 sensor stack and the
CA corridor, W5 ports / port buck / buzzer / LEDs. Each zone has its own fragment `r12_routes_Wn.tsv`;
`zones/merge.py` merges them into `m3_design/r12_routes.tsv`, which `r12_route.py` replays as before (no search in the
build). The W1 / W2 border moved to y 17.0–17.6 so the moved 3V3 buck cell lies in W2. The stage C copper is kept as
`r12_aid/r12_routes_f1.tsv` (frozen source). Routed by Opus agents with GPT Astra helpers (Astra drafted W2's J2 SWD /
NRST rows and about 90 % of W4's first sensor draft; the rest drawn by hand or script).
* **Frozen copper reused verbatim** (S-3 / S-8 / S-9 = 0, Measured): the battery entry BAT_*, the bus switch and fire
  path (RAW_ACT, ACT_FET_S, ACT_GATE, ACT_DRV, ACT_EN, PYRO1_IGN / OUT / GATE / COLD / CONT_MID, ARM1_SENSE_MID), the
  camera-buck internals, SERVOn_OUT, PE4's PYRO1_GATE_N, PYRO1_CONT, HSE_IN / OUT (HSE_OUT 0.08 mm aft to y 52.42) and
  the port-cell internals.
* **Redrawn where a move forced it** (every S- and K-neighbours rule still 0): ACT_EN_MCU's B run (PC1's stub-tip via
  (18.6, 54.0) kept; R122 / R123 / C123 / Q122 untouched); the 3V3 buck cell and the J3 cluster.
* **Tools** (`m3_design/r12_aid/zones/`): `zone_check.py`, `zone_guard.py`, `export_geom.py`, `kref.py` (K-reference
  diagnostic), `draw.py`, `score.py`, `escape_W3.tsv` (U1 escape and hand-offs). None runs in the build.

**D-R12-97 Two rotations.** R107 rotated 180° (rotation 270: SENSE pad north beside R105.1, DIV south), so CAM_5V_DIV
no longer crosses the sense run (the old orientation forced a 2.59× detour, Measured). LED2 rotated 180° so its anode
faces R33 (Measured on the placed pads).

**D-R12-98 Integration nudges.**
* C13 (17.8, 50.6) → (17.9, 50.5).
* **C24 (NRST filter capacitor)** (16.6, 55) → (26.6, 45.18), rotation 90, beside J2 on the NRST run. NRST's In2 lane
  could not reach the west pocket under the frozen PYRO1_CONT / ACT_EN_MCU B runs (K-reference). The capacitor now sits

* BAT_FAULT_N runs on F along x 20.2 to a via at (19.3, 45.1), off the In2 lanes; the QSPI_IO2 lane at y 53.5 is clear of
  non-3V3 vias; the W4 north-block GND fence via (23.7, 44.55) partners the MISO / SCK / MOSI / HG_CS vias; for J9's
  `5:RXI` legend the CAM_MCU_RX via moved to (31.0, 9.03) and its GND partner to (31.0, 8.0).

**D-R12-99 Outline 46 × 90 mm (owner: "Try 90 mm"; supersedes D-R12-24's 100 mm).** *(Amended by D-R12-144, round 7: 46 × 88 mm, forward supports (3.5, 82.1) / (42.5, 82.1), the port row and port buck 1.85–2 mm further aft; the text below records the 90 mm step.)*
* **Density (Measured).** Courtyards 2117 mm² = 46 % of 46 × 100 and 51 % of 46 × 90.
* **Moved aft.** The port row J410 / J420 / J440 with R440 / R441 / R450 / R451 / R470 / R471 / C410 / C420 / C444 by
  10 mm as a block (the frozen port-cell copper moved with it); J430 and R460–R464 5.4 mm (J430 at (20.0, 69.0)); the
  port buck U400 / L400 / C400 / C402 / R400 / R401 / R404 1.5 mm; C406 (37.6, 77.825) rotation 90; C407 (16.75, 82.0)
  rotation 90; R111 / R112 (27.2, 80.6) / (28.2, 80.6); TP74 (31.0, 79.5); the 3V3 bulk C461 / C23 / C460 to (31.5, 70.0)
  / (31.5, 73.0) / (31.5, 75.5). The PORT_4V8 In2 island is y 80.7–89.5.
* **Supports.** Forward (3.5, 84.1) / (42.5, 84.1), 5.9 mm from the forward edge; aft unchanged (3.0, 5.9) / (43.0, 5.9).
* **Unchanged.** U1 and the W3 core, every W1 part and its copper.
* **Fit.** 90 mm is under the 120 mm rule (P1) and fit_check's 100 mm R12 ceiling. `docs/VEHICLE.md` gives no internal
  electronics-bay or sled length (its stack row still budgets 102 mm for R9), so R10's "wholly forward of joint + 47 mm"
  rule could not be checked: open item O-R12-28, not guessed.
* **Fallback.** The 100 mm board on `r12-relayout-int` at `4686580` (gated), board `ef3194c0…`: the same copper except
  the forward zone.

**Allowlist (route_check `ALLOWLIST_R12` / `ROUTE_FIGURES_R12`; D-R12-75A's SPI1_SCK and USB_DP entries removed as
stale).** Three critical-net entries, each Measured (owner-acceptance row):
* 3V3A `extra_vias`, 5 vias (72.31 mm against a 48.05 mm MST): the In2 link from the VDDA end (15.5, 60.6) to
  the R164 / U4 F.Cu island (19.65, 36.9) takes 2 vias. R164.2 supplies U4.5 (23.5, 40.64), C10, U4.8 and C31 on
  that island. One F→B via (23.5, 39.95) feeds C30 and U5 / C32 through 15.66 mm of B.Cu; each branch returns to
  F through its own via, (25.7, 42.75) and (25.8, 29.8), respectively.
  This matches lane C's corrected `ALLOWLIST_R12` reason (D-R12-101).
* SPI1_MISO `extra_vias`, 3 vias (22.36 mm against 17.25 mm): ball A6 is west of SCK / MOSI but U4.1 is on U4's east
  face, so MISO crosses both on B and back, plus its U6 branch.
* QSPI_CLK `detour` 1.63 (19.4 against 11.87 mm): the only F entry to U3.6 is from the pad row's inner end; C19 leaves
  one 0.7 mm gap west of U3, which IO3 uses; B under the BGA carries the IO2 and VCAP lanes.

### Round 2 fix pass (2026-11-06; D-R12-100 … -106)

Round 2 found **16 real errors (1 Major / 15 Minor)**, all fixed across lanes A–D; it is not a clean round.
PCB `be81f40be9d5b5ea60b3f1484324dab4dc999446dd75f493dd41e7f6982ddae8` is unchanged. These decisions correct
analysis, generator guidance, checks and records; they do not accept the board or close physical qualification.
Sources: round-2 triage, lanes A/B hand-off (`b52e9f7`), lane C (`0c621bd`), `stageD_budget.json`,
`stageD_analysis.md`, `stageE_relayout.md` and `pin_table.tsv`. Lane D checked text and arithmetic only;
KiCad, `tools/check.sh`, simulation and physical tests were not run by lane D.

**D-R12-100 Document corrections and evidence labels (R12F2-TRI-01, -02, -04, -09).**
* U9's 0.8 mm feed rise, 24.9 °C at 2.9 A, is an IPC-2152 upper bound (Calculation / Inference), now explicit in
  R12_VALIDATION row 4. The label sweep also covers drift / tolerance-derived figures in D-R12-05, -06, -09, -21,
  -51, -83, -84, -86, -91, owner row A-8, O-R12-05 / -10 / -26, M3_R12's port-fix summary,
  R12_VALIDATION A-8 / 20 and chatter carry-over, stageD §7 and the handoff's inherited short-current allowance.
* U403 EN is PC12, not PC14 (D-R12-93, `pin_table.tsv`); the rail table now agrees.
* C24 is 19.54 mm of NRST copper from E1, including 15.68 mm on In2 (Measured by triage, barrels excluded).
  Corrected D-R12-98, O-R12-30 and R12_VALIDATION row 23; no placement change.
* PC11 / R100 reaches J9.5, the camera's TX output, not its RX input (`schematic.net.xml`).

**D-R12-101 Correct the 3V3A allowlist reason (R12F2-TRI-05).** Lane C corrected `ALLOWLIST_R12`; owner rows 1 / 22,
the stack-up summary and the D-R12-98 / -99 allowlist record now match. R164.2 supplies the common U4 F.Cu island;
the VDDA In2 link takes two vias, and the 15.66 mm B.Cu branches to C30 and U5 / C32 take three (Measured).
The measured 5 vias and 72.31 mm remain; no topology or allowance changes.

**D-R12-102 Correct current attribution on RAW (R12F2-TRI-06, -07).** Lane A starts U400 IN at VIN pin 3 only,
excluding EN pin 5's 0.25 mm stub. Its old 47.5 °C claim is withdrawn. Series copper is 50.37 mΩ, not 54.78 mΩ
(Calculation, upper bound: parallel C400 links summed in series); D5 IN is 12.37 mΩ. With D5's current included
in the shared 11.06 mΩ, the then-current In2 landing → U400 VIN drop was about 0.12 V at design inputs / 35 mV at the budget
(round 8 `418e5ea9…`: 0.121 V / 36.1 mV, 50.78 mΩ series, D-R12-156; round-9 `a02a6181…` (historical; current figures: D-R12-172 / stageE_relayout §4): 0.1475 V / 43.9 mV, D-R12-163; Calculation / Inference: currents)
(Calculation / Inference). On the historical `be81f40b…` model, the shared trunk carries U400 + D5: 3.348 A design / 1.018 A fire-time budget;
46.1 / 4.3 °C, or 34.6 °C at U9's 2.9 A (IPC-2152 upper bound, Calculation / Inference: load allocations,
80 % efficiency and D5 drop). The F link joins two In2-joined vias, so this treatment is conservative.
O-R12-31 withdraws the EN-stub issue and now asks the owner to accept the shared trunk or widen it later.

**D-R12-103 Gate drive referenced to ACT_FET_S (R12F2-TRI-08).** Lane A subtracts Q120's fire-current drop before
computing Q120 / Q121 VGS: 1.250 A × 73.5 mΩ = 91.9 mV; ACT_FET_S 5.629 V; VGS −4.576 V at four stalls,
0.076 V beyond the −4.5 V RDS(on) row (Calculation / Inference: Q120 hot factor and Q122's 0.1 V drop). Corrected by
D-R12-131 (R12F5-TRI-03): with the J1 → Q120 and Q120 → Q121 copper drops, Q120 −4.569 V and Q121 −4.560 V, margins
69 / 60 mV; shorted match 4.39 / 4.37 V, match and leads 4.33 / 4.32 V.
Fire current is unchanged. Bus-on-before-fire enable times 27.4 / 14.7 / 11.4 ms and the 14.41 V maximum-ratio
corner are unchanged (Calculation / Inference, same inputs). Shorted-match gate magnitude is 4.40 V, or 4.35 V
with the leads shorted too: below that RDS(on) row, so the fault-current / dissipation estimates assume an
RDS(on) lower than real (Inference); they are not qualified fault bounds.

**D-R12-104 Retain and record the PE4 exception (R12F2-TRI-12).** D-R11-23's B2 / D2 3V3-neighbour exception remains
in check_r12. Ball-to-ball spacing is 0.731 mm; on `be81f40b…` PE4's lane is 0.150 mm from U1.B2's land and
0.300 mm from its track (Measured by triage). A bridge can drive Q124 and may fire an enabled match (Inference).
R12_VALIDATION row 25 requires owner acceptance before order; row 17 still requires U1 X-ray. D-R12-86 now
separates unchanged net identities from the changed spacing. No copper change or owner acceptance is implied.
Round 5 (D-R12-126, -128): the exception covers 18 PE4-to-3V3 item pairs inside U1's courtyard (floors 0.150 …
1.031 mm, including B2's dog-bone via (19.8, 50.8) at 0.525 mm and D2's via (20.6, 53.2) at 0.839 mm), all listed in
row 25 and in `m3_design/r12_fire.py`.

**D-R12-105 Include X-ray in order totals (R12F2-TRI-13).** JLC's
[PCBA price page](https://jlcpcb.com/help/article/pcb-assembly-price), read 2026-11-06 in the lane A hand-off,
requires X-ray for BGA, QFN and other leadless packages: $1.64 per piece at 1–10, $0.82 at 11–50, for Economic
and Standard. U1 alone adds $3.28 / $8.20 at 2 / 5 boards (Calculation): the round-2 minimum all-in totals were
(Calculation / Inference) $176.75 / $282.99 ($88.38 / $56.60 each), with 2 boards $6.75 over the $170 target.
If U1 / U4 / U5 / U6 / U9 count, X-ray is $16.40 / $20.50–28.70; all-in $189.87 / $295.29–303.49
($94.94 / $59.06–60.70 each), above $170 / $295 (Calculation / Inference: qualifying count and tier application).
Possible D402 / Y1 inclusion gives seven parts per board, at most $22.96 / $57.40 per order at $1.64 each
(Inference). Confirm JLC's count and tier on the quote. The bare PCB is $25.10, which is also cost_study.md §2.1's Live 4L
46 × 90 ENIG 0.5 oz quote (2026-11-04), so the earlier "100 mm quote used for 90 mm" Inference is withdrawn (D-R12-133).
Historical totals retain their dated BOM prices and add the minimum fee separately; parts + feeder metrics
are unchanged. No cost-tool output is represented as including this separately calculated fee.

**D-R12-106 Generated guidance and check fixes (R12F2-TRI-03, -10, -11, -14, -15, -16).**
* Lane B's four servo modes now say 333 Hz (REQUIREMENTS F4); schematic and board content are unchanged.

  (3.4314 × 10.1 / 40.1), 0.877 V at the 3.4641 V drift maximum with the pull-down at its −1.67 % drift end

  (Both restated on the PFM 3V3 window by D-R12-139: 0.877 / 0.890 V and 3.52 / 3.58 mA.)

## Round 3 fix pass (2026-11-06, R12F3-TRI-01 … -14)

Round 3 found 14 real errors (2 Major / 12 Minor); all are fixed by D-R12-107 … -112. The round is not clean.
PCB `66323cf0e78768beb9934cae316490ce357ee6216851843619e62da47105ad2f` replaces `be81f40b…` (itself replaced by `db1f0999…` in round 4, D-R12-113):
only C25 / C26's Value, Datasheet, LCSC and Manufacturer Part properties changed. Copper, placement, legends
and zones are unchanged (Measured: lanes A–D hand-off at `5b1f006`, two builds with the same hash and eight changed
board property lines). CPL byte-identical; only the C25 / C26 BOM line changed. No physical test.

**D-R12-107 C25 / C26 18 → 15 pF (R12F3-TRI-05).** Amends D-R12-14. Use FH 0402CG150J500NT, C1548,
15 pF C0G ±5 %, Basic, Economic and Standard; stock 1,220,918, $0.0026 each (Live 2026-11-06T17:54–17:56Z,
`fab/cost_check_{2,5}.json` / `stock_check_{2,5}.json`). The C1548 datasheet is byte-identical to C1549's:
resistance to soldering heat 265 ± 5 °C (Datasheet, lanes A–D hand-off). Added cost $0.0016 per board (Calculation);
parts still round to $32.78 per board. Y1's YXC YSX321SL sheet (C9006) gives ESR ≤ 50 Ω, C0 ≤ 3 pF,
drive 10–200 µW and no C1 (Datasheet); its CL 12 pF is from the LCSC C9006 listing, the sheet giving "10pF, 20pF, or
specify" (D-R12-117). The corrected capacitor-tolerance calculation gives:

- Old 18 pF: gm_crit 0.906–1.5027 mA/V; the +5 % / 5 pF-stray corner exceeds DS12110 Rev 8 Table 141's
  1.5 mA/V startup maximum (Calculation / Inference: stray). The old pass claim is withdrawn.
- New 15 pF: CL 9.125 / 11.0 / 12.875 pF, gm_crit 0.726–1.244 mA/V, 17.1 % margin (Calculation / Inference:
  2–5 pF stray). Pulling −26 … +120 ppm for assumed C1 ≤ 10 fF and C0 0.5–3 pF; including tolerance,
  temperature and five-year ageing, ≤ ±165 ppm against USB FS ±2500 ppm (Inference: C1 / C0 and stray assumptions).

`r12_budget.py` reads the capacitor value and tolerance. `circuit.json`, the hot-plug discharge-part record,
`pads_r12.json` and `jlc_corrections_r12.json` were updated by lanes A–D. Hot-plug remains 0 / 2304 failing,
minimum −14.255 V, fall 9.254 V/µs, rise 5.402 V/µs, retained-output bound 5.585 V (Simulation / Inference).
Electrical SHA is `dfba47cd…`, inventory 1419.818 µF, inputs SHA `db6e6b21…`, model SHA `3a353ec5…` (hand-off).
O-R12-09 remains open for cold start-up, drive level and MCO frequency checks.

**D-R12-108 Q124 VOH allowance and PA6 branch drops (R12F3-TRI-06, -09, -10).**
`r12_budget.py` and `r12_stageD.py` subtract DS12110 Rev 8 Table 158's 0.4 V VOH allowance:
Q124 VGS ≥ (3.2401 − 0.4) × 9.9k / 10.1222k = 2.778 V in stage D (R126 / R128 1 % worst ends),
2.761 V in the stage A placeholder (both corrected to 2.777 V at the ±1.67 % drift ends by D-R12-131). Both clear AO3400A's 2.5 V RDS(on) row (Calculation / Inference: drift).
PE4's steady load is about 3.6 mA (≤ 3.9 mA) with R127 1k, within the 4 mA row (D-R12-114; this record first said
0.32 mA, which left out R127). On the PFM 3V3 window (D-R12-139): Q124 VGS ≥ 2.761 V, PE4's load ≤ 3.925 mA.

The PA6 calculation charges each RAW branch at min(its own resistance from U9.6, D5's): U403 10.81 mΩ,
U400 and D5 + R92 23.53 mΩ. RAW drop at D5 is 37.5 / 38.4 / 38.7 mV for healthy / match shorted /
match and leads shorted. Four-stall margins to the trip top, nominal ×11 / 1 % divider, are lower bounds:
healthy +0.174 / +0.083 V; match shorted +0.025 / −0.066 V; match and leads shorted −0.019 / −0.110 V
(Calculation / Inference: shared-resistance bound, loads, pack and diode drop). Healthy VLOGIC 5.124–5.324 V is
about 26 mV below the 100 mm board's (Calculation / Inference). O-R12-07 then used PA6 0.42–0.45 V (superseded by D-R12-164),
VLOGIC 4.62–4.95 V nominal / 4.537–5.041 V with 1 % resistors (historical round 3 comparison;
round 8 adds the 4.482–5.103 V drift window and supersedes these margins in D-R12-156). Log a trip during a fire; do not treat it as
battery loss. Lanes A–D report `test_r12_stageD` 11 / 11 and `tools/check.sh` exit 0, including hot-plug numerics.

**D-R12-109 R12 dummy-fire reject line (R12F3-TRI-07).** R10 rule 4's 0.2 V BAT_IN − RAW_ACT reject line
used AONR21357; R12 uses two AO3401A, each ≤ 60 mΩ at VGS −4.5 V, 25 °C (Datasheet, D-R12-10).
Fix the first-article test to a 2 Ω dummy plus 0.3 Ω total leads, the budget's 7.4 V / 0.15 Ω source,
no servo stalls and the budget converter loads. Corrected by D-R12-163 (R12F9-TRI-05):
the old 1.505875 A / 0.264 V figure used R134's high-resistance end and was not an upper bound.
At R134's drift low end **1.9666 Ω**, current is **1.526022 A**. Assigning all **28.434 mΩ** of fire copper
plus the two **73.5 mΩ** AO3401A FETs to BAT_IN → RAW_ACT gives a drop bound of **0.2677 V**
(Calculation / Inference: 70 °C hot factors, pack, converter loads, copper model and drift).
Reject **above 0.4 V**,
between that bound and R10 rule 4's 0.7–1.0 V body-diode case (Inference when carried to R12; bench qualification
required). This is a conditional first-article criterion, not a measured limit for arbitrary sources or loads.
M3_R12 "Firmware" and R12_VEHICLE_HANDOFF state the override; the per-flight closed-switch check uses a 2 Ω
dummy without firing. Cold-screen, powered-BUS and gate-voltage checks otherwise remain.

**D-R12-110 Preferred Extended feeder waiver (R12F3-TRI-08).** [JLC PCBA FAQ 7](https://jlcpcb.com/help/article/pcb-assembly-faqs),
read 2026-11-06, exempts Preferred Extended parts from the Economic feeder fee. `jlc_cost.py` now applies it;
Standard is unchanged. At that round, D120 / C37704 was the sole exempt code (D8 / D121 join it in D-R12-165): 21 charged codes × $3.07 = $64.47 per order.
O-R12-12 is closed. Seeded test: `test_economic_exempts_preferred_extended_codes_from_the_feeder_fee`.

Live cost 2026-11-06T17:55:07Z / 17:56:22Z and stock 17:54:30Z / 17:55:45Z, 2 / 5 boards:
58 codes, 22 Extended / 36 Basic, none short or thin. Parts + feeder $65.02 / $45.68 per board;
minimum U1-only X-ray all-in $173.69 / $279.93 ($86.84 / $55.99 each), $3.69 over the two-board $170 target.
Five X-ray parts (U1 / U4 / U5 / U6 / U9) give $186.81 / $292.23–300.43 ($93.40 / $58.45–60.09 each),
$16.81 over at two boards and straddling $295 at five (Calculation / Inference: qualifying X-ray count and tier; the
bare PCB is the Live 46 × 90 quote, D-R12-133). Confirm JLC's count and tier, including possible D402 / Y1 fees. D-R12-105
and stageD.md keep dated costs as history; current totals are in M3_R12 "Cost and stock" and stageE_relayout.md §5.

**D-R12-111 Fab content gate and outline export (R12F3-TRI-03, -04).**
`fab_manifest_r12.content_checks` now runs `check_r12.py --only S-4 --bom … --cpl …` first and binds
`check_r12.py` as a package input. `test_r12_check.FabContent` mutates the Q122 / Q123 / Q124 BOM row to Qty 0,
MPN AO3401A and footprint R0402; S-4 rejects it. The package is re-exported and re-bound. `export_geom.py` reads
Edge.Cuts rather than a constant: 46 × 90 mm. These tool fixes make no copper change (lanes A–D hand-off).

**D-R12-112 Labels and current records (R12F3-TRI-01, -02, -11, -12, -13, -14).**
FXLS90322's ±150 g maximum is an Inference from search summaries until FXLS9xxxx Rev 7 is read (O-R12-21),
not a range published by the NXP part page. D-R12-93's 0.399 / 0.273 / 0.213 V are Calculation / Inference
from D-R12-86's resistor drift and source bounds. O-R12-04 names SH friction mating; O-R12-02 removes the
already-read TI IC, SMAJ12CA and LED2 ratings. A-8 gives the 90 mm port floor 4.563 V, retaining 4.529 V as

round 3: 14 real errors). Lane E updates only its eight assigned records, preserving historical hashes and costs.
The additional label sweep marks the camera-sense drift range, stage A fire / short-fault hot-factor figures,

No figure from that label-only sweep is changed.

## Round 4 fix pass (2026-11-06, R12F4-TRI-01 … -08)

Round 4's ten lenses reviewed `66323cf0…` at `65e14de`; the triage found 8 real errors (0 Critical / 0 Major / 8 Minor),
none rejected (power-1 and safety-1 merged into TRI-02). All are fixed by D-R12-113 … -120. The round is not clean.
PCB `db1f09992fc257b85eeaa6c2f22c04455e17dadec1d4ac732a9f955305dd45ee` replaces `66323cf0…`: only R166's footprint
`descr` line changed. Copper, placement, fields, legends, zones and the netlist are unchanged; JLC BOM and CPL are
byte-identical, so the Live cost and stock of 2026-11-06T17:54–17:56Z carry over (Measured: lanes A–D hand-off at
`f76c684`, two `PYTHONHASHSEED=0` builds with the same hash). Stage D and the hot-plug model were re-run on it to bind
the hash: every hot-plug row is identical, 0 / 2304 required cases fail, minimum −14.255 V (Simulation / Inference);
electrical SHA `dfba47cd…` unchanged, inputs SHA `1064def3…` (was `db6e6b21…`). No physical test.

**D-R12-113 R166 footprint description names its part (R12F4-TRI-06).** R12 copies R11's `M3_R2512_UR` byte for byte,
and its `descr` named 25121WF330LT4E / C25468 (0.33 Ω); R166 is 25121WF360LT4E / C168925, 0.36 Ω 1 % 2512 (its Value,
LCSC and Manufacturer Part fields and BOM line). `r12_parts.py` now applies an R12-only text override
(`R11_TEXT_OVERRIDES`) to the pinned R11 source, so R12's `kilib` land says "25121WF360LT4E, LCSC C168925, 0.36 ohm 1 %;
R12 override of the R11 land text". Copper, courtyard and model are unchanged. The board hash changes for this text
alone (`db1f0999…`); Gerbers are identical except their creation date, and the fab package is re-exported and re-bound.
R10's `assembly_review/kilib` and R11's copies (sha1 `a802c51b…`) still name C25468; they are outside R12 and left
for those revisions.

**D-R12-114 PE4's load includes R127 (R12F4-TRI-02).** Amends D-R12-10 and D-R12-108. PYRO1_GATE_N is U1.C3 (PE4),
R126.1 and R127.1 (netlist), so PE4 sources R127 1k to GND as well as R126 220R + R128 10k. Its steady high load is
3.2401 / 1000 + 3.2401 / 10220 = **3.557 mA** at the 3V3 drift minimum (was 0.317 mA, which left out R127), and
**≤ 3.868 mA** at the drift maximum 3.4641 V with every resistor at its −1.67 % drift end: about 3.6 mA (≤ 3.9 mA),
within DS12110 Rev 8 Table 158's 4 mA VOH row (Calculation / Inference: drift). Q124 VGS ≥ 2.778 V (stage D) and
2.761 V (stage A) are unchanged by this load (both 2.777 V at the drift ends since D-R12-131). On the PFM 3V3 window
(D-R12-139): 3.539 mA at the drift minimum 3.2237 V, ≤ 3.925 mA at 3.5156 V, still inside the 4 mA row; Q124 ≥ 2.761 V. `r12_stageD.py` records `pe4_load_mA`, `pe4_load_max_mA` and the row check in
`stageD_budget.json`; `test_r12_stageD` seeds both figures.

**D-R12-115 The bus-on window includes the pad (R12F4-TRI-03).** Amends D-R12-10. The R12 procedure raises PC1 at the
on-rail closed-switch check (R12_VEHICLE_HANDOFF step 3), and the inherited rule leaves the bus on from the on-rail
check until switch-off. The |VGS| > 12 V case for Q120 / Q121 (pack above about 14.4 V) therefore applies from the
on-rail check until switch-off: Inference that no transient above 14.4 V occurs while the bus is on, pad or flight
(O-R12-10). `r12_budget.py`'s gate note in `r12_windows.json` says so; it said "only enabled in flight".

**D-R12-116 Drift bounds use the resistor drift (R12F4-TRI-04).** Amends D-R12-93 and D-R12-106. The project's drift
case is ±1.67 % per resistor (`r12_budget.py` `TOL['drift']`), and 3.4641 V is that case's 3V3 maximum. PA15's JTDI
pull-up / PA10's loader USART1_RX pull-up against the 10 kΩ servo pull-down: **0.877 V** drift
(3.4641 × 10.167 / 40.167; was 0.873 V with the 1 % end). PC11 into J9.5 through R100: **3.52 mA** drift
(3.4641 V / 983.3 Ω; was 3.50 mA). The initial figures 0.864 V and 3.47 mA stand (Calculation / Inference: drift).
On the PFM 3V3 window (D-R12-139) all four move: 0.877 / 0.890 V and 3.52 / 3.58 mA (initial / drift).
`r12_sch.py` carries the new text into `pin_table.tsv`.

**D-R12-117 Y1's CL source (R12F4-TRI-05).** Amends D-R12-92 and D-R12-107. YXC's YSX321SL sheet (LCSC's PDF for C9006,
the board's Datasheet URL) gives load capacitance as "10pF, 20pF, or specify"; CL 12 pF is from the LCSC C9006 listing
("Load Capacitance: 12pF"), as `r12_budget.py` and stageE_relayout.md already cite. The sheet remains the source for
ESR ≤ 50 Ω (16–31 MHz), C0 ≤ 3 pF and drive 10–200 µW. The 12 pF value and D-R12-107's gm_crit figures are unchanged.
C5444544's CL has not been read from its listing: 12 pF for it is an Inference until it is.

**D-R12-118 `circuit.json` pin notes follow D-R12-93 (R12F4-TRI-07).** The R111 / R112 notes named PORT_SENSE on PA4 and
gave the 47k reason as a PC1 (F2) bridge; U6's named HG_CS on PC11. They now name PD10 (U1.H9) and PD2 (U1.B7), with PA4
(G3, now NC) and PC11 as history; 47k / 100k stay, with D-R12-86's reason given as history (PD10 is not a PE4 / PC1
neighbour). Record text only: nets are unchanged and the text does not reach the schematic. `circuit.json` is a fab
manifest input, so the package was re-bound.

**D-R12-119 R12 fab and stage D self-tests are gated (R12F4-TRI-01).** `tools/test_r12_fab.py` still expected round 3's
two content checks and failed (1 of 15) while `check.sh` exited 0, because `check.sh` ran neither it nor
`tools/test_r12_stageD.py`. `test_r12_fab` now expects S-4 first, then `--verify-cpl` and `--paste`. `check.sh` runs
`test_r12_fab.py` under KiCad's Python (15 tests; its package tests use `R12_BOARD`, else the repository R12 board) and
`test_r12_stageD.py` under `python3 -I` (13 tests), and `test_check_shell.py`'s self-test list names both.

**D-R12-120 Work zones end at the outline (R12F4-TRI-08).** `r12_aid/zones/zones.json` W5 closed at y 100 on the
46 × 90 mm board, so `zone_guard.py` accepted an off-board fragment. W5 now closes at y 90, and `zone_guard.py` reports
any zone polygon corner and refuses any fragment point outside Edge.Cuts (four tests in `tools/test_zone_check.py`, which
`check.sh` does not run). Only the aid tools read `zones.json`; the build, the gate and the board are unaffected.

## AVA polish pass (2026-11-06, branch `r12-polish`; D-R12-121 … -124)

A signal-only routing polish judged against the AVA reference (the owner judges by a render). No battery, fire-path,
pyro gate, PE4, RAW_ACT, J121, power or placement copper changed: every frozen row is untouched and S-1 … S-9 stay OK
(Measured). PCB `68e6b65091acd4c0bed89cba40308bc70f403057e98b9daf6136bdb230bd9d4f` replaces `db1f0999…`; two
`PYTHONHASHSEED=0` builds give the same hash; DRC, unconnected and parity 0; check_r12 every rule OK (K-reference,
K-region, K-plane, K-neighbours included); route_check r12 0 failing, 3 allowlisted (entries and route figures
unchanged), 0 stale, 8 non-critical leftovers (was 11). The JLC BOM and CPL are byte-identical (Measured), so the Live
cost and stock of 2026-11-06T17:54–17:56Z carry over. Stage D and the hot-plug model were re-run on the new board:
`stageD_budget.json` differs only in the board hashes, the 3V3 DC solves (+0.0015 mΩ at most; U6 VCC still ≥ 3.253 /
3.223 V) and the order of two equidistant GND vias in the fire-return list; the fire loop, GND return, EN, hold-up,
PA6, rail and port figures are unchanged (file comparison; Calculation / Inference as before). Hot-plug: electrical SHA
`dfba47cd…` unchanged, inputs SHA `8cf8f9bd…` (was `1064def3…`), every row identical, 0 / 2304 required cases fail
(Simulation / Inference). Records: `stageE_relayout.md` §2 "AVA polish pass". No physical test.

**D-R12-121 Port UART B bundle at one pitch.** The four port UART lanes south of U1 run straight on B at x 24.3 / 24.8 /
25.29 / 25.8 (RADIO_TX / RADIO_RX / GPS_TX / GPS_RX). GPS_TX's via moved from (25.65, 58.8) to (25.29, 59.41), so
GPS_RX leaves its dog-bone (25.4, 57.2) by one 45° step to x 25.8 instead of the 0.8 mm east-and-back zig-zag round the
old via (two bends fewer). The lane is x 25.29, not 25.3: GPS_TX's F step (25.65, 59.05) → (25.29, 59.41) is then
0.509 mm long, clear of route_check's 0.5 mm tiny_jog limit, and the via's In2 antipad stays off GPS_RX's K-region
samples (at x 25.3 the run had 5 samples over no In2 copper; Measured). The RADIO pair's west run is at y 78.6 / 79.1
(was 78.6 / 79.3) with equal 0.4 mm chamfers; RADIO_RX's via (13, 79.3) → (13, 79.1). PORT_SENSE keeps x 26.45: its
centred dog-bone (26.2, 56.4) and VCAP2's B run at x 26.9 leave no slot at 26.3. GND partners are unchanged:
(25.225, 58.15) within 1.5 mm of all four U1-end vias, (25.95, 80.7) and (12.6, 78.0) at the port end.

**D-R12-122 NRST under J2 on B.** NRST reached J2.3 (east column, middle row) by a 12.0 mm F loop round J2's north
(y 41.6, x 30.8 … 35.0). Measured constraints: J2's TC2030 footprint keeps tracks off F over its pad field
(32.165–33.435 × 43.73–46.27), so nothing passes between the pads; the 0.99 mm NPTH guide holes at y 42.46 / 47.54
leave 0.38 mm to the pad rows; SWCLK and SWDIO hold the west side. NRST now leaves its F run at (30.4, 45.6) →
(30.8, 45.2) → via (30.8, 44.85), runs 3.4 mm on B along y 44.85 under the pads, and returns through via (34.2, 44.85)
to J2.3 (F stub ending at (33.65, 44.85), outside the keep-out). GND partners (30.25, 46.2) at 1.46 mm and
(34.2, 43.73) at 1.12 mm. NRST copper 35.37 → 28.20 mm, routed / MST 1.96 → 1.56, vias 2 → 4. NRST is not a
critical route_check net, so its informational `detour` leftover becomes an `extra_vias` leftover. C24 stays
(D-R12-124).

**D-R12-123 Leftover hygiene and two GND vias.** CC1's via (38.05, 46.95) → (38.05, 46.75), on the pad's line (straight
F entry; B (38.05, 46.75) → (36.65, 46.75) → (35.5, 47.9)). LED_YEL's B run is at y 55.0 (was 55.2) with its via at
(13.5, 55.0), so R33 is entered straight. I2C1_SDA's 0.92 mm F link from via (19.8, 42.7) is 0.15 mm, like the run it
joins (was 0.10 mm: a neck in open copper). That removes three of the 11 leftovers (tiny_jog CC1, tiny_jog LED_YEL,
neck_down I2C1_SDA). The free GND vias (19.6, 65.0) and (21.6, 59.6) are removed: every signal via within 1.5 mm of
either keeps another GND via within 1.5 mm ((18.445, 65.0) for SD_CS at 0.85 mm and SD_MOSI at 1.405 mm (corrected from 1.43 mm, D-R12-133); (20.3, 59.18)
for SD_CS at 1.05 mm), and route_check's critical_via_no_gnd and nognd_audit stay 0 (Measured). They offset NRST's two
new vias.

**D-R12-124 Targets not reached, with the measured reason (O-R12-29, -30).** Metrics `db1f0999…` → `68e6b650…`
(Measured, score.py and a scratch track / via count): vias 318 → 318 (GND 149 → 147, signal 100 → 102); diagonal share
17.06 → 16.95 %; In2 track 137.4 → 137.4 mm; non-plane track 1947.6 → 1940.1 mm; tracks 1061 → 1053; worst routed /
MST NRST 1.96 → QSPI_CLK 1.63 (then USB_DM 1.57, QSPI_IO0 1.57, NRST 1.56, CC2 1.54); most vias on one signal net 4
(VLOGIC_SENSE, now also NRST).
* **C24 stays beside J2.** On F between Y1 and U1's west column the NRST stub (y 53.45) has CAM_5V_SENSE's F run 0.25 mm
  north (y 53.2), the NRST / CAM_5V_SENSE pocket vias (16.85 / 17.6, 52.9), HSE_IN / HSE_OUT, PYRO1_GATE_N at x 18.7
  and the frozen PYRO1_CONT / ACT_EN_MCU vias (17.1, 54.3) / (18.6, 54.0) to the south: no 0402 site with its own GND
  via on the NRST copper within 3 mm of E1 (Measured on the fragment rows). Moving it to the ball would not shorten
  NRST (MST 18.07 → about 18.7 mm, Calculation).
* **QSPI_CLK / QSPI_IO0** unchanged: the four QSPI lanes leave U1 at y 54.8 / 55.6 / 56.8 / 57.6 with CLK outermost, so
  CLK cannot turn south before IO0 turns at x 32.6, and U3's pins 5 / 6 are reachable on F only from the pad row's
  inner end (D-R12-96's allowlist reason).
* **USB_DM / DP** unchanged: the J3 crossover (A6 / B6 DP, A7 / B7 DM) needs the DP B hop, and the west side of J3's
  pad column holds CC1, VBUS and the C28 / C8 cluster, so the pair turns at x 41.15 / 41.55 east of the pads. Pulling it
  0.45 mm west would save about 1 mm of 23–25 mm (Calculation); not done on the USB pair for that gain.
* **CC2** unchanged: R7 (CC1) is north of R8 (CC2) while J3's CC1 pad is south of CC2's, so CC2 goes round CC1's via
  (35.5, 47.9) on B. The fix is an R7 / R8 swap (placement and legends), not taken in a signal-only pass.
* **VLOGIC_SENSE (4 vias) and In2 length.** The sense nets run on In2 because B carries the servo / camera bundle at
  x 27.4–29.8 and In2 critical copper needs B GND under it (check_r12 K-reference's lower reference); C60 / C94 sit
  7–9 mm west of their balls behind the C14 … C22 decoupler row. Shortening them needs C60 / C94 in U1's west pocket,
  which has no room (above).
* **Vias ≤ 290, GND vias ≤ 140.** 118 of the 147 GND vias end a GND track (decoupler and pad returns); the 29 free
  ones are partners of critical layer changes or stitch the W2 power cells, and only the two above were redundant
  (Measured by the partner scan).
* **Diagonal share ≤ 15 %.** 101.5 mm of the 357.4 mm of diagonal copper is power copper, the frozen copper included;
  the 255.9 mm of signal diagonals are mostly short 45° steps that route_check's tiny_jog / local_bump rules and
  AGENTS §3's 45° bends keep (Measured lengths; that the rules forbid an orthogonal redraw is stageE_relayout.md §2's
  finding, not re-tested here).

## Round 5 fix pass (2026-11-06, R12F5-TRI-01 … -22; D-R12-125 … -133)

Round 5's ten Opus lenses and four Astra second-opinion lenses reviewed `68e6b650…` at `ad486db`; the triage found
22 real errors (1 Critical / 0 Major / 21 Minor, R12F5-TRI-01 … -22), none rejected. req-1 was raised to **Critical**:
R12's copper broke the carried R10 fire-net limits (rows 1–2, D-R26-02 / -04), and check_r12 had no rule for them, so
one bridge forming after PC1 is high could fire an enabled match (Measured by the triage on `68e6b650…`:
PYRO1_GATE–3V3 0.101 mm on In2, PYRO1_GATE–PYRO1_COLD 0.665 mm, PYRO1_GATE_N–3V3 0.480 / 0.525 / 0.534 mm (C13.1, B2's
via (19.8, 50.8), C13's via) beyond row 25's recorded pairs, PYRO1_OUT–3V3 0.130 mm, PYRO1_OUT–GND 0.101 mm at J121.1, PYRO1_GATE_N–PYRO1_OUT 0.980 mm). All 22 are
fixed by D-R12-125 … -133; round 5 is not clean.

- **Lane P** (`cef5fda`): fire-net copper, the S-10 gate, U6's land, GPS_TX and gate text: board `14b82d39…`.
- **Lanes A and C** (`8b730b9`): the stage A / D generators, re-bound stage D and hot-plug, and the zones.
- **Independent verifier** (on `cef5fda`): "sound with issues". The copper fix holds and no undeclared fire-capable
  pair was below the carried limits, but four scope gaps let a planted fire-capable bridge pass both S-10 and DRC (the
  PYRO1_OUT list missed the low-side sinks ACT_DRV / BUZZ_OUT; a blanket "inert" list exempted QSPI_IO2 and HSE_OUT at
  any distance; the PE4 exception covered 18 pairs against row 25's 3; protected-net fills were not measured), plus one
  record gap (row 25). None was a violation on the board.
- **Lane P2** (`9f3c03d`): S-10 made default-deny with item-pair exceptions, protected-net fills measured, the pours
  pulled back from J121.2 and the PYRO1_OUT trunk moved 0.15 mm west: PCB
  `6e874d4e96ba3c6ab5d2a4163769c80aea6ba87002a4dd5c38eb85e7d93ca067` replaces `68e6b650…`.

Verified (Measured, lane P2 and this docs lane): two `PYTHONHASHSEED=0` builds give the same hash and a zone refill
leaves it unchanged; DRC, unconnected and parity 0; check_r12 every rule OK, S-10 included; route_check r12 0 failing,
3 allowlisted (no new entry); fab package re-exported and bound: the JLC BOM is byte-identical, so the Live cost and
stock of 2026-11-06T17:54–17:56Z carry over, and four CPL rows moved (C13, D120, R131, R173); stage D and the hot-plug
model re-run on `6e874d4e…` (every hot-plug row identical, 0 / 2304 required cases fail, minimum −14.255 V; Simulation /
Inference); `tools/check.sh` exit 0. Schematic and netlist unchanged. No AGENTS §2 rule changes: §2.3's rules gain
the BGA restatement of R10 rule 3 (D-R12-127) without changing their meaning, and every §2.4 gate network is unchanged
(R126 220 Ω / R128 10 kΩ / C126 220 nF / R127 1 kΩ; verifier). No physical test.

**D-R12-125 Fire-net copper meets the carried R10 limits (R12F5-TRI-01, copper).** Amends D-R12-94 (placement);
`r12_place.tsv` gives each move's basis.
- **PYRO1_GATE is F-only.** TP122 moves from (3.4, 50.4) to (14.8, 42.4) on F beside R126, fed from R126.2 by a 0.2 mm
  track (11.03, 44.2) → (13, 44.2) → (14.8, 42.4); both gate vias, the In2 run (0.101 mm from the In2 3V3 fill), the F
  stub and the GND partners (9.4, 45.6) / (4.4, 48.2) are removed. Stage D: 9.55 mm of F.Cu, no via, microstrip
  L ≤ 4.33 nH (was 7.20 mm F + 5.37 mm In2, 2 vias, 7.3 nH; Calculation).
- **D120 / R131 / PYRO1_OUT.** R131 turns to 270 (PYRO1_OUT pin north, PYRO1_COLD south); D120 moves from (9.6, 47.4)
  rotation 0 to (6.6, 49.2) rotation 270, cathode (COLD) north to R131.2, with its 3V3 anode on a new via (5.65, 51.2);
  the old 3V3 via (9.8, 48.3) and its track go. PYRO1_OUT runs north of D120: C128 → R131.1 at y 46.2, then y 46.7 →
  R173.1 (the dip keeps 1.13 mm from R127.1). Lane P2 moved the J121.1 → Q124 trunk 0.15 mm west to x 5.45, so the
  larger In1 void round J121.2 left K-reference's In1 reference intact.
- **R173** 0.1 mm forward to (12.6, 46.1); CONT_MID is redrawn (13.05, 46.1) → (12.15, 47.0), midway between R173.1 and
  R129.2 (0.131 mm from each, through ≥ 1 kΩ: not a fire path).
- **C13** turns to 0 at (17.4, 50.0) (0.5 mm west and aft, clear of Y1's courtyard), GND pad toward PE4's lane, GND via
  (17.82, 49.2), 3V3 via (15.05, 50.45) west of the VLOGIC_SENSE In2 lane; the vias (17.9, 51.52) / (17.9, 49.48) are
  removed. C13's 3V3 return to B2's via is now 4.76 mm through the In2 plane (was 1.95 mm; breaks no rule, verifier).
- **J121 pours.** The S-10 fill rules pull every pour ≥ 0.5 mm from J121.1 (GND_pour_B 0.101 → 0.5005 mm) and from
  J121.2 (GND_pour_B 0.100 → 0.5005 mm on B, GND_In1 0.251 → 0.5005 mm on In1).
- **Result (Measured on `6e874d4e…`, lane P2 / verifier, two independent tools agreeing to 0.5 µm).** PYRO1_GATE has no
  3V3, PYRO1_COLD or PYRO1_IGN copper within 1.6 mm (nearest non-GND copper: PE4's PYRO1_GATE_N at R126.1, 0.167 mm
  from the gate track (11, 44.1)–(10.5, 43.6), and R126's own lands 0.294 mm, the gate pair, not a path; then Q124.3, its
  own drain land, 1.044 mm; corrected by D-R12-143, R12F6-TRI-06: this line first named Q124.3 as the nearest).
  PYRO1_GATE_N–3V3 outside U1's courtyard 1.420 mm (C13.1); PYRO1_GATE_N–PYRO1_OUT / CONT_MID 1.080 mm (R173).
  PYRO1_OUT–3V3 / 3V3A: none within 1.6 mm on F; J121.1's barrel 1.0405 mm from the In2 3V3 fill (to the finished
  hole, as R10; the drilled wall is probably 0.025–0.075 mm nearer, Inference). PYRO1_OUT is ≥ 1.040 mm from every net
  but GND, PYRO1_CONT (five recorded pairs, minimum 0.464 mm) and the not-a-path pyro nets; its GND and PYRO1_CONT pairs
  are owner rows 31 and 32 (D-R12-128). One new near pair on a non-firing net:
  ACT_EN_MCU–3V3 1.075 mm on B (C13's new via; was 1.225 mm).
- Stage D moves only through the PYRO1_OUT trunk: fire loop 4.726699 → **4.726884 Ω**, four-stall current 1.250000 →
  **1.249952 A** (+11.1 % over 1.125 A), PYRO1_OUT copper 5.855 → 6.010 mΩ, fire copper 23.76 / 28.43 mΩ at 20 / 70 °C,
  idle current 1.505875 A, GND return Q124 → J1.2 2.585 → **2.607 mΩ** (< 5 mΩ allowance) (Calculation / Inference as
  before). C13's move changes the 3V3 solves: L1 → U6 12.047 mΩ, R14 → U6 13.162 mΩ; U6 VCC ≥ 3.2535 / 3.2232 V at
  U2's 1 A, margin 118.5 / 88.2 mV (Calculation / Inference: load currents). Hot-plug: electrical SHA `6b54be98…`
  (C13's pads moved), every row identical (Simulation / Inference).
- **Silkscreen.** Legends re-drafted by `r12_legends_aid.py` (the hand-tuned C15, J2, R150, R152, R463 rows kept):
  hidden designators 76 → **77** (C126, C127 and C25 now hidden; R127 and R33 now shown), 110 shown (44 on the bottom),
  50 legends (Measured).

**D-R12-126 check_r12 S-10: fire-net copper gaps, default-deny (R12F5-TRI-01, gate; the verifier's four gaps).** R10's
`test_r10_check.test_r26_*` limits were never ported to R12; S-10 ports and widens them. One definition,
`m3_design/r12_fire.py`, is read by check_r12 S-10 (measuring the board) and by `r12_sch.dru()` (writing the DRU's
clearance rules, so DRC holds the same limits and every pour pulls back on refill); `fab_manifest_r12` binds it.
- **Measured items:** tracks, flashed vias and pads, unflashed via and plated barrels and every zone fill on all four
  layers, protected-net fills included, both directions (the fill limit applies when either item is a fill).
- **Q124's gate nets (PYRO1_GATE, PYRO1_GATE_N): ≥ 1.0585 mm (DRU 1.06 mm) from every net** except GND (holds the gate
  off), the gate pair itself (a bridge only shorts R126) and **U1's NC balls only** (U1 pads on `unconnected-*`; other
  parts' NC pads, NC nets on tracks and no-net copper count). The blanket inert list is gone. (The code gave the NC-ball
  exemption to PYRO1_OUT and PYRO1_IGN too; since D-R12-136, R12F6-TRI-02, it is the gate nets' only.)
- **PYRO1_OUT: ≥ 1.040 mm from every net** (rails, VLOGIC, MCU-pin nets and the low-side sinks ACT_DRV / BUZZ_OUT
  included), except GND (≥ 0.25 mm copper, ≥ 0.5 mm fills), the gate nets (their own rule) and three not-a-path nets with
  their calculations: PYRO1_IGN (the match's own terminals), PYRO1_COLD (shorts R131 only; D120 reverse biased with the
  bus on, < 1 mA with it off) and PYRO1_CONT_MID (shorts R173 only; ≤ 1.4 mA, PC0 ≤ 1.38 V at 8.4 V) (Calculation).
  Round 6 corrected both: the COLD reason missed the commanded-fire state (R12F6-TRI-01: an unlimited 3V3 → D120 → Q124
  path, fixed by R136, D-R12-134 / -135), and PC0 is ≤ 1.42 V at the drift ends (R12F6-TRI-05, D-R12-139).
- **PYRO1_IGN (new): ≥ 0.25 mm copper, ≥ 0.5 mm fills** from every net but PYRO1_OUT and RAW_ACT (R134's other end).
  Not a match-fire path, but a bridge to GND with the bus on puts about 3.7 A and 27 W through R134, a 2 W part
  (Calculation).
- **Netlist guard:** S-10 fails if a not-a-path net is a FET low-side drain, an open drain, a supply or inductor net or
  a U1 pin in `schematic.net.xml`.
- **51 item-pair exceptions** (`EXCEPTIONS`, reasons in `WHY`), each with a floor at its measured gap rounded down to
  1 µm: a new or moved item is not excepted, a pair below its floor fails, and a table entry no longer on the board
  fails as stale. Classes: D-R11-23 3V3 (18 pairs, floor 0.150 mm; row 25), HSE_IN (7, 0.150 mm; no DC drive, about
  16 mV with PE4 high-Z, Calculation / Inference on the ~200 kΩ feedback resistor; 17 mV at the PFM window's 3.516 V,
  D-R12-139), HSE_OUT (8, 0.720 mm; gate ≤ 0.39 V
  while PE4 drives low if OSC_OUT sources ≤ 8 mA, Inference; conditional on rule 3, D-R12-127), QSPI_IO2 (8, 0.839 mm;
  fire-capable, 1.19–1.98 V on the gate, Inference; conditional on rule 3), BOOT0 (2, 0.625 mm; input with R5 10 kΩ to
  GND, Calculation), BAT_FAULT_N (2, 0.950 mm; gate ≤ 0.29 V, below V_GS(th) 0.65 V, Calculation; ≤ 0.302 V on the PFM window, R12F6-TRI-04,
  and ≤ 0.158 V since R95, D-R12-138 / -139), Q124 (1, 1.044 mm;
  the SOT-23's own gate and drain lands) and PYRO1_CONT (5, 0.464 mm; PC0's input clamp could pass bus current through
  the match, Inference; owner row 32). U1 NC balls within 1.0585 mm of PE4's lane are listed in S-10's evidence, not
  excepted: B1 0.150, B3 0.316, C4 / D3 0.400, A1 0.450, B4 / D4 0.731 mm (PC14 / PC15 / PE0 / PE1 / PE3 / PE5 / PB8),
  which firmware keeps analog (rule 3).
- **DRU rules:** "R12 JLC SMD pad to pad (different nets)" 0.15 mm (D-R12-129) first, then R11's scoped rules, then the
  six S-10 class rules and the 51 exception rules (KiCad applies the last matching rule). The GND rules skip the TP*
  and J2 pads so they cannot weaken those pads' 1.0 / 0.508 mm rules.
- **Tests** (`test_r12_check.FireGaps`, 15; `test_r12_check` 74 OK): the board passes; planted 0.1–0.2 mm items fail
  both S-10 and DRC for PYRO1_GATE–3V3, PYRO1_GATE_N–PYRO1_COLD / –PYRO1_IGN, PYRO1_OUT–3V3 / GND / GND fill /
  ACT_DRV / BUZZ_OUT / VLOGIC, an In2 3V3 track near J121.1's barrel, QSPI_IO2 and HSE_OUT on the gate track, a 3V3
  track 0.15 mm from PE4's lane inside U1, a PYRO1_OUT fill over a 3V3 track, PYRO1_IGN–GND copper and fill, another
  part's NC net, no-net copper and a U1 NC net on a track; a raised floor, a stale entry, a moved excepted via and a
  "not a path" ACT_DRV fail; the DRU's rules, order and floors are checked against S-10's.
- Lane P's first S-10 gave 25 findings on `68e6b650…` and 0 on `14b82d39…`; the final S-10 gives 0 on `6e874d4e…`.
- **KiCad quirk:** DRC reports at most one violation per track / net pair and stops at its error limit, so item-level
  DRC probing needs the full DRU loaded (lane P2).

**D-R12-127 R10 firmware rule 3 restated for R12 (R12F5-TRI-01; the QSPI_IO2 / HSE_OUT exceptions depend on it).**
R12 carries R10 rule 3 (R23-SAFETY-08 (1)) with the BGA's own neighbours, in `docs/M3_R12.md` "Firmware":
- Physics as R10: C126 220 nF, R126 220 Ω, R127 1 kΩ, R128 10 kΩ. A steady IO2-against-PE4 fight puts 1.19–1.98 V on
  Q124's gate and fires the match after about 0.55 ms (Inference). AO3400A V_GS(th) min 0.65 V (Datasheet).
- Ground refusal: before PC1 goes high, a QSPI quad read-verify of a known pattern; refuse on any mismatch.
- QSPI use while PC1 is high: PE2 (ball A3) stays in its QSPI alternate function, never a GPIO driven high; no
  memory-mapped or parked quad reads, no long all-ones writes at a low clock, flash output drive not at 100 %.
- PE4: write ODR low and select output mode before HSE is enabled; keep PE4 driven low except during a permitted fire.
  An HSE start failure (HSERDY timeout) or a CSS event on the ground refuses.
- Other neighbours: never make PC13 (ball A2, BAT_FAULT_N) an output; keep U1's NC balls A1, B1, B3, B4, C4, D3 and D4
  (PC14, PC15, PE3, PB8, PE1, PE5, PE0) analog.
- Cold screen: during part of the passive cold screen run sustained QSPI traffic and refuse if CONT or BUS moves out of
  band.
- First article, before first power and coating: X-ray U1 for bridges round ball C3 (replaces R10's "inspect U1 pins
  1–5", an LQFP step that does not fit the BGA); then, unpowered, meter U3.3 (QSPI_IO2) to R127.1 both ways: an intact
  board reads above 0.9 kΩ or open, a beep or under 100 Ω is a bridge: reject. 0.91 kΩ is R127 ∥ (R126 + R128)
  (Calculation); the reading through the clamps is an Inference.
The QSPI_IO2 and HSE_OUT exception classes of D-R12-126 are accepted only together with this rule (R12_VALIDATION
row 30).

**D-R12-128 Records for the fire-net limits (R12F5-TRI-01, records).** Amends D-R12-34 and D-R12-104.
- **R10 rows 1–2 re-listed with R12's measured pairs** (R12_VALIDATION "R10 / R11 rows that carry to R12"): row 2
  (gate nets ≥ 1.0585 mm) holds with only D-R12-126's exceptions; row 1 (PYRO1_OUT ≥ 1.040 mm from 3V3 / 3V3A) holds,

  clearance (Inference on KiCad's rule precedence, consistent with the measurement); the fills sit at 0.10 mm from
  foreign copper except where a DRU rule asks more (the S-10 fire rules, the S-8 keep-outs). A 0.2 mm In2
  pour-to-track rule was tried and not adopted: it made K-plane fail at a fill neck under 1.0 mm by C13's new 3V3 via.

**D-R12-129 U6 corner lands chamfered; SMD pad-to-pad 0.15 mm (R12F5-TRI-06).** Amends D-R12-51 (d). The eight
corner lands of `M3_FXLS_HLQFN16` keep NXP's 0.5 × 0.85 mm at 1.975 mm and gain a 0.143 mm 45° cut on the inner
corner, as NXP's Rev 6 Figure 104 ("(8X 0.143)" at the corner terminals, its extension lines starting at the chamfer
vertices; verifier) and Figures 110 / 111 (the same cut on the land and mask openings, about 0.12–0.15 mm by scale on a
drawing marked not to scale) show. Reading 0.143 mm as the terminal cut and the land cut's size are Inferences. JLCEDA
C5190712's 2.075 mm placement was not taken: it would leave part of a maximum-length terminal off the land. Measured:
corner-pair gap (U6.8 / 9, 12 / 13, 4 / 5 …) 0.1414 → **0.3437 mm**, mask web 0.041 → **0.244 mm**; land 0.4148 mm²,
covering the terminal with zero margin at the maximum terminal size and about 0.075 mm at nominal (Calculation, before
its 0.10 mm position tolerance). No different-net SMD pair on the board is now under 0.15 mm: U4's eight 0.5 mm-pitch
pairs are at 0.150 mm and J3 / U9 at 0.200 mm (corrected by D-R12-143, R12F6-TRI-07: this line first said "under
0.2 mm"). New DRU rule "R12 JLC SMD
pad to pad (different nets)": 0.15 mm (JLC's limit; REQUIREMENTS M2). `tools/paste_check.py` accepts a chamfered pad
with zero paste margin and refuses one with a margin (seeded tests).

**D-R12-131 Generator figures (R12F5-TRI-02, -03, -04, -05, -16, -17; lane A).** Amends D-R12-10, -83, -93, -103,
-108.
- **PORT_4V8 window is conditional (TRI-02).** U400 is TPS563208DDCR; SLVSD90B §7.3: maximum recommended duty 75 %,
  so the window holds only while U400 VIN ≥ VO / 0.75: **6.14–6.58 V initial, 6.09–6.63 V drift** (Calculation). At
  four stalls U400 VIN is 5.552 V and on a 6.0 V open pack 5.486 V (stage D with copper, ports at 0.561 A; the drop to
  VIN is an upper bound). Duty needed at the window top 0.898 / 0.904 (initial / drift) and 0.909 / 0.915; ceilings
  0.820 / 0.872 at 580 kHz (tOFF(MIN) 310 / 220 ns) and 0.938 / 0.956 at the ~200 kHz foldback below 7 V (§6.4.1).
  Highest held output at four stalls: 4.51 / 4.80 V at 580 kHz, 5.16 / 5.26 V with foldback (6.0 V pack: 4.46 / 4.74,
  5.10 / 5.19 V). So the window cannot be held at 580 kHz, and with foldback its 4.972 V top should be held: an
  Inference (U9 RON, pack model, converter allocations, foldback frequency, DCR and hot RDS(on)). No design change;
  bench item (R12_VALIDATION row 15). Stage A (`r12_windows` `port_input`, no copper): VIN 5.63 / 5.56 V.
- **Q120 / Q121 VGS per FET (TRI-03).** During a four-stall fire (BAT_IN 5.7209 V, 1.250 A): BAT_IN copper drop 9.1 mV
  (6.096 mΩ × 1.1965), Q120 drop 91.9 mV, Q120 source **5.6199 V**; ACT_FET_S copper drop 9.05 mV, Q121 source
  **5.6108 V**; **VGS Q120 −4.569 V, Q121 −4.560 V, margins 69 / 60 mV** beyond the −4.5 V row (replaces −4.576 V /
  76 mV and "ACT_FET_S 5.629 V"). During a fire at 6.0 / 7.4 / 8.4 V: Q120 −4.80 / −5.93 / −6.75 V, Q121 −4.79 / −5.92 /
  −6.73 V; bus on, no fire −4.88 / −6.04 / −6.87 V. Shorted match at four stalls |VGS| 4.39 / 4.37 V, match and leads
  shorted 4.33 / 4.32 V (Q120 / Q121). Calculation / Inference: the Q120 hot factor and the 0.1 V Q122 drop.
- **D-R12-10's formula withdrawn (TRI-04).** "VGS = −0.833 × V_BAT: −4.77 V … −7.0 V at 8.4 V … ≥ 4.5 V down to a 5.4 V
  pack" is replaced by the figures above; the stage A placeholder (`r12_windows`, no copper) gives −4.577 V at four
  stalls, −6.87 V at 8.4 V bus on, −10.485 V at 12.6 V and −16.609 V at the 19.9 V clamp (largest ratio); the −4.5 V row
  holds down to BAT_IN **5.54 V with the bus on** (4.5 / 0.8277 + 0.1) and **5.63 V during a 1.25 A fire**
  (Calculation / Inference); ±12 V only above 14.41 V, unchanged.
- **Q124 VGS ≥ 2.777 V (TRI-05):** (3.2401 − 0.4) × 9833 / (9833 + 223.67) with R126 / R128 at the drift ends
  (±1.67 %, `TOL['drift']`); the stage A placeholder is now also 2.777 V (2.761 V took a stale 3.222 V and nominal
  resistors). Calculation / Inference: drift.
- **PD10 leakage ±250 nA (TRI-16):** PD10 is FT_h (DS12110 Rev 8 Table 9, H9); Table 60 gives ±250 nA for FT_xx and
  ±350 nA for FT_u only, so "replaces PA4's TT ±250 nA" was false. PORT_SENSE reads **3.103–3.388 V initial,
  3.068–3.426 V drift, ≤ 0.025 V off** (Calculation; drift Inference).
- **PORT_SENSE thresholds from the rail window (TRI-17):** VIH 0.7 × the 3V3 maximum = **2.402 V initial (3.4314 V) /
  2.425 V drift (3.4641 V)**; ceiling VDD + 0.3 at the 3V3 minimum 3.570 / 3.540 V (was a hard-coded "2.39 V at 3.43 V");
  the check still passes (Calculation). On the PFM window since D-R12-139: 2.438 / 2.461 V, ceiling 3.554 / 3.524 V.
- Stage D and the hot-plug model re-bound (lane A on `14b82d39…`, lane P2 on `6e874d4e…`).
