## Round 6 fix pass (2026-11-07, R12F6-TRI-01 … -17, verifier V-1 … V-5, PACK_FLT; D-R12-134 … -143)

Round 6's ten Opus lenses and four Astra second-opinion lenses reviewed `6e874d4e…` at `daa08ef`; the triage found
17 real errors (1 Critical / 1 Major / 15 Minor, R12F6-TRI-01 … -17), none rejected. astra-safety-1 is the
**Critical** one (TRI-01): during a commanded fire Q124 holds PYRO1_OUT near GND, so a bridge across R131's own lands
(0.294 mm, Measured) made 3V3 → D120 → bridge → Q124 → GND with no resistor, limited only by U2's 1.075–1.6 A output
limit (TI SLUSDW3A §7.5, Eq. 6): D120 (BAT54C, I_FSM 600 mA) overstressed and the logic browned out or reset during the
wanted fire (Calculation / Inference on V_F, the logic load and the reset sequence). The (PYRO1_OUT, PYRO1_COLD) "not a
path" reason covered only the bus-on-Q124-off and bus-off states. gate-1 was raised to **Major** (TRI-02): S-10 and the
DRU gave U1's NC-ball exemption to PYRO1_OUT and PYRO1_IGN as well as to the gate nets. All 17 are fixed by D-R12-134 …
-143. An independent verifier then reviewed lane P's fix ("sound with issues") and found V-1 … V-5; V-1 and V-2 (Major,
pre-existing) and the PACK_FLT follow-up changed the board again. Round 6 is not clean.

- **Lane P** (`ca42e37`): TRI-01 (R136, R131 5.1 kΩ, fire-state guard), TRI-02, TRI-04, -05, -09, -15: board
  `3f9579fa…`.
- **Verifier** (on `ca42e37`): the R136 fix and the NC-ball rescope hold (its own scan of 950 item pairs / 89 net pairs
  on all four layers matched S-10's 51 exceptions); V-1 RAW_ACT / ACT_FET_S, which nothing on the board limits, were not
  in S-10 (RAW_ACT–GND 0.792 mm at R132.1, ACT_FET_S–BAT_DAMP 0.772 mm at R120.1); V-2 RAW_ACT–ARM1_SENSE 0.546 mm
  (R132.1–R175.2) put the bus straight on PC5 (TT_a); V-3 a false mechanism in the (PYRO1_IGN, RAW_ACT) reason; V-4 the
  fire-state guard missed parts with unknown prefixes; V-5 a flaky S-10 DRC test (4 of 8 runs failed).
- **Lanes A and B** (`207fda7`): TRI-03 (3V3 window), the first R12 cold-screen bands, TRI-16, -17; no board change.
- **Lane P3** (`752073c`, merged with A / B as `5a670bb`): V-1 … V-5: board `6a1c99e0…`.
- **Lane P4** (`3da8840`): PACK_FLT (R95 and D95): PCB
  `f26dfef181327c27cbf6c71b1f89e8d0be83f80e665716e0be1b7e31efcd0df0` replaces `6e874d4e…`.

Verified (Measured, lanes P, P3, P4 and this records lane): `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 r12_build.sh` gives the same hash three
times (P4); DRC, unconnected, parity and ERC 0; `r12_sch_check` OK with its seeds; check_r12 every rule OK, S-10
included; route_check r12 0 failing, 3 allowlisted, 8 non-critical leftovers; fit_check, K-legends, K-fill OK; the fab
package re-exported and bound (JLC BOM 58 lines, 181 CPL rows); stage D and the hot-plug model re-run on `f26dfef1…`
(2304 / 2304 required cases pass, every row identical; Simulation / Inference); `test_r12_check` 89 tests (29 of them
`FireGaps`), `test_r12_stageD` 23 and `test_r12_fab` 15 pass; `tools/check.sh` exit 0 (lanes P, A / B, P3 twice, P4,
and again after this records lane). No AGENTS §2 rule changes: every §2.4 gate network is unchanged, the pyro bus is
still taken after Q120 / Q121, and R136 / D95 add series limits without changing any gate. No physical test.

**D-R12-134 Cold pull-up series resistor R136; R131 5.1 kΩ (R12F6-TRI-01, circuit and copper).** Amends D-R12-125
(D120 / R131).
- **Circuit.** New R136 5.1 kΩ 1 % 0402 (C25905, 0402WGF5101TCE, already on the BOM): pin 1 3V3, pin 2 the new net
  **PYRO1_COLD_PU** to D120.1 (A1). R131 10 kΩ → **5.1 kΩ** (C25905, same land). The chain is now 3V3 → R136 5.1k →
  PYRO1_COLD_PU → D120 (A1 → K) → PYRO1_COLD → R131 5.1k → PYRO1_OUT, 10.2 kΩ in all (was D120 → R131 10k, 10 kΩ).
  D120.2 (A2) stays NC.
- **Placement and copper.** R136 at F(4.1, 50.2), rotation 0, west of D120.1: a 0.2 mm track D120.1 (5.65, 50.2) →
  R136.2 (4.53, 50.2), and a 0.3 mm 3V3 track R136.1 (3.67, 50.2) → its own 3V3 via (2.65, 50.2) on the In2 3V3 plane;
  the old D120.1 3V3 track and via (5.65, 51.2) are removed. A site south of D120 put R136.2 under D120's pin-1 dot (DRC
  silk_over_copper), so R136 went west.
- **Gaps (Measured on `f26dfef1…`).** PYRO1_COLD_PU is ≥ 3.266 mm from every PYRO1_OUT item (D120.1 to R131.1); R136's
  3V3 pad ≥ 3.733 mm (R136.1 to the OUT track (5.2, 46.2)–(6.6, 46.2)); R136's own lands 0.294 mm.
- **Every bridge, in every state (Calculation / Inference (3V3 window, D-R12-139): 3V3 ≤ 3.516 V; resistors at the ±1.67 %
  drift ends; label corrected by D-R12-149).**
  R131's lands bridged in a commanded fire: 3V3 → R136 → D120 → bridge → Q124 passes ≤ 3.516 V / 5.015 kΩ ≈ 0.70 mA
  (D120 V_F taken as 0; `r12_fire.py` states ≤ 0.71 mA), against U2's 1.075–1.6 A before. R136's lands bridged: R131
  remains, the same bound. D120 bridged with the bus on (Q124 off): (8.4 − 3.224) V / 10.03 kΩ ≈ 0.52 mA back into 3V3.
  PYRO1_COLD_PU to PYRO1_OUT: ≤ 0.70 mA in a fire, ≤ 1.04 mA into 3V3 with the bus on. D120.2 (NC, an anode onto COLD)
  bridged to .1 or .3: harmless. The triage's sweep of the other not-a-path classes and own-land pairs with Q124
  conducting (R173 / R129, R130 / R178 / C127, C128 the disclosed row 31 pair, Q124.1–.3, R126 a C126 transient,
  R127 / R128 / C126 hold-offs, R134 / J121 the analysed shorted-igniter cases) found R131 the only unlimited path; it
  is now ≤ 0.71 mA.
- **Records.** check_r12 S-1 / S-2: R10's named supply tie (D120, PYRO1_COLD, 3V3) is replaced by (R136,
  PYRO1_COLD_PU, 3V3), and `ARMED_NET_PARTS` gains PYRO1_COLD_PU = {D120, R136}, so a D120 anode back on 3V3 fails S-2.
  `r12_sch_check`: contract rows R136.1 / .2 and D120.1; R131, R136 and D120 required; seeds "cold pull-up anode on 3V3"
  and "R136 removed". K-neighbours' note names the pull-up "R136 5.1k, D120 and R131 5.1k". The cold-screen bands move
  to 10.2 kΩ (D-R12-140).
- **BOM / CPL.** C25744 10 kΩ 21 → 20 (R131), C25905 5.1 kΩ 9 → 11 (R131, R136); still 58 lines; CPL +1 row (R136 at
  (4.1, −50.2), Top, 0). Legends: R136's designator on F at (2.83, 51.47); D120's on B under its part.
- **Owner note, inherited (not audited).** The unlimited 3V3 → D120 → R131 → PYRO1_OUT path in a commanded fire was
  inherited from earlier revisions: R4's FMEA H-1 (`docs/FMEA_REDUNDANCY.md`) added D120 for the bus-on case only, and
  the triage found the same D120 → R131 10k → PYRO1_OUT topology in R9's and R10's netlists (BAT54A). Those boards were
  not audited for it: the owner asked to focus on R12 only.

**D-R12-135 S-10: every not-a-path class holds in the fire state; the fire-state guard; part classes default-deny
(R12F6-TRI-01 gate, verifier V-4).** Amends D-R12-126.
- **Fault model.** A class is "not a path" only if it holds in every state: bus off; bus on, Q124 off; and a commanded
  fire, Q124 on, PYRO1_OUT near GND (about 0.05 V), so a bridge to PYRO1_OUT is a bridge to GND. Every NOT_A_PATH reason
  in `m3_design/r12_fire.py` now states all three: (PYRO1_OUT, PYRO1_COLD) as D-R12-134; (PYRO1_OUT, PYRO1_CONT_MID):
  bus on, PC0 ≤ 1.42 V and ≤ 1.41 mA (D-R12-139), in a fire PC0 reads near 0 V, bus off < 0.71 mA (Calculation /
  Inference (3V3 window, D-R12-139)); the gate–GND classes
  are hold-offs (PYRO1_GATE–GND ≤ 17 mA through R126; PYRO1_GATE_N–GND drives PE4 above its per-pin rating, Inference
  on damage; the fire fails, 3V3 is not collapsed); the gate pair is a C126 transient; (PYRO1_IGN, RAW_ACT) is V-3's
  corrected text (D-R12-137).
- **`fire_state_guard()`, run in S-10.** For every (PYRO1_OUT, f) not-a-path class it walks `schematic.net.xml` from f
  to the nearest source and fails if any source is reached through less than **FIRE_SERIES_MIN = 5 kΩ** (≤ 3.516 V /
  5.015 kΩ ≈ 0.70 mA, Calculation / Inference (3V3 window, D-R12-139); ≤ 1.7 mA from 8.4 V; under BAT54C I_F 200 mA and
  negligible on 3V3, Calculation), or if the cold
  pull-up diode's anode is on 3V3 or another source. Evidence on `f26dfef1…`: PYRO1_COLD reaches 3V3 through 5100 Ω,
  PYRO1_CONT_MID reaches PYRO1_CONT through 5100 Ω, D120.1 is on PYRO1_COLD_PU. It fails on the round 6 netlist.
- **PYRO1_COLD_PU is not classed out.** It would qualify (0.70 mA with Q124 on, ≤ 1.04 mA into 3V3 with the bus on), but
  it stays under default-deny at OUT_MIN 1.040 mm; the board keeps it 3.266 mm away.
- **Part classes, default-deny (V-4, `part_kind()`).** A two-pin capacitor carries no DC; a one-pin TP / H / MH / FID
  carries nothing; a two-pin R counts at its value only if the value parses; D / L / FB parts of any pin count and every
  other two-pin part (F, JP, LED, an unparsed R, any unknown prefix) count as 0 Ω either way; every other part is a
  source on every pin. The verifier's seven plants that passed the old guard (F1, LED9 or JP1 in place of or across
  R136, RN1 0R, LED9 from 3V3 to COLD, X1 on COLD_PU, IC1 on CONT_MID) now fail it. "5.1 k" with a space reads as
  5.1 Ω (conservative).
- **Tests (`test_r12_check.FireGaps`).** The committed chain and values; the fire-state evidence; every OUT class reason
  states the Q124-on case; D120.1 planted on 3V3 fails S-10 twice and S-2 once; R136 at 0 Ω or 1 kΩ fails; a planted
  (OUT, LED_RED_A) class passes the old guard but fails this one (220 Ω through R32); PYRO1_COLD_PU copper 0.2 mm from
  J121.1 fails S-10 and DRC; the V-4 classes and plants.

**D-R12-136 U1's NC-ball exemption is the gate nets' only (R12F6-TRI-02, Major).** Amends D-R12-126. The exemption's
reason (analog in reset, kept analog by firmware rule 3) covers raising a gate, not sinking PYRO1_OUT's current or
taking PYRO1_IGN's bus voltage. `pair_limit()` now applies it only when the protected net is PYRO1_GATE or
PYRO1_GATE_N, and `NOT_U1_NC` is removed from the DRU rules "S-10 PYRO1_OUT to every net" and "S-10 PYRO1_IGN to every
net" (the fill rule never had it); the DRU is regenerated. Against a U1 NC ball PYRO1_OUT now needs 1.04 mm and
PYRO1_IGN 0.25 mm (0.5 mm fills). Tests: a PYRO1_OUT track 0.38 mm from U1.A1 fails S-10 and DRC; the IGN plant is at
0.20 mm (deviation from the triage's 0.38 mm: IGN's own limit is 0.25 mm, so 0.38 mm is rightly allowed; the verifier
confirmed 0.3 mm passes and 0.2 mm fails). No board pair changed: no OUT or IGN item is within reach of a U1 NC ball
(S-10 evidence lists 21 NC-ball pairs, all PYRO1_GATE_N). KiCad reports at most one clearance violation per track per
footprint or net, so the DRC probe strips other nets' copper round U1.A1 in a subprocess (D-R12-142 made it
deterministic).

**D-R12-137 Pack-energy nets in S-10 (verifier V-1, V-2, V-3; copper).** Amends D-R12-126.
- **Which nets (`pack_nets()`, default-deny).** From the battery pad J1.1 through every part that can carry the pack's
  current unlimited: it does not cross a capacitor, a one-pin test point, a resistor of at least PACK_R_LIMIT (2 Ω,
  R134's value; PYRO1_IGN behind it has its own class), a MOSFET's insulated gate, GND, or the named limiter U9 (BAT_IN
  is its IN pin; OUT is behind its current limit and fault latch; *R12F7-TRI-01, D-R12-145: the limit bounds the current,
  not the voltage, so RAW_PROTECTED is an FMEA I-5 rail held from 3V3 / 3V3A at PACK_MIN*). A transistor conducts drain to source either way
  (channel or body diode). Result: **BAT_IN, ACT_FET_S** (through Q120's channel or body diode, live even with J400
  open) **and RAW_ACT**. An unclassified part on a pack net, or a derived set that differs from `r12_fire.PACK`, fails
  S-10. BAT_DAMP is not a pack net (0 V DC); it is foreign copper held at GND's limit (0.36 Ω to GND).
- **Limit `PACK_MIN = 1.040 mm` from every net, copper and fills alike** (R10 D-R26-02's PYRO1_OUT figure): pack
  voltage on a logic or MCU-pin net is the same rail-onto-logic, FMEA I-5 mechanism as PYRO1_OUT, and an unfused pack
  short to GND is no less severe (R10 recorded about 23 A, D-R21-05). One generated DRU rule per pack net, placed after
  the IGN rules so 1.04 mm wins over IGN's 0.25 mm; the gate nets keep their stricter rule. DRU item conditions now
  match coordinates within 0.5 µm (16.4 mm is stored as 16.399999 mm).
- **Classed out, each checked by `pack_guard()`** (the foreign net is no supply, MCU, open-drain or sink net and
  reaches every source, GND included, only through ≥ PACK_SERIES_MIN 5 kΩ, MOSFET gates insulated; ≤ 8.4 V / 5.015 kΩ =
  1.68 mA): the pack nets against each other (both sides are the pack; Q124 with R128 still holds the match); ACT_GATE
  (a hold-off: Q122 sinks ≤ 8.4 V / 9.83 kΩ = 0.86 mA through R121; evidence: ACT_DRV through 10 kΩ; *R12F7-TRI-04,
  D-R12-146: a hold-off only against BAT_IN / ACT_FET_S; a RAW_ACT–ACT_GATE bridge turns the bus on with PC1 low or J400
  open, the bypass classes' consequence*); RAW_ACT to
  ARM1_SENSE_MID (R132's other end: PC5 ≤ 8.4 × 1.017 / (5.015 + 1.017) = 1.42 V, ≤ 1.42 mA; evidence: ARM1_SENSE
  through 5.1 kΩ); RAW_ACT to PYRO1_IGN (IGN's class holds it).
- **Copper moved.** R132 to F(17.17, 32.2) rotation 0 (was F(16.6, 33.6) rotation 90), running east with RAW_ACT on pin
  1 beside R134.2, fed by a new track (15.4, 32.2)–(16.74, 32.2). R175 turned to 270° at (17.6, 33.6): ARM1_SENSE_MID
  north to R132.2 (a straight 0.2 mm link), ARM1_SENSE south, re-routed by a 45° diagonal down x 18.1. R120 0.55 mm aft
  to F(6.53, 26.95) (was F(6.4, 26.4)); ACT_FET_S re-routed over a 45° step and ACT_GATE (4.0, 26.3) → (4.4, 25.9) →
  (5.91, 25.9) → R120.2 → (7.5, 26.95) → Q121.1 (one route_check local bump gone). The 3V3 and GND pours pull back to
  1.04 mm from all pack copper, clearing BAT_IN to the In2 3V3 fill at J1.1's barrel (0.55 mm; fire-capable through
  I-5) and J1.1 to GND_pour_B (0.10 mm).
- **Result (Measured on `f26dfef1…`).** RAW_ACT and ACT_FET_S are ≥ 1.040 mm from every net they are not classed out
  from, and need no exception: RAW_ACT–ARM1_SENSE 1.313 mm (R132.1–R175.2, was 0.546 mm), ACT_FET_S–BAT_DAMP 1.207 mm
  (R120.1–R166.1, was 0.772 mm). BAT_IN keeps 117 measured exceptions, all in the pack-entry cell (J1, U9, its input
  capacitors and dividers), under six keys, each an owner row (R12_VALIDATION rows 34–39):

  | Key | Pairs | Least gap | Foreign nets | One bridge |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | PACK_GND | 55 | 0.130 mm (BAT_IN track to R167.2) | GND (47), BAT_DAMP (8) | an unfused pack short: BAT_IN collapses, the logic browns out once C84's hold-up is spent (in flight a reset, the tab to neutral, recovery on the Blue Jay); no bus, so not a fire; a pre-existing bridge shows at first pack connection (Calculation / Inference on pack ESR) |
  | PACK_EN | 3 | 0.200 mm (U9.5–U9.1, the package's own lands) | BAT_ENABLE | J400 can no longer turn the board off; up to 8.4 V on EN, above its 6.5 V absolute maximum (TI SLVSFC9C; Inference for this variant); the per-flight switch-off check sees it. Not a fire |
  | PACK_SW | 3 | 0.483 mm (R80) | BAT_SW_SOURCE | shorts R80: EN ≤ 3.34 V with J400 closed (Calculation), but J400's contact and harness lead then carry the pack with no limit, so a chafe to GND is a PACK_GND short |
  | PACK_FLT | 12 | 0.150 mm (BAT_IN track (15.3, 15.1)–(16.6, 15.1) to U9.4; U9's own lands U9.5–U9.4 0.200 mm; corrected by D-R12-151, R12F7-TRI-16) | U9_FLT | since D-R12-138 neither fire-capable nor logic-destroying; U9's FLT is over-driven (U9 damaged, open or short; Inference) |
  | PACK_OUT | 8 | 0.131 mm (U9.5 to the RAW_PROTECTED fan-out) | RAW_PROTECTED | U9 bypassed: the logic converters fed from the pack with no limit, OVLO or fault latch; no overload by itself. Not a fire |
  | PACK_U9 | 36 | 0.166 mm | BAT_OVLO, BAT_OVLO_B, BAT_DVDT, BAT_ILIM, U9's NC AUXOFF / ITIMER | U9 trips off (OVLO: the board goes dark) or is over-driven (Inference: the pins' absolute maxima not re-read). Not a fire |

  12 of P3's 120 pairs were a part's own lands; the rest need a re-layout of the pack-entry cell (respin item,
  O-R12-32). R10 has the same U9 IN / FLT / EN adjacencies. The exception table holds 168 entries (51 fire-net + 117
  pack); since D-R12-147 removed U9's in-pad debris, 165 (51 + 114: PACK_EN 2, PACK_U9 34).
- **V-3.** The (PYRO1_IGN, RAW_ACT) reason no longer cites U9's current limit: U9 is not in the bus path (the bus is
  taken from BAT_IN, U9's input), so nothing on the board limits a bypassed-R134 current; about 3.1 A at 8.4 V with a
  sound match (Calculation / Inference on the match and loop) and more into a match that shorts after firing (R10 about
  12 A, Inference). Not an unwanted fire (current flows only while PE4 commands Q124 on) and not a pack-energy pair.
- **Tests.** The pack nets derived and held, and their DRU rules; the verifier's pairs cleared with no RAW_ACT /
  ACT_FET_S exception; RAW_ACT plants (GND, ARM1_SENSE) fail S-10 and DRC, an ACT_FET_S plant (BAT_DAMP) fails S-10; the
  pack netlist guard fails on a 0R off RAW_ACT, a fuse off BAT_IN, an unclassified IC on BAT_IN, R134 at 1 Ω and
  classed-out ARM1_SENSE / LED_RED_A / ACT_DRV; the V-3 text.
- **Legends.** C120 and R119 designators now shown on B (hidden 77 → 75 at `6a1c99e0…`).

**D-R12-138 U9's FLT reaches PC13 only through R95 10 kΩ with D95 clamping PC13 (PACK_FLT, verifier V-1 follow-up;
circuit and copper).** Before, PC13 (U1.A2, BAT_FAULT_N) read U9.4 directly, and U9.4 sits 0.200 mm from U9.5 (BAT_IN)
in the TPS259470A RPW package: one bridge put the pack straight on PC13 and back-drove 3V3 (logic-destroying, and
fire-capable through I-5, Inference).
- **Why a resistor alone is not enough.** PC13 is an FT pin that takes no positive injection (DS12110 Rev 8 Table 120
  note 3; VIN max Min(VDD, …) + 4.0 V, Table 119, and only 4.0 V with the board off), so a series resistor into its
  high impedance would still leave it at the pack voltage; no resistor-only divider keeps PC13 under about 6 V at 19.9 V
  and still reaches VIH 0.7 VDD with FLT released (lane P4, Calculation).
- **Circuit.** New net **U9_FLT** on U9.4 and R89.2 (R89 stays 10 kΩ to 3V3, on U9's side). **R95** 10 kΩ (C25744, on
  the BOM) from U9_FLT (pad 1) to BAT_FAULT_N / PC13 (pad 2). **D95** BAT54C (C37704, on the BOM): A1 on BAT_FAULT_N,
  K on 3V3, A2 unused, as D120. pin_table's PC13 row: "U9 FLT through R95 10k; R89 on U9_FLT; D95 clamp"; firmware keeps
  PC13 an input with no pull.
- **Placement.** R95 at F(18.15, 22.89) rotation 270, in line on the B trunk at x 18.15, vias U9_FLT (18.15, 21.65)
  and BAT_FAULT_N (18.15, 24.1). D95 at F(20.77, 24.27) rotation 180, its own 3V3 via (21.77, 25.1) on the In2 3V3 plane.
  Every R95 / D95 item is about 1.3 mm or more from BAT_IN (lane P4; their pads ≥ 2.15 mm, Measured). Three GND stitching
  vias nudged off the BAT_IN feed: (11.5, 9.6) → (11.55, 9.6), (12.2, 8.1) → (12.12, 8.1), (15.3, 16.75) → (15.3, 16.8),
  clearing three PACK_GND pairs (R167.1 to its via; tracks (13.3, 9)–(14.1, 9.8) and (14, 15.1)–(15.3, 15.1)).
- **Calculation / Inference (3V3 window, D-R12-139) (resistors at the ±1.67 % drift ends, 3V3 3.224–3.516 V; label
  corrected by D-R12-149).** BAT_IN–U9_FLT bridged at 8.4 V: R89 and
  R95 + D95 each pass ≤ (8.4 − 3.224) / 9.833 kΩ = 0.53 mA into 3V3 (1.05 mA together); at the SMAJ12CA's 19.9 V clamp
  (a transient) 1.70 mA each, small beside the running MCU's supply current (Inference). PC13 ≤ 3V3 + V_F ≤ 3.516 +
  0.8 = 4.32 V (V_F ≤ 800 mV at 100 mA, LCSC C37704 listing; the Nexperia sheet could not be opened), with no current
  into the pin beyond its leakage; with the board off PC13 sits V_F above the unpowered rail, under the FT pin's +4.0 V.
  FLT low: R89 sinks 0.36 mA into U9 (R_FLT 12.3 Ω typical, TI SLVSFC9C §6.5); no DC in R95; PC13 = V(FLT) + R95 × (D95
  reverse leakage + pin leakage), and the leakage budget to VIL 0.3 VDD = 0.967 V is 94 µA (D95 ≤ 2 µA at 25 °C, LCSC;
  hot leakage an Inference, R12_VALIDATION row 15). FLT released: PC13 ≥ 3.19 V even with 1 µA of FLT and pin leakage.
- **Consequence now.** A BAT_IN–U9_FLT bridge is neither fire-capable nor logic-destroying; U9 itself is still
  over-driven (FLT above its 6.5 V absolute maximum, SLVSFC9C §6.1; with a fault asserted FLT's pull-down sinks the pack:
  U9 damaged, open or short, Inference). So the 12 PACK_FLT pairs stay measured exceptions (owner row 37), moved to
  U9_FLT in `EXCEPTION_NETS` and the DRU.
- **`flt_guard()`, run in S-10.** U9.4's net carries no MCU pin; it reaches every MCU-pin net and 3V3 only through
  ≥ PACK_SERIES_MIN (5 kΩ; today 10 kΩ each); every MCU signal net it reaches has a diode with its anode on that net and
  its cathode on 3V3. Evidence: U9_FLT → 3V3 and → BAT_FAULT_N 10 kΩ each, clamp D95. Seeds and tests: PC13 on U9_FLT,
  R95 removed / 0 Ω / 1 kΩ, R89 1 kΩ or on PC13's side, D95 removed / reversed / cathode on GND all fail.
- **BAT_FAULT_N gate exception (S-10).** With PE4 high-Z the gate is ≤ 3.516 V × 0.952 k / 20.75 k × 10 / 10.22 =
  **0.158 V**, plus D95's leakage × 0.908 kΩ × 10 / 10.22 (2 µA at 25 °C: 1.8 mV; 0.089 V even at 100 µA hot, Inference),
  below AO3400A V_GS(th) min 0.65 V (Calculation / Inference (3V3 window, D-R12-139); was 0.302 V after TRI-04,
  D-R12-139).
- **BOM / CPL / legends.** C37704 BAT54C 1 → 2 (D95, D120), C25744 10 kΩ 20 → 21 (R95): still 58 lines, no new code or
  feeder fee; CPL 179 → 181 rows (D95 (20.77, −24.27) Top 180, R95 (18.15, −22.89) Top 270). D95's designator on F at
  (23.15, 24.27); R95 and C123 hidden (C123's old spot is under R95 / D95): hidden 75 → **77**; TP81's designator to B
  (22.2, 21.4, rotation 0), U5's to F (24.61, 24.74, rotation 90) (corrected by D-R12-151, R12F7-TRI-10: the record said
  both rotation 90). A duplicated WHY block (the PACK_* texts defined twice) was removed.

**D-R12-139 3V3 window on the PFM band and every figure restated on it (R12F6-TRI-03, -04, -05).** Amends D-R12-06,
-51, -93, -108, -114, -116, -126, -131.

**D-R12-140 Cold-screen bands at 10.2 kΩ (new; R12F6-TRI-01 consequence, lane A; `r12_windows.json` `cold_screen`,
`r12_budget.cold_screen()`).** R10's passive-screen model with R12's netlist values, the first cold-screen band set on
record for R12 (R11's was 10 kΩ with 3V3 ±5 %). Pull-up 3V3 → R136 5.1k → D120 → R131 5.1k → PYRO1_OUT; PC0 reads
PYRO1_OUT through R173 + R129 (5.1k each) over R130 ∥ R178 (2k ∥ 2k); PC5 reads RAW_ACT through R132 + R175 over R133
∥ R180; a fitted match joins the two through R134 2 Ω. State: bus off, PE4 low; zero leakage, ADC offset and reference
error. Corners: the 3V3 window per resistor case, D120 V_F 0–0.40 V (Inference: BAT54C ≤ 240 mV at 0.1 mA and ≤ 320 mV
at 1 mA at 25 °C, about 0.26 V at 0.2 mA, plus about 2 mV/K over 67 K), match 1–2 Ω, leads 0–0.3 Ω, each resistor pair
at its tolerance end. Calculation on the netlist values; V_F, the match and leads are Inference.

| Case | PC0 initial (mV) | PC5 initial (mV) | PC0 drift (mV) | PC5 drift (mV) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Open (no match) | 130.84–165.86 | 0 | 127.81–169.59 | 0 |
| Healthy match | 88.06–113.03 | 88.03–112.99 | 85.66–116.04 | 85.63–116.01 |
| Shorted match | as healthy (edges ≤ 0.01 mV apart) | | | |
| J121 to GND | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| R131 (≡ R136) bridged, no match | 171.83–217.69 | 0 | 167.88–222.54 | 0 |
| R131 bridged, match fitted | 130.28–166.59 | 130.23–166.54 | 126.88–170.82 | 126.84–170.77 |
| R173 (≡ R129) bridged, no match | 171.83–217.69 | 0 | 167.88–222.54 | 0 |
| R173 bridged, match fitted | 127.57–163.11 | 69.13–89.22 | 124.25–167.24 | 67.12–91.77 |

Margins (initial / drift): open PC0 above healthy 17.81 / 11.77 mV; healthy PC5 above open 88.03 / 85.63 mV; R131
bridged with a match, PC0 and PC5 above healthy 17.25 / 10.84 mV and 17.24 / 10.83 mV; R173 bridged with a match, PC0
above healthy 14.54 / 8.21 mV; R131 or R173 bridged with no match against the open band +5.97 / **−1.71 mV** (they
overlap at the drift corners, but PC5 reads 0, so the screen refuses them as "open" anyway). Nominal (3.35 V, V_F 0.25
V): open PC0 144.86 mV, healthy PC0 98.11 / PC5 98.08 mV. **Firmware (M3_R12 "Firmware"):** band PC5 as well as PC0,
and refuse unless both read in the healthy band: with a match fitted and R131 (or R136) bridged, PC0 reads like a
healthy board with no match (126.88–170.82 against the open band's 127.81–169.59 mV at drift), and only PC5 tells them
apart (the verifier: the margin to a healthy fitted board shrinks from about 185 to 33 ADC counts against the 10 kΩ
design). A shorted match cannot be told from a healthy one at DC, as before (R134 limits it in a fire). The bands are
ratiometric to VDDA (`PC0_of_VDDA` / `PC5_of_VDDA` in the JSON).

## Round 7 fix pass (2026-11-07, R12F7-TRI-01 … -17; D-R12-144 … -151)

Round 7's eleven Opus lenses and four Astra second-opinion lenses reviewed `f26dfef1…` at `852dfda`; the triage found
17 real errors (0 Critical / 2 Major / 15 Minor, R12F7-TRI-01 … -17) and rejected three lens claims in part (the D402
half of astra-power-1, a copper move for the TP120 track, astra-layout-1's C23 move). The two Major ones: **TRI-01**,
FMEA I-5 on R12's own copper (pack- and port-voltage rails about 0.10 mm from the In2 3V3 fill, and no R12 record of
it), and **TRI-02**, figures resting on the inferred 3V3 window labelled "Calculation" only (AGENTS §2.5). The board
changed, so the next two rounds review the new hash. Round 7 is not clean.

- **Lane P** (`bc7af8b`): TRI-01 copper and rules, TRI-03 (46 × 88 mm), TRI-06, -07 (copper and route_check), TRI-08,
  and the `r12_fire.py` texts of TRI-02, -04 and -05: PCB
  **`82eddc69c72cb0687183e8c51cb01a9290cbf4ec71b765c81ea7a093d6035af6`** replaces `f26dfef1…`.

  `r12_budget.py`, stage D re-run on `82eddc69…`, and every record (TRI-01 … -17).

Verified (Measured, lane P and this lane): `PYTHONHASHSEED=0 r12_build.sh` twice with the same hash; DRC, unconnected,
parity and ERC 0; check_r12 every rule OK (S-10 with the I-5 classes, K-plane, K-region, K-reference, K-fill,
K-legends); route_check r12 0 failing, 3 allowlisted, the same 8 non-critical leftovers; fit_check OK; the fab package
re-exported and bound (58 BOM lines, 181 CPL rows), cost and stock Live; stage D re-run on a zone-filled copy with
`--source-board` (this lane) and the hot-plug model re-run (lane P: 2304 / 2304 required cases pass, minimum
−14.255 V, unchanged; Simulation / Inference); `test_r12_check` 94 (none skipped), `test_r12_stageD` 25 (two new:
the C87 range and the window labels), `test_r12_fab` 15, `test_route_check` 87; `tools/check.sh` exit 0 (lane P, and
again after this lane). No
AGENTS §2 rule changes: every gate network is unchanged and the pyro bus is still taken after Q120 / Q121. No physical
test.

**D-R12-145 FMEA I-5 rails in S-10 (R12F7-TRI-01, Major; amends D-R12-137).**
- **The error.** D-R12-137 set PACK_MIN 1.040 mm because pack voltage on a logic net is "the same rail-onto-logic, FMEA
  I-5 mechanism", but `pack_nets()` stopped at U9 because OUT "is behind its current limit and fault latch". U9's
  3.92–4.89 A limit bounds the current of a short, not the voltage: RAW_PROTECTED still carries the pack (up to OVLO)
  onto any net it is bridged to. On `f26dfef1…` the In2 RAW_PROTECTED fill sat about 0.10 mm from the 3V3 fill (68.7 mm
  of edge under 0.15 mm), three RAW_PROTECTED vias 0.1255 mm from it, the PORT_4V8 fill about 0.10 mm, and CAM_5V
  (R104.1) 0.212 mm from a 3V3 via (Measured by the triage). The carried R10 row 1 still described this route with
  R10's figures (0.300 mm, and the deleted PORT_5V3). **Mechanism (Inference, R10 FMEA I-5):** 3V3 over U1's 4.0 V
  absolute maximum may make the MCU drive PE4 (and PC1); R12 has no 3V3 clamp, and once the bus is on PE4 alone holds
  the match, so a bridge is fire-capable.
- **Rule (`r12_fire.py` `i5_rails()` / `i5_guard()`, default-deny over the netlist's supply nets).** Pack-voltage rails
  (I5_PACK: U9's OUT RAW_PROTECTED, every net a diode carries it to (VLOGIC through D5), and the switch and bootstrap
  nodes of a converter fed from one: BST, SW_NODE, CAM_BST, CAM_SW, PORT_BST, PORT_SW) are held **≥ PACK_MIN
  1.040 mm** from 3V3 / 3V3A and every derived victim on every layer, fills included (D-R12-152). Port-voltage rails (I5_PORT: CAM_5V, PORT_4V8 and USB VBUS,
  the rails of `r12_windows.json` whose drift maximum is above 3V3's, ≤ 5.76 V) are held **≥ I5_PORT_MIN 0.300 mm**
  (R10 owner row 1's figure; 1.04 mm there was optional per the triage, and its trial broke K-region on the port UART
  B runs). Any other supply net must be 3V3 / 3V3A, a pack-energy net or a named rail below 3V3 (VCAP1 / VCAP2), or
  S-10 fails. Round 8 extended both classes to 71 victims; round 9 corrects the scope to **80**, every IC
  pin (D-R12-161). Five direction-guarded FT port exemptions, CAM_EN rating exemption, eight VLOGIC_SENSE
  and 21 IC-pin own-land/escape pairs remain (owner rows 27 / 40 / 46). Only I-5 port rules carry NOT_PROBE; CAM_EN is isolated from PC12 by R436 / D436 (D-R12-153). `pack_nets()`'s U9 reason now says the limit does not
  bound the voltage. DRU rules are generated per rail; seeded tests cover the derivation, the limits, the DRU text, F
  and In2 plants with DRC probes, and a netlist guard that fails a new diode-fed rail, a converter switch node or an
  unclassified supply.
- **Copper (lane P).** The RAW_PROTECTED In2 island reshaped (`r12_planes.tsv`): a 3V3 pocket x 26.15–32.6, y 7.0–16.8
  for the camera UART B lanes, and R89's 3V3 corner x < 22.3 below y 14.6; C8's 3V3 via (44.05, 17.925) → (42.6, 19.0)
  on a 0.6 mm stub; R89's 3V3 via (20.6, 13.55) → (20.6, 13.45) (a ≥ 1 mm neck, K-plane); R104 0.2 mm east to
  (28.8, 7.6) with its CAM_5V feed redrawn; the CAM_MCU_RX B lane x 31.6 → 31.0; R431.2's GND via (31.0, 11.8) →
  (31.75, 11.85).
- **Historical round 7 result (Measured on `82eddc69…`; the narrow rail-to-3V3-only scope).** No exception in that
  scope; D-R12-152 supersedes it with the full victim set, measured gaps and the PA6 exception group. Least gaps to
  3V3 / 3V3A: RAW_PROTECTED **1.0405 mm** (In2 fill to fill); VLOGIC and the SW / BST nodes > 1.1 mm; PORT_4V8
  **0.3003 mm** (In2 fill to fill); CAM_5V **0.3633 mm** (R104.1 to the 3V3 via (27.6, 8.2)); VBUS **0.9528 mm** (track
  (35.5, 44)–(35.5, 46) to the 3V3 via (34.2, 46.27)).
- **What stays (owner row 40).** U2's high-side switch short puts VLOGIC on 3V3 through the part itself, which copper
  cannot remove; a port-rail bridge at 0.300 mm pulls 3V3 toward that rail (PORT_4V8 ≤ 4.972 V, CAM_5V ≤ 5.757 V at
  the drift corner; U2 cannot sink, an Inference). Both are the R10 I-5 class (Inference on the MCU's behaviour). Respin option: a 3V3 clamp or crowbar. The carried R10 row 1's I-5 figures do not apply to
  R12; row 40 gives R12's, and FMEA_REDUNDANCY carries an R12 note under I-5.

**D-R12-146 RAW_ACT–ACT_GATE is bus-live, not a hold-off; Q124's own gate–drain lands (R12F7-TRI-04, -05; amends
D-R12-137, -128).**
- **TRI-04.** D-R12-137 classed ACT_GATE out of the pack rule as "a hold-off". For RAW_ACT that is false: with the bus
  off (PC1 low, Q122 off, or J400 open with ACT_FET_S live through Q120's body diode), a RAW_ACT–ACT_GATE bridge ties
  the gate node to RAW_ACT, which its 11.2 kΩ load holds low; the node sits near 0.18 × ACT_FET_S (R119 ∥ R120 50 kΩ
  against the load), so Q120 / Q121 see V_GS ≈ −0.82 × ACT_FET_S, far past V_GS(th) −0.5 … −1.3 V: both turn on, Q121
  settles diode-connected and the bus goes live (RAW_ACT ≈ BAT_IN − 1 V) with PC1 low or J400 open (Calculation; the
  settling point Inference). With the bus on it holds the bus on uncleanly; Q122 sinks ≤ 0.86 mA through R121. The
  consequence is the bypass classes' (BAT_IN / ACT_FET_S to RAW_ACT): a bus live without command, the carried R10 row 2
  class; not a fire alone (Q124 with R128 still holds the match). For BAT_IN / ACT_FET_S to ACT_GATE the hold-off
  reason stands (V_GS ≈ 0). `r12_fire.py`'s NOT_A_PATH text is corrected (lane P). No copper move: the board already
  has single bridges with the same consequence nearer, ACT_GATE's vias (6.8, 30.1) / (8.6, 31.0) 0.1255 mm from
  GND_pour_B and ACT_FET_S track (5.1, 30.4)–(6.3, 31.6) 0.200 mm from the RAW_ACT TP120 track; the RAW_ACT–ACT_GATE
  pair is that TP120 track (6, 32.3)–(12.2, 32.3) 0.300 mm from C120.2 (Measured on `82eddc69…`). All three are listed
  under the carried row 2 in R12_VALIDATION; the per-flight J121.2 < 0.1 V check and the cold screen see a bus live
  before the e-match goes in.
- **TRI-05.** Q124.1 (PYRO1_GATE) to Q124.3 (PYRO1_OUT), the AO3400A SOT-23's own lands, is **1.044 mm**, under
  GATE_MIN, and was excepted without an owner row. With the bus on and Q124 off a gate–drain bridge ties the gate to the
  bus-fed drain against R128 10 kΩ (and R126 220 Ω into PE4); Q124 runs diode-connected at V_GS ≈ 1.8–2 V and passes
  about 1.6–2 A through the match, above the J-Tek 1.125 A all-fire: **fire-capable** (Calculation / Inference on the
  AO3400A transfer curve). Unavoidable in the package (as C128's own lands, row 31): new owner row 41, and
  WHY['Q124'] states it (lane P). The exception counts by row are now named: of the 51 fire-net exceptions, rows 25
  (D-R11-23, 18), 30 (QSPI_IO2 8, HSE_OUT 8), 32 (PYRO1_CONT 5) and 41 (Q124, 1) hold the fire-capable 40; HSE_IN (7),
  BOOT0 (2) and BAT_FAULT_N (2) are not fire-capable and are explained under the carried row 2.

- **TRI-07.** PYRO1_GATE's run from R126.2 passed R128.1 at 0.030 mm without joining it and entered Q124.1 beside a
  second run (a sub-0.1 mm same-net slit). It is now one 45° track (11, 44.1)–(10.2, 43.3) ending in R128.1, and

- **route_check (gated RULE categories, `tools/test_route_check.py` seeded tests):** `pad_debris`, a segment of any
  length wholly inside a same-net pad whose ends meet no track or via; `pad_slit`, a track passing a same-net pad it
  does not join at under 0.1 mm (pad exits excluded; a multi-pad land such as U9's counts as one pad). On `f26dfef1…`
  they flag exactly the TRI-06 / -07 items; the R10, R11 and new R12 boards are clean.

**D-R12-148 check.sh hands its kicad-cli to the self-tests (R12F7-TRI-08).** `tools/check.sh` resolved kicad-cli from
`$KICAD_CLI`, PATH or either macOS app folder but exported only `KICAD_PY`, so `test_r12_fab.py` / `test_r12_check.py`
fell back to one hard-coded path and skipped on a PATH or `/Applications/KiCad.app` install. Now `check.sh` exports
`KICAD_CLI="$CLI"`; both tests look in KICAD_CLI, PATH, then both app folders; under `R12_BOARD_REQUIRED=1` with the
board present a missing kicad-cli fails (`require_kicad()`, `drc_s10`) instead of skipping; `CliResolution` tests and a
shell test (it fails with the export removed) cover it. `test_r12_check` now runs 94 tests with no skip.

## Round 8 fixes (2026-11-07; R12F8-TRI-01 … -19)

Round 8 reviewed `82eddc69…` and found **19 real errors: 0 Critical / 2 Major / 17 Minor**, all fixed or
recorded below. Copper lane P `33ce766` and analyses lane A `c5c695e` produce board
`418e5ea985402d00ba8008e9775e037321eddc48683754eba1939744d75f791e`, 46 × 88 mm. This round-8 board was subsequently reviewed in round 9; D-R12-167 supersedes the stop rule.

**D-R12-152 I-5 victim classes and probe precedence (TRI-01, -05; amends D-R12-145).**
**Amended by D-R12-161 (R12F9-TRI-01 … -03).** The round-8 implementation derived 71 victims from
3V3 / 3V3A and U1 pins, despite this record claiming every IC pin. Current S-10 derives **80 victims**
from every IC pin (`U<n>`) with the same < 5 kΩ walk; nine previously missed nets are now protected.
Pack rails keep 1.040 mm and port rails 0.300 mm, subject to the explicit exceptions in owner rows 27 / 46.
Port exemptions are CAM_MCU_RX, CAM_RX_WIRE, CAM_TX_WIRE, VBUS_SENSE and PORT_SENSE; CAM_MCU_TX is removed.
The guard checks pin direction and loader roles; CAM_TX_WIRE's ≥ 1 kΩ series path permits its TX mode.
`I5_RATED` contains CAM_EN only (U403.5 EN 19 V; RAW/CAM_SW 10.833 V, CAM_BST 16.833 V maxima).
CAM_FB has no rating exemption. NOT_PROBE applies **only to port rules**; pack rules keep 1.040 mm at J2.
TP local clearances also meet their net's floor: gate 1.06 mm, protected nets 1.040 mm, others 1.0 mm.
D-R12-158's round-8 record context is amended accordingly; full scope and consequences are D-R12-161 / owner row 46.

**D-R12-153 CAM_PWR_EN series protection and reset pull-down (TRI-01).**
PC12's CAM_PWR_EN now reaches U403.5 **CAM_EN through R436 5.1 kΩ (C25905)**; D436 BAT54C (C37704)
clamps the PC12 side to 3V3 (A1 CAM_PWR_EN, K 3V3, A2 unused). R436 is F(28.2, 26.6), rotation 90;
D436 F(29.15, 29.25), rotation 270. The 0.400 mm U403.5–U403.6 CAM_BST pair is on the isolated EN side.
R431 changes **100k → 47k (C25792)** on CAM_EN: in reset D436 leakage flows through R436 into R431 ∥ REN.
At 100k, 0.8 V is reached at about 8.2 µA; at 47k it stays below 0.8 V up to 16.7 µA (Calculation / Inference:
resistor drift, TI SLVSD90B §6.5 REN 600–2400 kΩ). BAT54C leakage is ≤ 2 µA at 25 °C (LCSC); the hot leakage
is an Inference and remains a bench item. Enable floor **(3.2237 − 0.4) × 42.91 / (42.91 + 5.185) = 2.52 V**,
above VENH 1.6 V (Calculation / Inference: 3V3 window, ±1.67 % drift; DS12110 Table 61 VOH ≥ VDD − 0.4 V).
An EN bridge at CAM_BST = 8.4 + 5 V passes at most about **2.0 mA** into D436 / 3V3 and holds PC12 ≤ **4.32 V**;
FT_h PC12 takes no positive injection (Calculation / Inference: 3V3 window, diode VF and BST bound).
`en_guard()` and schematic contracts gate the topology; five planted netlists exercise it. The two servo lanes
beside the x 28.2 bus moved outward 0.05 mm. R436 / D436 designators are hidden on F.Fab.

**D-R12-157 R12 READY threshold behind D5 (TRI-11).**
PA6 reads VLOGIC behind D5, not the pack. At the pad load,
use a corrected pack estimate `Vpack_est = VPA6 / kPA6 + ΔVfeed_pad + VF_D5_pad`, where `kPA6` is the measured
R60 / R61 ratio and `ΔVfeed_pad` includes U9 (45 mΩ maximum RON) and the battery/RAW copper to D5.
The corresponding READY threshold is **`VPA6 / kPA6 ≥ 7.4 V − ΔVfeed_pad − VF_D5_pad`**, with measured ADC/divider
error reserved on the refusing side. D5 VF **0.25–0.45 V is an Inference**, so this is a Calculation / Inference
until the pad-load drops are measured; without `ΔVfeed_pad` it is 6.95–7.15 V on VLOGIC, not a fixed 7.4 V.
For conservative refusal before calibration, use the minimum supported drop correction (0.25 V for D5 within
that inferred range), and do not treat it as a qualified READY threshold. Firmware/first-article qualification
must establish the correction and error bound; telemetry labels the corrected estimate as pack voltage and
raw PA6 as VLOGIC. All other rule 9 checks carry. The falling PA6 trip is separately amended by D-R12-164.

**Rule 4 extension (D-R12-164; PA6, VLOGIC sense; R12F9-TRI-04).** The PA6 trip threshold is
0.425 V falling / 0.455 V band top (R60 / R61 0.1 % 25 ppm/K; window 4.652–5.029 V VLOGIC at the drift
corner, Calculation / Inference). A PA6 trip that begins during a commanded fire pulse (from PE4 high
to the end of the ≤ 20 ms pulse plus its tail) is logged with its time and the PA6 reading. It is not
treated as battery loss: no battery-loss shutdown, shed or state change follows from it, and the fire
pulse is not cut short by it. A shorted match (or match and leads) still trips PA6 at the drift corner
(−0.092 / −0.136 V below the trip top; Calculation / Inference), so this rule is required. A healthy
four-stall fire is +0.059 V clear of the trip top at drift (Calculation / Inference). A PA6 trip outside
a fire pulse is battery loss as before (hold-up 0.487–1.853 ms unshed at drift, Calculation / Inference).
Firmware and bench qualification remain open.

PA6-trip hold-up nominal / initial / drift: unshed **0.501–1.811 / 0.496–1.827 / 0.487–1.853 ms**;
shed **1.500–3.386 / 1.484–3.415 / 1.457–3.464 ms** (Calculation / Inference: C84 allowance,
constant-power loads, D5 VF, 3.8 V endpoint and drift). O-R12-07's design choice is resolved; O-R12-17's
trip-bottom item closes as a calculation. Firmware implementation, READY calibration and bench work remain.

**D-R12-167 Stop rule and severity scale (owner, 2026-11-08).** Supersedes D-R11-09's / the round loop's
zero-real-error rule for R12: stop after **two consecutive full rounds with zero Critical and zero Major
real errors**. Minor real errors are fixed each round but do not reset the count. Then JLC DFM and quote;
the owner orders. Round 9 had two Majors; rounds **10 and 11** are the first possible pair.

- **Critical:** could fire the pyro wrongly or prevent a needed fire, destroy hardware in normal operation,
  or the board cannot be built.
- **Major:** a required function fails under **normal operating conditions** (nominal / datasheet-typical
  parts, within the stated temperature and supply range, no stacked worst case); or an AGENTS §2 rule
  actually violated on the board; or a single credible fault (one solder bridge) in the fire path / arming
  chain that could fire or block a fire; or the order package builds the wrong thing (part, footprint,
  rotation, net).
- **Minor:** every other real error: margins thin / negative only at stacked worst-case corners; spacing
  below our own extra rules but at or above fab / DRC limits outside the fire path (e.g. non-fire I-5 pairs);
  gate / test coverage gaps where the board itself is right; missing Inference labels; records drift.

**D-R12-168 Layout exemplars (owner, 2026-11-08).** Judge clean routing and AVA / M2 untidiness that a
specific redraw can fix against **both BPS.space AVA and LEVIA-H7**. Give both to the copper/layout lens
and any layout lane. AVA's repository reading is `m3_design/assembly_review/sources/r10_design/AVA_LAYOUT.md`;
LEVIA-H7 is the owner's routed STM32H7 flight-computer reference. These exemplars do not replace the gates.

## Round 10 fixes and chatter decision (2026-11-09; R12F10-TRI-01 … -16)

Round 10 reviewed `ae22387d…` (four Opus and five Astra lenses) and found **16 Minor real errors,
0 Critical / 0 Major** (R12F10-TRI-01 … -16): **CLEAN under D-R12-167, the first of the clean pair**.
The fixes produce **`a1e65bb4…`, 46 × 83 mm** (D-R12-170 … -173). Lane P (`b20de30`) reports two
byte-identical builds and the full gate exit 0; lane A (`3d96eb5`) reports the full gate exit 0 and
42 stage-D tests passing. At that historical checkpoint round 11 was pending; it later completed (D-R12-175).

**D-R12-170 R12 chatter bench line and pack-load sensitivity (orchestrator decision).** Keep the fire
model convention: charge the pack the converters' input power at BAT_IN (GAP-1), with D5 loss solved
from U2 input current. The headline is **5.71921 V / 1.249586 A** at four stalls. Charging the pack
`raw_point`'s full current, including U9 and copper losses and the lower converter input nodes, gives
**5.7073 V / 1.24699 A** and an EN worst-corner drift chatter limit of **6.984 ms**, below D-R25-01's
7 ms bench line (`stageD_budget.json` `fire.pack_load_sensitivity`, **Calculation / Inference**:
pack/load allocations, efficiencies, D5 VF, temperature and drift).

For R12, tighten the pass rule to **under 6.9 ms total open time in every sliding 100 ms window**,

(15.50, 61.05); R431.2 GND at (31.75, 15.70); C81.2 GND at (16.50, 22.05). Ball escapes have no second
channel; the far points have the measured geometry / K-plane / acute-angle blockers in the owner table.
BAT_SW_RETURN now has partners (13.8, 7.9) and (15.1, 10.8); row 44's slow-reference exemption remains.

The remaining single-via classes are disposed of by this rule (no existing tap is waived):

1. **A series layer change on a point-to-point signal whose path already has single solder joints** (servo, UART,
   SPI, QSPI, SD, I²C, USB, LED, SWD, IMU_INT1, BUZZ_DRV, HG_CS, CC, the CAM_5V sense chain, VBUS_SENSE, CAM_EN /
   CAM_PWR_EN, U9_FLT / BAT_FAULT_N). A second barrel leaves the series resistor, the ball and the connector contact
   single. The loss is one function, it cannot fire or arm, and bench bring-up and the pad checks show it. PC13 is
   logged only. Each pair would also need a PARALLEL_VIAS_R12 entry. R10's rule (D-R22-02) never covered this class.
2. **One of several decoupling capacitors on a rail whose IC pin keeps another** (C13–C18, C23, C29, C10, C31, C32,
   C99, C406, C407, C410, C420, C444, C460.2, C461.2), and the 3V3 dog-bones of U1's own supply balls (B2, D2, F4–F7,
   K1), which have no second channel and are in parallel inside the package.
3. **A pull-up whose line also has a driver or an internal pull** (R10, R11, R460–R464), test-point and LED returns
   (TP74, TP88, LED1, LED2, J2.1, J2.5), the crystal can (Y1.2, Y1.4), J430's shell pins (.9, .10, .13, .14),
   R105.2, C28 / C83 / R110.2, and U5.5 (CSB, joined to the tapped U5.4 inside the part).
4. **No legal spot and a benign loss; recorded here once, not as taps:**
   - C25.2 and C26.2 (HSE load): any new barrel in the crystal cell breaks the In2 neck (trials V5–V7). Open: HSE
     shifts or fails to start, and firmware rule 3 refuses at boot.
   - NRST's C24 path (25.75, 49.85), (16.85, 56.75): no spot. Open: NRST keeps its internal pull-up and loses only the
     filter.
   - C30.2 / U4.2 / U4.3 (24.90, 45.45): the only spots cross GND's own F track. C30.1 is already a recorded exception,
     and U4's GND pins 6 / 7 are tapped.
   - D121.5 (27.60, 12.05): no action. With its reference pin open the SRV05-4 still clamps to GND through its own TVS
     (Inference from the ProTek circuit).
   - C407.1 (16.75, 84.90): the second output capacitor; C406 stays.

**D-R12-177 C120 doubled transitions and S-8 inner tracks.**

C120.2's ACT_GATE jumper keeps its In2 route. Added barrels (7.2, 33.55) and (9.15, 34.85) double both
transitions. Five fill-only voids cover the inner track ends and bend. S-8 now holds every inner-layer track
of a BARREL net ≥ 1.0 mm from foreign inner copper, including fills. Lane P measured In2 track clearance
≥ 1.102 mm and via-ring clearance 1.099 mm; its missing-void seed fails S-8. No safety rule is relaxed.

**D-R12-178 R118 second Q122 gate hold.**

Add R118 10 kΩ 0402 C25744, ACT_EN to GND, at (17.17, 33.75), rotation 0, F. Pad 1 is (16.74, 33.75);
pad 2 (17.6, 33.75), with separate GND vias (17.6, 33.2) / (17.6, 34.3). The exact ACT_EN part set and
R118 value/topology are gated. R13 and R118 designators are hidden on F.Fab: 87 hidden total.
PC1 → R122 1k → R123 2k ∥ R118 10k gives 2.0625 V at 3.30 V ideal; worst drift / VOH gate 1.743 V,
0.293 V above AO3400A's 1.45 V maximum threshold. PC1 source ≤ 1.341 mA. R118 open gives 1.861 V;
R123 open 2.559 V. Sub-mA operation below the 2.5 V RDS row is Inference; qualify on the first article
(Calculation / Inference, stageD_budget.json q122_gate).

**D-R12-179 Bottom GND pour and plated-pad spokes.**

GND_pour_B minimum width is 0.25 mm, allowing its 0.3 mm thermal spokes to survive the fill.
J1.2 now has four spokes on B and four on In1. K-copper checks every plated GND pad inside a GND pour
for ≥ 2 spokes on each such layer, and J1.2 for four on each. Lane P's restored-0.5-mm seed fails.
GND return Q124 → J1.2 is 2.125 mΩ in the DC solve (Calculation on CAD copper).

**D-R12-180 R110 and VBUS_SENSE.**

R110 becomes 1.5 kΩ 1 % C25867 on its existing land. R9 1k / R110 1.5k gives 0.6 nominal ratio.
With drift and ±250 nA PC9 leakage, VBUS 4.45–5.5 V gives 2.634–3.344 V; high from 4.158 V VBUS.
VIH is 2.461 V at the 3V3 maximum; high margin 0.173 V. PC9 is FT, 4.0 V maximum even with VDD zero,
so maximum divider voltage has 0.656 V margin (Calculation / Inference). Firmware rule 6 uses this ratio
and no internal pull. A VBUS bridge bypasses the divider; its FT / loader exemption is unchanged.
FMEA H-4's 1.79 V belongs to its historical PD15 circuit, not R12, and is not edited.

**D-R12-181 J1 polarity legends and K-legends allowance.**

Top BAT+ at (2.0, 17.35) and GND at (2.0, 12.35), 1.0 mm, rotation 90°, identify J1's contacts.
K-legends permits exactly these two polarity words, once each on F within reach of J1 and nearer their own
contact than the other by 1 mm. A swapped pair fails. Existing bottom contact legends remain.

**D-R12-182 I-5 minimum ratings, victims and one-way path rule.**

I5_RAIL_MIN assigns switch nodes −2 V and other rails 0 V; rating guards compare pin minima and maxima.
CAM_SW therefore loses CAM_EN's rating exemption. Two pairs at 0.524 / 0.525 mm are I5_U403_EN_SW
(owner row 53): camera disturbance/damage only, PC12 protected by R436 / D436 (Calculation / Inference).
CAM_BST and RAW_PROTECTED keep their supported rating exemptions.

D95.2 and D436.2 unconnected diode anodes are derived I5_NC_PASS victims. D120.2 remains outside: its
3V3 path includes R136. I5_FB_MID derives PORT_FB_TOP and CAM_FB_TOP and holds them from pack rails.
Eleven PORT_FB_TOP pairs at 0.537–1.011 mm are I5_U400_FB_TOP (row 54); no CAM_FB_TOP exception is needed.
A bridge can over-drive U400 VFB and lose the ports; a divider-leg bridge moves the output (Inference on damage).

NOT_A_PATH is one-way: RAW_ACT's pack rule may exempt PYRO1_IGN downstream of R134, but PYRO1_IGN's own
rule holds RAW_ACT ≥ 0.25 mm copper / 0.5 mm fills. Reverse exemption removed. Total S-10 exceptions 203:
51 fire-net, 105 BAT_IN, eight VLOGIC_SENSE, 39 I-5 IC-pin. DRU rules 223. Limits and fault reasons remain
in R12_VALIDATION; seeded negatives cover each extension.

**D-R12-183 BAT_IN network, R80 EN basis and 6.7 ms bench rule.**

**EN timing and chatter (D-R12-183; Calculation / Inference).** BAT_IN is a network with J1.1,
U9.5, Q120.3 and R80.1 terminals. EN is evaluated at R80.1, not J1. At the four-stall GAP-1 point,
J1 is 5.71941 V and R80.1 is 5.70082 V (18.6 mV lower). Single drop-out immunity is
7.967 / 7.446 ms initial / drift; periodic limits are 7.410 / 6.935 ms.
Whole-current loading gives J1 5.70765 V, R80.1 5.68849 V, fire current 1.24573 A and the worst
periodic limit **6.843 ms**. The 7 ms line fails both conventions. The bench pass rule is **under 6.7 ms
total open time in every sliding 100 ms window**, recorded at **≥ 10 kS/s under flight-level vibration**,
with **U9 on throughout at 5.72 V**. This supersedes D-R12-170's historical 6.9 ms rule; the margin to
the worst model limit is 0.143 ms. `en.chatter_rule.bench_rule_ms` stores 6.7 and the gate checks both
load conventions. Turn-on is 30.3–131.5 ms initial / 29.9–135.9 ms drift over C87 0.72–1.265 × 220 nF.
C87's X7R allowance, leakage corners, pack/load and drift are Inference.

The DC network has J1.1 as reference and U9.5, Q120.3, R80.1 terminals. At 20 °C its self-resistances are
7.837 / 7.385 / 13.239 mΩ; U9–R80 mutual 6.013 mΩ, Q120–U9 and Q120–R80 1.483 mΩ. Four-stall copper
uses ×1.1965. Fire loop 4.72843 Ω, fire 1.24833 A, converter 2.27559 A in GAP-1; U9.5 5.69586 V,
Q120.3 5.70434 V. The full-current sensitivity fire is 1.24573 A. Pack/load and temperature are Inference.

Port and U6 drift minima now use 70 °C copper; PA6 upper endpoints solve at D5 VF 0.25 V with their own
currents. Current values are stageD_budget.json and M3_R12 Power / CH1 pyro. Dummy-fire bound 0.27318 V
at 1.52468 A, including converter coupling, remains below the unchanged 0.4 V rejection line.
The C84 graph now joins tracks crossing pads; every unjoined rail fails record validation.

**D-R12-184 3V3 current budget and 200 MHz limit.**

**U2 / 3V3 load budget (D-R12-184; Calculation / Inference).** With L1 4.7 µH at −20 %, VIN 8.15 V,
VOUT 3.35 V and ISC minimum 1.25 A, the output bound is 0.875 A at 700 kHz and 0.813 A at the 600 kHz
minimum (TI SLUSDW3A §7.5; applying the open-loop limit to closed-loop operation is an Inference).
DS12110 Rev 8 Tables 30 / 31 give 200 MHz VOS3 MCU maxima of 0.220 A at Tj 85 °C and 0.300 A at 105 °C
with all peripherals enabled. Add microSD 0.200 A (F5), U3 0.025 A, U4/U5/U6 0.008 A, LEDs 0.012 A,
pull-ups 0.004 A (allocations, Inference), and buzzer 0.320 A on-phase / 0.160 A average (3.5156 V / 11 Ω,
QMB-09B-03 16 ± 5 Ω, ≤ 50 % duty; Calculation / Inference). At 200 MHz / 85 °C this is 0.629 A average /
0.789 A peak; at 105 °C, 0.709 / 0.869 A. The hot stacked peak exceeds the 600 kHz bound by 56 mA.
Firmware must not start a microSD write burst while the buzzer sounds; schedule writes in beep gaps.
At 480 MHz / 85 °C the peak is 1.008 A, so **200 MHz is a hardware clock limit**. Tj 85 °C as the budget
point and I/O switching current are Inference; qualify load steps and temperatures on the first article.

**D-R12-185 Hot-plug validation and gate board binding.**

Lane A re-extracted inputs on c9b9f0fa and re-ran 2304 required cases: all pass, minimum U9 IN −14.077 V
(Simulation / Inference). The electrical signature is unchanged from lane P's run; the model binding changed.
The gate calls the installed validator, recomputes metrics with 1e-12 relative float tolerance, compares
identities exactly, and checks stage-D board/source hashes and hot-plug input hash against the gated board.
Seeds cover failed metrics, repeated corners, stale inputs/model and stale board hashes. Lane A reports
60 stage-D tests passing. The final full gate remains the orchestrator's commit condition.

D8.2 is ideal ground in the model. Its two 1.45 mm / 0.3 mm F runs and barrels are estimated as 1.29 mΩ
at 20 °C / 1.54 mΩ at 70 °C and 0.27 nH in parallel (Calculation / Inference; plane spreading omitted),
small against the clamp's 0.26 Ω slope and ≥ 0.1 µH harness. Owner row 52 records the omission.

**D-R12-186 Firmware additions (a)–(g).**

**Additional R12 firmware rules (D-R12-186; R12F12-TRI-13).**

(a) Drive BUZZ_DRV (PE8) only as a software-toggled approximately 2.7 kHz square wave at ≤ 50 % duty.
Its timer function TIM1_CH1N is SERVO3's complement and must not drive the buzzer. Force PE8 low on every
exit and keep IWDG running while it sounds. A steady high draws about 0.32 A in a ≤ 50 mA coil
(Calculation / Inference: coil resistance and 3V3 window).

(b) Run SPI1 in mode 0 for U4 and U6. Write U4 IF_CFG.I2C_I3C_disable as the first access, before any U6 traffic.

(c) Keep USB VBUS sensing disabled: PA9 is SERVO4 on R12.

(d) Set PUPDR = 00 on PC9, PC10, PC11 and PD10. This overrides the carried R11 RX pull-up rule.

(e) Keep the system clock ≤ 200 MHz (U2 / L1 hardware budget, D-R12-184).

(f) The flight estimator and fire rule must work with U6 absent. The LIFTOFF AND barometric-gain rule stays.

(g) Do not start a microSD write burst while the buzzer is sounding. Schedule writes in beep gaps.
This is the hot stacked-corner margin rule in D-R12-184.

**D-R12-187 J1, plug identity and coating procedures.**

The R12 handoff requires a permanent J1 pigtail to a keyed pack connector. Touch J1 screws only with
the pack unplugged there, J400 open, no e-match in J121 and an insulated driver (step 1b). Use 20 AWG
(22 acceptable), insulation OD ≤ 1.8 mm; 18 AWG only at ≤ 2.2 mm OD with its bend checked. Bare strands
or uninsulated ferrules protruding ≤ 0.5 mm; never tinned. Recovery step 7 unplugs the pack at its connector.
Step 0b identifies every plug against the legend, J400 takes only the switch lead, switch and camera leads
have distinct tags, and step 2 repeats after any plug moves. Retention remains step 0c.

The handoff gives current pad envelopes for pyro / enable coating, includes R118, and names the keep-off
items. Check NRST-to-GND leakage ≥ 250 kΩ after coating, at installation and before flight; powered
NRST at C24.1 ≥ 0.90 × 3V3 at LS120 pin 1 (3.8, 70.25; J2 is covered after the final SWD session) with a 10 MΩ DMM. Powered check is step 3 with dummy load and no match.
No existing cold-screen, switch-off or fire rule is relaxed.

**D-R12-188 U6 dual-channel read and open supply-monitor item.**

**U6 supply monitor (D-R12-188, O-R12-22 reopened).** NXP's dual-channel FXLS9XXXX Rev 7.0,
3 March 2025, was read for round 12. Table 85 gives FXLS90xxx unprogrammed COMMTYPE = 000. Table 86 row 000
lists DSI3 + SPI, “Enabled based on ARM_CFG[2:0]” in the arming column and “Disabled” under BUS_I undervoltage
detection. Table 14 gives the SPI minimum 3.135 V, production tested; the feature list says “3.3 V or 5 V
single supply operation”. These favour operation as shipped at 3.3 V (**Inference**).

Against that reading, Table 15 says “BUS_I Falling, COMMTYPE = 0, 1” with 3.31 / 3.50 / 3.67 V
(characterisation only, note 6), and the note after Table 87 says “In SPI only mode and in I C only mode,
the BUS_I undervoltage detection is disabled to allow for 3.3 V system operation.” Section 11.4.2 says
“If any supply falls below the specified threshold, SPI responses are terminated.” Pins 1, 13, 15 and 16
are VCC in SPI wiring. The sheet does not resolve whether an unprogrammed FXLS90322 answers SPI at 3.3 V.
This is an open owner item, not a demonstrated error. Ask NXP, or accept that the first two boards are the test.
Read DEVSTAT / DEVSTAT1, including BUSINUV_ERR and SUPPLY_ERR, with 3V3 at **3.22 V and 3.52 V** on the first
article. The estimator and fire rule must run without U6. No 3V3 setpoint clears the 3.67 V maximum threshold
while staying within the MCU's 3.6 V limit. No hardware hedge was adopted.

Figure 9 / Table 9 name pin 2 **PCM1/ARM1**, an output left unconnected as NXP recommends when unused
(Datasheet, not Inference). The axis-frame and range-code checks remain O-R12-21; the pin-name read is closed.

**D-R12-189 Options considered and not adopted.**

- U6 on a higher rail: PORT_4V8 is remote and off on USB, its MISO swing would over-drive the shared U4 line;
  CAM_5V exceeds U6's 5.25 V maximum. No 3V3 setpoint clears the 3.67 V threshold within the MCU's 3.6 V limit.
- C120 fully on F: no legal site for the present part joins both gates without a new gate–drain / pack pair.
  An 0402 1 µF / 25 V option changes effective capacitance and turn-off timing; respin only.
- VCAP1–VCAP2 link: not adopted; read AN4938 §2.2 before merging nets and their guards. DS12110's nominal
  two 2.2 µF requirement is met; operating capacitance / ESR remains open.
- J430 bypass: keep A-14's measured 6.38 / 7.27 mm; the card path, socket courtyard and RAW trunk block closer sites.
- U2 10 µH with Isat ≥ 1.9 A, or port/camera inductors with Isat ≥ 5.1 A: no verified same-land JLC part;
  retain hardware, budget rules and overload bench checks.
- Keep J400 contacts 1–2; contacts 3–4 would still admit conduction through a mis-plugged module's RX/TX network.
- Do not swap R155 / R157: an untested four-net reroute to save vias. Eight routing leftovers are recorded.
- LS120's 9.3 mm square 3D envelope and O-R12-28's 86.85 mm vehicle fit remain owner items.

None of these options is owner acceptance of the current deviations.

**D-R12-190 Inductor overload, D9, J1 holes and D8 return owner items.**

Amend D-R12-07 / -08 and A-5: a 2–3.3 A overload is not limited by either TPS563208; L400 / L401 are
2 A rated, listed Isat 3.2 A, versus valley limit 3.3–5.1 A. U9's 3.92–4.89 A is the bound. Row 15 requires
2.5 A for 60 s on port and camera loads, recording inductor temperature. No part change.
D9.1 is 9.73 mm from U9.6 through two vias and the In2 island, departing from SLVSFC9C §8.4.1 advice
(R10 R3-PWR-06); its courtyard is 0.01 mm from C82, so no move (row 50).
J1's 1.30 mm hole is Kefa's 1.20 +0.10 upper limit. The 2.6 mm land retains ≥ 0.58 mm annular ring
with JLC's tolerance (Calculation / Inference); inspect seating and fillet (row 51).
D8's return remains as built; widening introduces more BAT_IN–GND pairs. Model omission and bench check
are row 52 / D-R12-185. These are recorded deviations, not measured electrical qualification.

**D-R12-191 Current records, courtyard convention and source reads.**

Current electrical figures come from lane A's board-bound JSON; coordinates from placement / board data;
M2 inventory from lane P's route report, with all eight reasons copied from route_check. Historical round-10
blocks and hash table are marked historical; the current snapshot includes the live 2 / 5 board cost and stock.

Forward-cell courtyard gaps include the 0.05 mm stroke, edge to edge: J410–L400 0.425, L400–C410 0.080,
L400–C407 0.150, C406–R450 1.305, C406–L400 1.25, C406–TP88 1.20, J420 / J410–C407 0.40 mm;
TP88–LS120 1.51 mm (Measured). The outline is now bound by J1.

The round-12 records lens read DS12110 Rev 11 Table 10: all used AF numbers verified. It read AN2606 Rev 71
Table 145: loader outputs include PA9 → SERVO4, PB10 → QSPI_NCS, PB6/PB9 → barometer SCL/IMU_CS,
PA8/PC9 → SERVO3/VBUS_SENSE, PA6 → VLOGIC_SENSE, PC11 → camera UART and PE13 → SD MISO; USB PA11/PA12.
O-R12-23 retains physical behaviour only. Owner row 39 (PACK_U9) now cites SLVSFC9C §6.1 (the `WHY['PACK_U9']` string in `m3_design/r12_fire.py` keeps its older
"not re-read" wording: the file is a fab-manifest input and the string reaches no output, so it was left): OVLO at 8.4 V exceeds its 6.5 V
maximum; AUXOFF also 6.5 V, dVdt / ILIM / ITIMER internally limited. Damage is Inference.

Rise 46.1 °C at design 3.348 A, 34.6 °C at U9 2.9 A, 4.69 °C at budget 1.06755 A; 0.87 mm would hold 30 °C at 3.35 A (IPC-2152 upper bound, Calculation / Inference). U400.5 EN stub carries only µA.
